<!-- ==================================================================
     LALAGUNA - THE CITY (walkable Bogota, WebGL)

     Paste this ENTIRE file into the Code Block on the CITY page.

     What this is: the ground half of the Bogota flight. The film ends 406 m
     above the city looking south-west; this picks the viewer up at that exact
     spot and lets them walk. Same geodata, same grid, same metres - the
     buildings are the UAECD cadastre extruded by floor count and the streets
     are the OSM ways, both exported by the same Python that fed Unreal.

     Nothing is rebuilt for the web by hand. The pipeline already wrote glTF;
     these are those files, Draco-compressed about 14x. The whole 6 x 6 km
     core - 118,000 buildings and every street in it - is under 5 MB.

     The root keeps id="lala-page" so the sitewide footer still adds the
     wordmark, jaguar menu and SoundCloud bar on top. data-page="city3d"
     keeps the footer's own page builders out of the way.

     Colour: ink #08090C, pearl #DED6D8, champagne #E8D3A6, chambray #9EB4D3,
     mist #EDE6E8 - the same set as the rest of the site.
     ================================================================== -->

<script>
/* ============================================================
   LALAGUNA - THE CITY
   Only edit CITY_CONFIG.
   ============================================================ */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  var CITY_CONFIG = {

    /* Where the tiles live. Upload the whole city/ folder - manifest and
       tiles/ - to the lalaguna-assets repo and jsDelivr serves it. */
    BASE: 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/comcelion123/lalaguna-assets/main/city/',

    /* The tile index, carried inside this file on purpose.

       It used to be fetched from the CDN beside the tiles, and that is what
       made the last two rounds look like nothing had changed: jsDelivr serves
       a NEW filename immediately but holds an UPDATED one on @main for about
       twelve hours - and the manifest is the one file that changes every time.
       So the page kept loading new code, new tiles and yesterday's index, which
       told it none of the new data existed.

       Here it ships with the code that understands it, so the two can never
       disagree. Only the binaries live on the CDN, and those get new names
       whenever their contents change. */
    MANIFEST: {
      "tileSize": 2000,
      "grid": 1024,
      "note": "world units are metres; x = east, z = south, y = up. ground-atlas-v2.png is street level for the whole core, 16-bit as R*256+G mapped onto [lo,hi]. The ground mesh displaces itself from it in the vertex shader, so the plate and the roads cannot disagree. groundY is the lowest building vertex and sits below the street by design - never walk on it.",
      "groundY": 2544.55,
      "spawn": {
        "x": -10963.9,
        "z": 215.8,
        "yawDeg": -168.143,
        "note": "a street with room to walk, in a built-up block near where the flight ends"
      },

          "x": -9000,
          "z": 3000,
          "groundY": 2579.64,
          "topY": 3169.68,
          "buildings": 6915,
          "green": "tiles/green_07_13.glb"
        }
      ],
      "filmEnd": {
        "x": -10871,
        "z": 14.9
      },
      "roads": "ground-roads.webp",
      "atlas": {
        "file": "ground-atlas-v2.png",
        "size": 1024,
        "x0": -14000,
        "z0": -2000,
        "w": 6000,
        "h": 6000,
        "lo": 2547.86,
        "hi": 3150.48
      }
    },

    /* The three ways out of the street. Each is a lit doorway standing on the
       pavement; walk up to one and it offers to open.
         bearing  degrees clockwise from east, in the ground plane
         dist     metres from where the viewer lands */
    SHOPS: [
      { key: 'cedain', label: 'CEDAIN',   href: '/cedain' },
      { key: 'studio', label: 'STUDIO',   href: '/studio' },
      { key: 'fyma',   label: 'F.Y.M.A.', href: 'https://fyma.store' }
    ],

    /* The wordmark hanging over the city. The flight ends looking at it, so it
       sits on that same bearing - walk out of the gate and it is where the film
       left it. */
    LOGO: { text: 'LALAGUNA', dist: 2500, height: 900, width: 1500 },

    /* ---- THE SKY: CANAL VALLEY ---------------------------------------
       The sky is the Canal Valley hydra sketch - the one the intro and the
       About page run - running live and draped over the dome. Same chain,
       same scan, same numbers as the footer's buildHydraBg, so it is the
       same picture. hydra-synth comes from URL; the scan from SCAN (CORS is
       on at that host - it is the footer's own). If either fails the sky is
       plain ink with stars, and the console says why.
         GAIN   how far the valley sits below the city lights (the intro runs
                it at 0.9 over ink; a lit street wants less)
         TILE   how many times the frame wraps the sky - a whole number */
    CANAL: {
      URL:    'https://unpkg.com/hydra-synth@1.4.0/dist/hydra-synth.js',
      SCAN:   'https://d2w9rnfcy7mm78.cloudfront.net/30743927/original_2cbfe4c1957d7cf5745a42cd5467fb21.jpg?1726541805',
      W: 1024, H: 512,
      FPS:    30,
      ZOOM:   0.42,
      TERRAC: 5,
      BANDS:  9,
      FLOW:   0.10,
      SMEAR:  0.022,
      CRAWL:  0.05,
      GAIN:   0.5,
      TILE:   1,
    },

    KERB:       0.14,     /* how far the pavement stands above the asphalt */
    EYE:        1.7,      /* eye height, metres */
    /* Minecraft's feel: 4.3 m/s on foot, a run a touch over its sprint so
       a 6 km city stays walkable, a jump that tops out at 1.25 m and lands
       0.63 s later, full control on the ground and a little in the air */
    WALK:       4.3,      /* metres per second */
    RUN:        7.0,
    JUMP:       8.1,      /* take-off, metres per second (1.25 m at the top once the frame steps are counted) */
    GRAVITY:    25,       /* metres per second squared */
    REACH:      10,       /* how close before a doorway speaks up */
    FAR:        7000,
    FOG:        0.00052,  /* exp2 density - lower sees further */
    SKY_SPIN:   true,

    /* ---- THE LOOK ----------------------------------------------------
       Everything above describes the city; this describes how it is drawn.
       At POSTER 0 / LINE 0 / DOTS 0 it is the photographic night render the
       film ends on. Turning them up walks it toward flat graphic print:
       shading collapses to a few steps, silhouettes take an ink edge, and a
       screen-print dot comes up in the mid-tones. ACCENT is the one hot
       colour the whole frame is allowed, PAPER the one light.

         POSTER  0 = continuous, otherwise the number of flat steps
         LINE    0 = no outline, up to about 0.6 for a heavy one
         DOTS    0 = none, 1 = full screen-print
         DOTPX   the dot pitch in screen pixels
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    LOOK: {
      POSTER: 0,
      LINE:   0,
      DOTS:   0,
      DOTPX:  5.0,
      ACCENT: '#E8D3A6',
      PAPER:  '#EDE6E8',
    },

    /* ---- THE FACADES (v2) ----------------------------------------------
       The buildings as built things, in the white of the studio's models:
       real relief around the walker (one of five looks per building),
       drawn relief and living windows further out, the doors as glass bays
       in crystal frames, a moon with shadows. See THE FACADES below.
         ON            false puts the night boxes back as they were
         FILE          the facades file, in the city/ folder on the CDN
         RADIUS        metres of real relief around the walker
         REBUILD       metres walked before the relief is laid again
         MAX_INSTANCES the budget of pieces; the radius shrinks to fit
         WALL          the white of the walls
         MOON / MOON_INTENSITY / MOON_FROM   the key light and where it stands
         SKY / SKY_INTENSITY                 the fill from above
         SHADOWS       false saves the GPU on a small machine
         EXPOSURE      the tone of the whole picture
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    FACADES: {
      ON:             true,
      FILE:           'facades-v2.bin',
      RADIUS:         170,
      REBUILD:        18,
      MAX_INSTANCES:  90000,
      WALL:           '#E3DDE0',
      MOON:           '#C9D6EC',
      MOON_INTENSITY: 1.5,
      MOON_FROM:      [-230, 400, 230],
      SKY:            '#6B7A96',
      SKY_INTENSITY:  1.6,
      SHADOWS:        true,
      EXPOSURE:       1.0,
      DOOR_WALL:      7.0,    /* the shortest wall a door's crystal frame may stand on, metres */
    },

    /* ---- THE PLATFORM (v3) -----------------------------------------------
       The city ends where the city ends: a platform floating in the marble,
       a pale edge dropping away under it, an invisible wall at the edge.
         ON      false: the old open ground to the horizon
         CELL    metres per cell of the outline
         REACH   how far past the last building the platform runs, metres
         DEPTH   how far the edge drops under the platform, metres
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    PLATFORM: {
      ON:     true,
      CELL:   20,
      REACH:  60,
      DEPTH:  60,
    },

    /* ---- THE STREETS (v3) -------------------------------------------------
       Every street as vectors: pavement and carriageway as straight ribbons,
       the paint as real strips - white edge lines, the double yellow, dashed
       lanes - stopping at every junction.
         ON       false: the streets as the ground texture, as before
         FILE     the streets file, in the city/ folder on the CDN
         PAINT_R  metres of paint laid around the walker
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    STREETS: {
      ON:      true,
      FILE:    'roads-v1.bin',
      PAINT_R: 450,
    },

    /* ---- THE STREET LIFE (v4) ---------------------------------------------
       Bogota's materials and Midnight Club's night: brick, stucco, concrete
       and glass instead of the white model, neon on the shopfronts, wet
       streets, lampposts, SITP stops, parked cars and taxis, traffic, people
       (some dancing to whatever is playing), a car you can take, and finds.
         ON           false: the city exactly as v3 left it
         QUALITY      'auto' | 'low' | 'mid' | 'high' - auto reads the machine
                      and steps itself down if the first seconds run slow
         SIGN_CHANCE  how many shopfronts on a street get a neon sign (0..1)
         PARKED       how full the kerbs are (0..1)
         TOP_SPEED    the car's top speed, metres per second (46 = 165 km/h)
         REWARDS      the finds, hidden on pavements around the city. Each:
                        id     a name that never changes (remembers it was found)
                        kind   'code' or 'print'
                        title, text   what the card says
                        code   the discount code (empty: "coming soon")
                        link / linkLabel   a button, e.g. the shop
                        file   a print to download (a URL); preview   an image to show
                        at     optional { x, z } to put it somewhere exact
       ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
    LIFE: {
      ON:          true,
      QUALITY:     'auto',
      SIGN_CHANCE: 0.45,
      PARKED:      0.52,
      TOP_SPEED:   46,
      REWARDS: [
        { id: 'fyma-code-1',  kind: 'code',  title: 'F.Y.M.A. · a code',   code: '', text: 'A discount on fyma.store. This one is still being cut. Come back soon.', link: 'https://fyma.store', linkLabel: 'fyma.store' },
        { id: 'print-1',      kind: 'print', title: 'A free print',        file: '', preview: '', text: 'An original digital print by Cesar Tinjaca. Still in the darkroom. Come back soon.' },
        { id: 'fyma-code-2',  kind: 'code',  title: 'F.Y.M.A. · a code',   code: '', text: 'A discount on fyma.store. This one is still being cut. Come back soon.', link: 'https://fyma.store', linkLabel: 'fyma.store' },
        { id: 'print-2',      kind: 'print', title: 'A free print',        file: '', preview: '', text: 'An original digital print by Cesar Tinjaca. Still in the darkroom. Come back soon.' },
        { id: 'fyma-code-3',  kind: 'code',  title: 'F.Y.M.A. · a code',   code: '', text: 'A discount on fyma.store. This one is still being cut. Come back soon.', link: 'https://fyma.store', linkLabel: 'fyma.store' },
        { id: 'print-3',      kind: 'print', title: 'A free print',        file: '', preview: '', text: 'An original digital print by Cesar Tinjaca. Still in the darkroom. Come back soon.' },
        { id: 'fyma-code-4',  kind: 'code',  title: 'F.Y.M.A. · a code',   code: '', text: 'A discount on fyma.store. This one is still being cut. Come back soon.', link: 'https://fyma.store', linkLabel: 'fyma.store' },
        { id: 'print-4',      kind: 'print', title: 'A free print',        file: '', preview: '', text: 'An original digital print by Cesar Tinjaca. Still in the darkroom. Come back soon.' },
      ],
    },
  };

  var root = document.getElementById('lala-page');
  if (!root || root.getAttribute('data-page') !== 'city3d') return;

  /* Phones do not get the city. The walk is too heavy for them, so on a
     phone the homepage is the film and then the valley with the three doors
     - CEDAIN, STUDIO, FYMA - which is the footer's intro page. The page is
     handed to it here by changing the page attribute before the footer runs
     (it runs after this block, and reads the attribute then); the city's
     own markup goes, and none of the 3D libraries are fetched. A tablet
     with room to walk keeps the city. */
  var PHONE = !!(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) &&
              Math.min(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight) < 760;
  if (PHONE) {
    root.setAttribute('data-page', 'intro');
    root.innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  /* The 3D libraries, fetched here rather than by script tags above, so a
     phone never pays for them. In order: three, then its two loaders. */
  function loadScripts(urls) {
    return urls.reduce(function (p, u) {
      return p.then(function () {
        return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
          var sc = document.createElement('script');
          sc.src = u;
          sc.onload = res;
          sc.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('could not load ' + u)); };
          document.head.appendChild(sc);
        });
      });
    }, Promise.resolve());
  }
  loadScripts([
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js',
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/loaders/GLTFLoader.js',
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/loaders/DRACOLoader.js'
  ]).then(main, function (e) {
    var t = document.getElementById('ct-load-txt');
    if (t) t.textContent = 'the 3D libraries did not load - reload, or check the network';
    if (window.console) console.error('[CITY]', e);
  });

  function main() {

  var INK = 0x08090C, PEARL = 0xDED6D8, CHAMPAGNE = 0xE8D3A6, CHAMBRAY = 0x9EB4D3, MIST = 0xEDE6E8;

  var elLoad  = document.getElementById('ct-load');
  var elFill  = document.getElementById('ct-load-fill');
  var elTxt   = document.getElementById('ct-load-txt');
  var elStart = document.getElementById('ct-start');
  var elHud   = document.getElementById('ct-hud');
  var elWhere = document.getElementById('ct-where');
  var elPrompt= document.getElementById('ct-prompt');
  var elPmtNm = document.getElementById('ct-prompt-nm');
  var elStick = document.getElementById('ct-stick');
  var elNub   = document.getElementById('ct-stick-nub');

  function say(t) { if (elTxt) elTxt.textContent = t; }
  function pct(p) { if (elFill) elFill.style.width = Math.round(p * 100) + '%'; }

  if (!window.THREE || !THREE.GLTFLoader || !THREE.DRACOLoader) {
    say('the 3D libraries did not load - reload, or check the network');
    return;
  }

  var TOUCH = ('ontouchstart' in window) || navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0;
  var SMALL = window.innerWidth < 760;
  var reduceMotion = window.matchMedia &&
    window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  /* ================= load ================= */
  var MANI = null, TILES = [], OCC = {}, ATLAS = null, DATA_WARN = null;

  Promise.resolve(CITY_CONFIG.MANIFEST)
    .then(function (m) {
      if (!m || !m.tiles) throw new Error('no tile index in this file');
      MANI = m; return loadAll(m);
    })
    .then(function () { return fontsReady(); })
    .then(function () { start(); })
    .catch(function (e) {
      say('could not load the city: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e));
      if (window.console) console.error('[CITY]', e);
    });

  /* The signs, the door labels and the LALAGUNA over the city are drawn onto
     canvases in LalaTitle (Neoda v3, served by the footer). A canvas draws
     with whatever is loaded at that moment, and the face usually was not yet,
     so they came out in the system sans. Wait for it - briefly - first. */
  function fontsReady() {
    if (!document.fonts || !document.fonts.load) return Promise.resolve();
    var want = document.fonts.load("300 48px 'LalaTitle'").catch(function () {});
    var patience = new Promise(function (res) { setTimeout(res, 2500); });
    return Promise.race([want, patience]);
  }

  var draco = new THREE.DRACOLoader();
  draco.setDecoderPath('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/libs/draco/gltf/');
  draco.setDecoderConfig({ type: 'js' });
  var gltf = new THREE.GLTFLoader();
  gltf.setDRACOLoader(draco);

  function loadGLB(url) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      gltf.load(url, function (g) { res(g); }, null, rej);
    });
  }

  /* The occupancy grid is a 1-bit PNG per tile: one pixel per ~2 m of ground,
     set where a building stands. Decoding it into a bitfield once means the
     walk test is an array lookup instead of a raycast against half a million
     triangles. */
  /* Every image loader resolves to a value or REJECTS WITH A REASON. The old
     ones resolved null on any failure, which is how a ground file that was
     never uploaded turned into "the city feels broken" with nothing on screen
     to say so. A rejection here ends up named in the loading panel. */
  function loadOcc(url, grid) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var img = new Image();
      img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      img.onload = function () {
        var c = document.createElement('canvas');
        c.width = grid; c.height = grid;
        var x = c.getContext('2d');
        x.drawImage(img, 0, 0, grid, grid);
        var d;
        try { d = x.getImageData(0, 0, grid, grid).data; }
        catch (err) { rej(new Error('cors')); return; }
        var bits = new Uint8Array((grid * grid) >> 3);
        for (var i = 0, n = grid * grid; i < n; i++) {
          if (d[i << 2] > 127) bits[i >> 3] |= 0x80 >> (i & 7);
        }
        res(bits);
      };
      img.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('missing')); };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }

  /* Street level, as a picture. One byte per 7.8 m of ground, mapped onto
     that tile's own [hLo,hHi]. */
  /* The ground, as one 16-bit field across the whole core (R*256+G). Decoded
     once into a Float32Array of metres, which both the walk test and the
     ground mesh then read - there is only one description of the terrain. */
  function loadAtlas(url, size) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var img = new Image();
      img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      img.onload = function () {
        var c = document.createElement('canvas');
        c.width = size; c.height = size;
        var x = c.getContext('2d');
        x.drawImage(img, 0, 0, size, size);
        var d;
        try { d = x.getImageData(0, 0, size, size).data; }
        catch (err) { rej(new Error('cors')); return; }
        var a = MANI.atlas, span = a.hi - a.lo;
        var out = new Float32Array(size * size);
        for (var i = 0, n = size * size; i < n; i++) {
          out[i] = a.lo + ((d[i << 2] * 256 + d[(i << 2) + 1]) / 65535) * span;
        }
        res({ data: out, tex: new THREE.CanvasTexture(c) });
      };
      img.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('missing')); };
      img.src = url;
    });
  }

  /* what did not arrive, by file, with why */
  var MISSING = [];
  function miss(file, err, what) {
    var st = err && err.target && err.target.status;      /* the loader's XHR, on an HTTP failure */
    var why = (err && err.message === 'cors') ? 'served without CORS headers'
            : (st === 404 || (err && err.message === 'missing')) ? 'not on the CDN'
            : st ? ('HTTP ' + st)
            : 'did not load';
    MISSING.push({ file: file, why: why, what: what });
    if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] ' + what + ' missing: ' + file + ' (' + why + ')');
  }

  /* The streets as a texture on the ground, not as geometry over it. The
     ribbons were a second surface: at every junction two of them crossed
     at the same height and fought, a park polygon cut across a street, one
     road's kerb ran over the next one's asphalt. A texture cannot clash with
     itself. ground-roads.webp is the ribbons rasterised once, off-line, with
     their own across/along/width coordinates - asphalt, pavement, painted
     lines, parks - and the plate draws them. */
  var ROADS = null;
  function loadRoadsTex(url) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      var tl = new THREE.TextureLoader();
      tl.setCrossOrigin('anonymous');
      tl.load(url, function (t) {
        t.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping; t.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        t.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; t.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
        t.generateMipmaps = true; t.anisotropy = 8;
        res(t);
      }, undefined, function (e) { rej(e || new Error('missing')); });
    });
  }

  function loadAll(m) {
    var jobs = [], done = 0;
    var total = m.tiles.length * 2 + 2 + (FACADES_ON ? 1 : 0);
    function tick() { done++; pct(done / total); }
    if (STX.ON !== false) {
      total++;
      jobs.push(loadStreets(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + (STX.FILE || 'roads-v1.bin')).then(function (w) {
        STREETS = w; tick();
        if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] streets: ' + w.length + ' ways');
      }, function (e) { STREETS = null; miss(STX.FILE || 'roads-v1.bin', e, 'streets'); tick(); }));
    }
    if (FACADES_ON) {
      jobs.push(loadFacades(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + (FACX.FILE || 'facades-v2.bin')).then(function (f) {
        if (BLOCKSX.ON === false) return f;
        /* which walls face the street, measured from the footprints themselves (the file's own flags miss one wall in ten) */
        var openJob = fetch(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + (BLOCKSX.NBR || 'facades-nbr-v2.bin')).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); return r.arrayBuffer(); })
          .then(function (buf) {
            /* per wall: the roof of whatever stands in front of it, in dm over 2500 m - 0 where it faces the street */
            /* two per wall: the roof in front of it, and the roof it stands on (where it rises from a lower building) - dm over 2500 m, 0 = none */
            var o = new Uint16Array(buf), ne = f.flags.length; if (o.length !== ne * 2) throw new Error('length');
            f.ntop = new Float32Array(ne); f.wfoot = new Float32Array(ne);
            for (var q = 0; q < ne; q++) {
              var fr = o[q * 2], ft = o[q * 2 + 1];
              f.ntop[q] = fr ? 2500 + fr / 10 : 0; f.wfoot[q] = ft ? 2500 + ft / 10 : 0;
              f.flags[q] = (f.flags[q] & 254) | (fr ? 0 : 1);
            }
          })
          .catch(function (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] open walls: file flags kept (' + e.message + ')'); });
        var extraJob = loadExtraBuildings(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + (BLOCKSX.EXTRA || 'facades-extra-v3.json')).catch(function () { return null; });
        return Promise.all([openJob, extraJob]).then(function (r) {
          if (r[1]) { try { mergeExtras(f, r[1]); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] extra buildings skipped: ' + e.message); } }
          return f;
        });
      }).then(function (f) {
        FAC = f; tick();
        if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] facades: ' + f.n + ' buildings');
      }, function (e) { FAC = null; miss(FACX.FILE || 'facades-v2.bin', e, 'facades'); tick(); }));
    }

    m.tiles.forEach(function (t) {
      jobs.push(loadGLB(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + t.city).then(function (g) {
        t._city = g.scene; tick(); return g;
      }, function (e) { t._city = null; miss(t.city, e, 'buildings'); tick(); }));
      jobs.push(loadOcc(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + t.occ, m.grid).then(function (b) {
        OCC[t.ix + '_' + t.iy] = b; tick();
      }, function (e) { OCC[t.ix + '_' + t.iy] = null; miss(t.occ, e, 'collision'); tick(); }));
    });
    jobs.push(loadAtlas(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + m.atlas.file, m.atlas.size).then(function (a) {
      ATLAS = a; tick();
    }, function (e) { ATLAS = null; miss(m.atlas.file, e, 'ground'); tick(); }));
    jobs.push(loadRoadsTex(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + (m.roads || 'ground-roads.webp')).then(function (t) {
      ROADS = t; tick();
    }, function (e) { ROADS = null; miss(m.roads || 'ground-roads.webp', e, 'streets'); tick(); }));
    say('118,000 buildings, every street between them');
    TILES = m.tiles;
    return Promise.all(jobs).then(function () {
      /* the ribbon geometry is only fetched when something needs it: the
         ground has to be rebuilt from it, or the street texture is missing */
      if (ATLAS && ROADS) return afterLoad(m);
      say('the streets, as geometry');
      var more = [];
      m.tiles.forEach(function (t) {
        more.push(loadGLB(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + t.road).then(function (g) { t._road = g.scene; },
          function (e) { t._road = null; miss(t.road, e, 'roads'); }));
        more.push(loadGLB(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + t.green).then(function (g) { t._green = g.scene; },
          function (e) { t._green = null; miss(t.green, e, 'parks'); }));
      });
      return Promise.all(more).then(function () { return afterLoad(m); });
    });
  }

  /* Once everything that could arrive has: decide what the missing pieces
     mean, say so on screen, and build what can be built from what is here. */
  function afterLoad(m) {
    var cities = m.tiles.filter(function (t) { return t._city; }).length;
    if (!cities) throw new Error('no building tiles could be loaded from ' + CITY_CONFIG.BASE);

    if (!ATLAS) {
      /* the ground is the roads: bake the same field the atlas holds, from
         the road geometry that is already in memory */
      var baked = bakeGroundFromRoads(m);
      if (baked) {
        ATLAS = baked;
        DATA_WARN = 'ground rebuilt from the roads';
      }
    }
    if (!ROADS) {
      /* the street texture is missing: draw it here from the ribbons, so the
         streets stay one surface on the plate rather than geometry over it */
      var drawn = null;
      try { drawn = bakeRoadsFromRibbons(m); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] could not rebuild the streets: ' + e.message); }
      if (drawn) {
        ROADS = drawn;
        DATA_WARN = (DATA_WARN ? DATA_WARN + ', ' : '') + 'streets rebuilt from the ribbons';
      }
    }
    if (MISSING.length) {
      var el = document.getElementById('ct-missing');
      var lines = MISSING.slice(0, 8).map(function (x) {
        return '<b>' + x.file + '</b> \u00b7 ' + x.what + ' \u00b7 ' + x.why;
      });
      if (MISSING.length > 8) lines.push('+ ' + (MISSING.length - 8) + ' more');
      if (el) {
        el.innerHTML = 'MISSING FROM ' + CITY_CONFIG.BASE.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '') + '<br>' +
                       lines.join('<br>') +
                       (ATLAS && DATA_WARN ? '<br>' + DATA_WARN + ' \u2014 upload the file for the real one' : '');
        el.hidden = false;
      }
      if (!DATA_WARN) DATA_WARN = MISSING.length + ' file' + (MISSING.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' missing';
    }
  }

  /* The ground from the roads, in the browser.

     The atlas on the CDN is exactly this field baked off-line at 1024 cells;
     if it is not there, the road ribbons - which are what it was baked from -
     are already loaded, so the same thing can be built here at 256 cells:
     per cell the lowest road vertex, the gaps filled from the nearest street,
     a little smoothing, encoded the way the atlas is so nothing downstream
     changes. Coarser than the real file, but the plate, the roads and the
     walk all read the same numbers, which is the part that matters. */
  function bakeGroundFromRoads(m) {
    var a = m.atlas, N = 256;
    var lo = new Float32Array(N * N), known = new Uint8Array(N * N);
    for (var i = 0; i < N * N; i++) lo[i] = Infinity;
    var any = false;
    m.tiles.forEach(function (t) {
      if (!t._road) return;
      t._road.traverse(function (o) {
        if (!o.isMesh) return;
        var p = o.geometry.attributes.position;
        for (var k = 0; k < p.count; k++) {
          var gx = Math.floor((p.getX(k) + t.x - a.x0) / a.w * N);
          var gz = Math.floor((p.getZ(k) + t.z - a.z0) / a.h * N);
          if (gx < 0 || gz < 0 || gx >= N || gz >= N) continue;
          var id = gz * N + gx, y = p.getY(k);
          if (y < lo[id]) { lo[id] = y; known[id] = 1; any = true; }
        }
      });
    });
    if (!any) return null;
    /* fill: every unknown cell takes the mean of its known neighbours, pass
       after pass, until the field is complete */
    var next = new Float32Array(N * N), nk = new Uint8Array(N * N), left = 1, guard = 0;
    while (left && guard++ < 400) {
      left = 0; next.set(lo); nk.set(known);
      for (var z = 0; z < N; z++) for (var x = 0; x < N; x++) {
        var id2 = z * N + x;
        if (known[id2]) continue;
        var sum = 0, c = 0;
        for (var dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) for (var dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
          var xx = x + dx, zz = z + dz;
          if (xx < 0 || zz < 0 || xx >= N || zz >= N) continue;
          var j = zz * N + xx;
          if (known[j]) { sum += lo[j]; c++; }
        }
        if (c) { next[id2] = sum / c; nk[id2] = 1; } else left++;
      }
      lo.set(next); known.set(nk);
    }
    /* two box passes take the cell edges off */
    for (var pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
      next.set(lo);
      for (var z2 = 1; z2 < N - 1; z2++) for (var x2 = 1; x2 < N - 1; x2++) {
        var s9 = 0;
        for (var dz2 = -1; dz2 <= 1; dz2++) for (var dx2 = -1; dx2 <= 1; dx2++) s9 += lo[(z2 + dz2) * N + x2 + dx2];
        next[z2 * N + x2] = s9 / 9;
      }
      lo.set(next);
    }
    var mn = Infinity, mx = -Infinity;
    for (var q = 0; q < N * N; q++) { if (lo[q] < mn) mn = lo[q]; if (lo[q] > mx) mx = lo[q]; }
    var span = Math.max(1e-3, mx - mn);
    /* same encoding as the file: 16-bit as R*256+G over [lo,hi] */
    var cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = N; cv.height = N;
    var ctx = cv.getContext('2d'), im = ctx.createImageData(N, N), d = im.data;
    for (var r = 0; r < N * N; r++) {
      var v = Math.round((lo[r] - mn) / span * 65535);
      d[r * 4] = v >> 8; d[r * 4 + 1] = v & 255; d[r * 4 + 2] = 0; d[r * 4 + 3] = 255;
    }
    ctx.putImageData(im, 0, 0);
    a.size = N; a.lo = mn; a.hi = mx;
    var tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
    tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] ground rebuilt from the roads: ' + N + ' cells, ' + mn.toFixed(1) + '..' + mx.toFixed(1) + ' m');
    return { data: lo, tex: tex };
  }

  /* The streets from the ribbons, in the browser.

     ground-roads.webp is the ribbons rasterised off-line. If it is not on the
     CDN, the ribbons themselves are already in memory by now, so the same
     drawing is made here, at 2048 cells instead of 4096, with the same rules:
     u runs 0..1 across the carriageway and overhangs it by the pavement, w is
     the ribbon width, s the distance along. Coarser, and the painted lines
     are faint, but the streets are still one surface on the plate - never a
     second surface over it. R ribbon, G pavement, B paint, A green. */
  function bakeRoadsFromRibbons(m) {
    var a = m.atlas, N = 2048, PX = a.w / N;
    /* raw bytes, not a 2D canvas: a canvas premultiplies by alpha, and alpha
       here is the park mask - every street pixel off a park would lose its
       colour on the way in. Rows are written bottom-up so the texture reads
       exactly like the file does through the same 1 - v in the shader. */
    var d = new Uint8Array(N * N * 4);
    var any = false, tris = 0;
    var tmp = new THREE.Vector3();

    function raster(mesh, tx, tz, fill) {
      var g = mesh.geometry, p = g.attributes.position, ix = g.index;
      var n = ix ? ix.count : p.count;
      var X = new Float32Array(p.count), Z = new Float32Array(p.count);
      mesh.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      for (var k = 0; k < p.count; k++) {
        tmp.set(p.getX(k), p.getY(k), p.getZ(k)).applyMatrix4(mesh.matrixWorld);
        X[k] = (tmp.x + tx - a.x0) / PX; Z[k] = (tmp.z + tz - a.z0) / PX;
      }
      for (var t = 0; t + 2 < n; t += 3) {
        var ia = ix ? ix.getX(t) : t, ib = ix ? ix.getX(t + 1) : t + 1, ic = ix ? ix.getX(t + 2) : t + 2;
        var xa = X[ia], xb = X[ib], xc = X[ic], za = Z[ia], zb = Z[ib], zc = Z[ic];
        var x0 = Math.floor(Math.min(xa, xb, xc)), x1 = Math.ceil(Math.max(xa, xb, xc));
        var z0 = Math.floor(Math.min(za, zb, zc)), z1 = Math.ceil(Math.max(za, zb, zc));
        if (x1 < 0 || z1 < 0 || x0 >= N || z0 >= N) continue;
        x0 = Math.max(x0, 0); z0 = Math.max(z0, 0); x1 = Math.min(x1, N - 1); z1 = Math.min(z1, N - 1);
        var det = (xb - xa) * (zc - za) - (xc - xa) * (zb - za);
        if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-9) continue;
        tris++;
        for (var gz = z0; gz <= z1; gz++) {
          var cz = gz + 0.5;
          for (var gx = x0; gx <= x1; gx++) {
            var cx = gx + 0.5;
            /* barycentric weights, each the sub-triangle opposite its vertex */
            var l0 = ((xb - cx) * (zc - cz) - (xc - cx) * (zb - cz)) / det;
            var l1 = ((xc - cx) * (za - cz) - (xa - cx) * (zc - cz)) / det;
            var l2 = 1 - l0 - l1;
            if (l0 < -1e-4 || l1 < -1e-4 || l2 < -1e-4) continue;
            fill(((N - 1 - gz) * N + gx) * 4, ia, ib, ic, l0, l1, l2);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    m.tiles.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t._road) t._road.traverse(function (o) {
        if (!o.isMesh || !o.geometry.attributes.uv) return;
        var uv = o.geometry.attributes.uv, uv2 = o.geometry.attributes.uv2 || uv;
        raster(o, t.x, t.z, function (id, ia, ib, ic, l0, l1, l2) {
          var u = l0 * uv.getX(ia) + l1 * uv.getX(ib) + l2 * uv.getX(ic);
          var w = Math.max(4, l0 * uv2.getX(ia) + l1 * uv2.getX(ib) + l2 * uv2.getX(ic));
          var umin = Math.min(uv.getX(ia), uv.getX(ib), uv.getX(ic));
          var umax = Math.max(uv.getX(ia), uv.getX(ib), uv.getX(ic));
          var wc = w / Math.max(1e-3, umax - umin);          /* carriageway, metres */
          var pave = (u < 0 || u > 1);
          /* kerb lines and one centre line, as the file has them */
          var lineW = 0.8 / Math.max(wc, 1);
          var edge = Math.min(u, 1 - u);
          var line = (edge >= 0 && edge < lineW) || (wc >= 7 && Math.abs(u - 0.5) < lineW * 0.5);
          d[id] = 255;
          if (pave) d[id + 1] = 255;
          /* a 0.8 m line in a 2.9 m cell: keep its coverage, not a full cell */
          if (line) d[id + 2] = Math.max(d[id + 2], Math.round(255 * Math.min(1, 0.8 / PX * 1.5)));
          any = true;
        });
      });
      if (t._green) t._green.traverse(function (o) {
        if (!o.isMesh) return;
        raster(o, t.x, t.z, function (id) { d[id + 3] = 255; });
      });
    });
    if (!any) return null;
    var tex = new THREE.DataTexture(d, N, N, THREE.RGBAFormat, THREE.UnsignedByteType);
    tex.wrapS = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping; tex.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
    tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    tex.generateMipmaps = true; tex.anisotropy = 8;
    tex.flipY = false; tex.premultiplyAlpha = false; tex.unpackAlignment = 1;
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
    if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] streets rebuilt from the ribbons: ' + N + ' cells, ' + tris + ' triangles');
    return tex;
  }

  /* ================= scene ================= */
  var scene, cam, rend, clock, sky, logo, portals = [], markers = [], raf = null;
  var yaw = 0, pitch = 0, hadLock = false;
  var pos = new THREE.Vector3();
  var vel = new THREE.Vector3();
  var keys = {};
  var running = false, near = null, runMode = false;
  var grounded = true, jumpTap = false;     /* the jump: Space on a keyboard, a double tap on a phone */

  /* The plate is a 384-square grid displaced from the atlas at its
     vertices and flat between them. Anything that stands on the ground has to
     stand on THAT surface - the triangles you see - or it floats over a dip
     and sinks into a crest (a metre on the hills). So groundAt reads the
     atlas at the plate's own vertices and interpolates across the same two
     triangles per cell the GPU draws. */
  var PLATE_SEG = 384, PLATE_H = null;
  function groundAt(x, z) {
    if (!MANI || !MANI.atlas) return 2550;
    var a = MANI.atlas;
    if (!ATLAS) return a.lo + 10;
    var S = PLATE_SEG, S1 = S + 1;
    if (!PLATE_H || PLATE_H.src !== ATLAS) {
      PLATE_H = new Float32Array(S1 * S1); PLATE_H.src = ATLAS;
      for (var j = 0; j <= S; j++) for (var i = 0; i <= S; i++) PLATE_H[j * S1 + i] = atlasAt(a.x0 + i * a.w / S, a.z0 + j * a.h / S);
    }
    var fx = (x - a.x0) / a.w * S, fz = (z - a.z0) / a.h * S;
    var ix = Math.max(0, Math.min(S - 1, Math.floor(fx))), iz = Math.max(0, Math.min(S - 1, Math.floor(fz)));
    var u = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fx - ix)), v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fz - iz));
    var h00 = PLATE_H[iz * S1 + ix], h10 = PLATE_H[iz * S1 + ix + 1], h01 = PLATE_H[(iz + 1) * S1 + ix], h11 = PLATE_H[(iz + 1) * S1 + ix + 1];
    /* PlaneGeometry splits each cell along (ix, iz+1)-(ix+1, iz) */
    return u + v <= 1 ? h00 + u * (h10 - h00) + v * (h01 - h00)
                      : h11 + (1 - u) * (h01 - h11) + (1 - v) * (h10 - h11);
  }
  function atlasAt(x, z) {
    /* Street level, from the single ground atlas. Bilinear, so it is smooth
       everywhere including across what used to be tile joins. */
    if (!MANI || !MANI.atlas) return 2550;
    var a = MANI.atlas;
    if (!ATLAS) return a.lo + 10;     /* only before afterLoad has run */
    var N = a.size, D = ATLAS.data;
    var fx = (x - a.x0) / a.w * N - 0.5;
    var fz = (z - a.z0) / a.h * N - 0.5;
    var x0 = Math.max(0, Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor(fx)));
    var z0 = Math.max(0, Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor(fz)));
    var x1 = Math.min(N - 1, x0 + 1), z1 = Math.min(N - 1, z0 + 1);
    var tx = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fx - x0)), tz = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fz - z0));
    var p = D[z0 * N + x0], q = D[z0 * N + x1], r = D[z1 * N + x0], u = D[z1 * N + x1];
    return (p * (1 - tx) + q * tx) * (1 - tz) + (r * (1 - tx) + u * tx) * tz;
  }

  /* The grid is one cell per two metres and a footprint rounds OUTWARD into
     it, so the cell just in front of a door that is flush with the real wall
     is often still marked solid - the viewer would stop a stride short of a
     frame they can see. Each door gets its doorstep cleared: a few cells in
     front of it, out to the street, so you can walk right up to it. */
  function clearDoorstep(x, z, dx, dz) {
    if (!MANI) return;
    var s = MANI.tileSize, g = MANI.grid, half = s / 2, cell = s / g;
    for (var out = 0.0; out <= 3.2; out += cell * 0.5) {
      for (var side = -1.0; side <= 1.0; side += cell * 0.5) {
        var px = x + dx * out - dz * side, pz = z + dz * out + dx * side;
        for (var i = 0; i < TILES.length; i++) {
          var t = TILES[i];
          var lx = px - t.x + half, lz = pz - t.z + half;
          if (lx < 0 || lz < 0 || lx >= s || lz >= s) continue;
          var bits = OCC[t.ix + '_' + t.iy];
          if (!bits) break;
          var idx = ((lz / s * g) | 0) * g + ((lx / s * g) | 0);
          bits[idx >> 3] &= ~(0x80 >> (idx & 7));
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function blocked(x, z) {
    if (!MANI) return false;
    if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at(x, z) < 0.55) return true;     /* the edge of the platform: an invisible wall */
    var s = MANI.tileSize, g = MANI.grid, half = s / 2;
    for (var i = 0; i < TILES.length; i++) {
      var t = TILES[i];
      var lx = x - t.x + half, lz = z - t.z + half;
      if (lx < 0 || lz < 0 || lx >= s || lz >= s) continue;
      var bits = OCC[t.ix + '_' + t.iy];
      if (!bits) return false;
      var cx = (lx / s * g) | 0, cz = (lz / s * g) | 0;
      var idx = cz * g + cx;
      return (bits[idx >> 3] & (0x80 >> (idx & 7))) !== 0;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /* The graphic pass, shared by everything that draws a surface.

     These three do the whole job between them: poster() collapses shading
     into flat steps, inkEdge() finds the silhouette from the face normal and
     the view, and dots() lays a screen-print halftone over the mid-tones.
     They are written once here and spliced into every fragment shader, so a
     look is a handful of numbers in LOOK rather than four shaders to keep in
     step with each other. */
  var LOOK_GLSL = [
    'uniform float uPost, uLine, uDots, uDotPx;',
    'uniform vec3 uAccent, uPaper;',
    'float lum(vec3 c){ return dot(c, vec3(0.2126,0.7152,0.0722)); }',
    'vec3 poster(vec3 c){',
    '  if(uPost < 0.5) return c;',
    '  float v = lum(c);',
    '  float q = floor(v*uPost + 0.5)/uPost;',
    '  return c * (v > 0.001 ? q/v : 0.0);',
    '}',
    /* the dot is laid in screen space and turned 45 degrees, the way a press
       screen is, so it never lines up with a wall or a kerb */
    'vec3 dots(vec3 c){',
    '  if(uDots < 0.004) return c;',
    '  vec2 p = gl_FragCoord.xy / max(1.0, uDotPx);',
    '  vec2 r = vec2(p.x - p.y, p.x + p.y) * 0.70710678;',
    '  float d = length(fract(r) - 0.5) * 2.0;',
    '  float on = step(d, clamp(lum(c), 0.0, 1.0) * 1.42);',
    '  return mix(c, c*on, uDots);',
    '}',
    'vec3 inkEdge(vec3 c, vec3 n, vec3 w, vec3 ink){',
    '  if(uLine < 0.004) return c;',
    '  float rim = 1.0 - abs(dot(n, normalize(cameraPosition - w)));',
    '  return mix(c, ink, smoothstep(1.0 - uLine, 1.0, rim));',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  function lookUniforms() {
    var L = CITY_CONFIG.LOOK || {};
    return {
      uPost:   { value: L.POSTER || 0 },
      uLine:   { value: L.LINE   || 0 },
      uDots:   { value: L.DOTS   || 0 },
      uDotPx:  { value: L.DOTPX  || 5 },
      uAccent: { value: new THREE.Color(L.ACCENT || CHAMPAGNE) },
      uPaper:  { value: new THREE.Color(L.PAPER  || MIST) }
    };
  }

  function buildingMaterial() {
    /* No normals and no UVs come out of the exporter - it ships positions only,
       which is a third of the bytes. The face normal is recovered in the
       fragment shader from the screen-space derivatives of world position,
       which gives exact flat shading for free, and the facade is drawn
       procedurally on the same 3.2 x 3.0 m window grid the Unreal material
       used, with 45% of the panes lit warm or cool. */
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({
        uInk:   { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
        uWall:  { value: new THREE.Color(0x1A2230) },
        uWarm:  { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMPAGNE) },
        uCool:  { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMBRAY) },
        uFog:   { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG },
        uBaseY: { value: 2544.55 }
      }, lookUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW;',
        'varying vec3 vL;',
        'uniform float uBaseY;',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec4 w = modelMatrix * vec4(position,1.0);',
        '  vW = w.xyz;',
        /* Tile-local, and with the 2.5 km of altitude taken off. The facade
           pattern is hashed from this rather than from vW: sin() at a world x
           of -11000 and a y of 2550 has no precision left, and whole walls came
           back as a single window. Here the numbers stay inside about 1000. */
        '  vL = vec3(position.x, position.y - uBaseY, position.z);',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW;',
        'varying vec3 vL;',
        'uniform vec3 uInk,uWall,uWarm,uCool;',
        'uniform float uFog;',
        LOOK_GLSL,
        'float h21(vec2 p){',
        '  vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);',
        '  q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33);',
        '  return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z);',
        '}',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec3 n = normalize(cross(dFdx(vL), dFdy(vL)));',
        '  float up = abs(n.y);',
        '  vec3 col;',
        '  if(up > 0.55){',
        '    col = uWall * 0.34;',
        '  } else {',
        '    float u = (abs(n.x) > abs(n.z)) ? vL.z : vL.x;',
        '    vec2 cell = vec2(floor(u/3.2), floor(vL.y/3.0));',
        '    vec2 f    = vec2(fract(u/3.2), fract(vL.y/3.0));',
        '    float pane = step(0.14,f.x)*step(f.x,0.86)*step(0.20,f.y)*step(f.y,0.84);',
        '    float lit  = step(0.55, h21(cell)) * pane;',
        /* at LOOK.POSTER the two window colours collapse onto the one accent
           and the one paper, which is what makes a printed frame read */
        '    vec3 glow  = mix(uWarm, uCool, step(0.72, h21(cell*1.7+11.3)));',
        '    if(uPost > 0.5) glow = mix(uAccent, uPaper, step(0.72, h21(cell*1.7+11.3)));',
        '    col = mix(uWall*0.42, glow*0.95, lit);',
        /* a touch of sheen on whichever way the wall faces, so a corner still
           reads as a corner when none of its windows are lit */
        '    col *= 0.82 + 0.26*abs(n.x);',
        '  }',
        '  col = poster(col);',
        '  col = inkEdge(col, n, vW, uInk);',
        '  col = dots(col);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog,0.0,1.0)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    m.extensions = { derivatives: true };
    return m;
  }

  /* ======================= THE FACADES (v2) =======================
     The buildings as built things, in the white of the studio's models.

     Every building in the core keeps its cadastre box; the box is now
     drawn pale, lit by a moon and the sky, and dressed:
       near the walker  (FACADES.RADIUS) real relief, as instanced geometry
                        laid on every wall that faces the open: plinth,
                        string courses, window surrounds, sills, pediments,
                        arches, keystones, balconies, pilasters, the cornice
                        with its dentils and the parapet - one of five
                        looks per building, with its own bay rhythm, and
                        the glass of every window as its own pane that
                        lights, switches and flickers
       further away     the same windows and a drawn relief in the wall
                        shader, so the far city reads as the same city
     Which walls face the open, each building's ring, its floors and its
     look come from facades-v1.bin (built off-line from the same tiles).
     The doors are glass bays set into the wall - the frame of folded
     crystal from the lalaguna portal study, the bay cut flush, the
     relief stopping at its edge.

     FACADES in CITY_CONFIG:
       ON        false puts the night boxes back exactly as they were
       FILE      the facades file, next to the tiles
       RADIUS    metres of real relief around the walker
       REBUILD   metres walked before the relief is laid again
       WALL      the white of the walls; SHADOWS false saves the GPU
       EXPOSURE  the tone of the whole picture
     ================================================================= */
  var FACX = CITY_CONFIG.FACADES || {};
  var FACADES_ON = FACX.ON !== false;
  var FAC = null;            /* the buildings, parsed */
  var RELIEF = null;         /* the instanced relief around the walker */
  var moon = null, hemi = null, wallShader = null, paneMat = null;
  var HOLES = [];            /* the doors' openings, cut out of the walls in the shader: [cx, cz, nx, nz, halfW, H, baseY] */

  function loadFacades(url) {
    return fetch(url).then(function (r) {
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
      return r.arrayBuffer();
    }).then(parseFacades);
  }

  function parseFacades(buf) {
    var dv = new DataView(buf);
    if (String.fromCharCode(dv.getUint8(0), dv.getUint8(1), dv.getUint8(2), dv.getUint8(3)) !== 'FAC2') throw new Error('not a facades file');
    var count = dv.getUint32(4, true), q = dv.getFloat32(8, true), off = 12;
    var style = new Uint16Array(count), floors = new Uint8Array(count), top = new Float32Array(count);
    var ringStart = new Uint32Array(count), ringLen = new Uint8Array(count), flagStart = new Uint32Array(count);
    var minx = new Float32Array(count), maxx = new Float32Array(count), minz = new Float32Array(count), maxz = new Float32Array(count);
    var verts = [], flags = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < count; b++) {
      style[b] = dv.getUint8(off); floors[b] = dv.getUint8(off + 1);
      top[b] = 2500 + dv.getUint16(off + 2, true) / 10;
      var n = dv.getUint8(off + 4); off += 5;
      var x = dv.getInt32(off, true), z = dv.getInt32(off + 4, true); off += 8;
      ringStart[b] = verts.length >> 1; ringLen[b] = n;
      var mx0 = x * q, mx1 = x * q, mz0 = z * q, mz1 = z * q;
      verts.push(x * q, z * q);
      for (var i = 1; i < n; i++) {
        x += dv.getInt16(off, true); z += dv.getInt16(off + 2, true); off += 4;
        var wx = x * q, wz = z * q;
        verts.push(wx, wz);
        if (wx < mx0) mx0 = wx; if (wx > mx1) mx1 = wx; if (wz < mz0) mz0 = wz; if (wz > mz1) mz1 = wz;
      }
      minx[b] = mx0; maxx[b] = mx1; minz[b] = mz0; maxz[b] = mz1;
      flagStart[b] = flags.length;
      for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) flags.push(dv.getUint8(off + k));   /* bit 0 faces the open, bits 1-7 the stand-off in cm */
      off += n;
    }
    /* a coarse grid so the walker's surroundings are a handful of lookups */
    var CELL = 64, hash = {};
    for (var c = 0; c < count; c++) {
      for (var cx = Math.floor(minx[c] / CELL); cx <= Math.floor(maxx[c] / CELL); cx++) {
        for (var cz = Math.floor(minz[c] / CELL); cz <= Math.floor(maxz[c] / CELL); cz++) {
          var key = cx + ',' + cz;
          (hash[key] || (hash[key] = [])).push(c);
        }
      }
    }
    return { n: count, style: style, floors: floors, top: top, ringStart: ringStart, ringLen: ringLen, flagStart: flagStart,
             verts: Float32Array.from(verts), flags: Uint8Array.from(flags), hash: hash, CELL: CELL,
             minx: minx, maxx: maxx, minz: minz, maxz: maxz, portals: {} };
  }

  /* ---- the windows, shared by the far walls and the near panes ----
     Each window keeps its own clock: it switches on or off every minute
     or so, stutters like a tube for a moment when it comes on; some have a
     television in them, some have someone walking past. */
  var WIN_GLSL = [
    'float h21(vec2 p){ vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031); q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33); return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z); }',
    'float windowLit(vec2 cell, vec2 f, float t, out vec3 glow){',
    '  float s1 = h21(cell), s2 = h21(cell * 1.7 + 11.3), s3 = h21(cell * 2.3 + 5.1), s4 = h21(cell * 3.1 + 9.7);',
    '  float on = step(0.52, h21(cell + 0.37));',
    '  glow = mix(uWarm, uCool, step(0.72, s2));',
    '  float b = 0.8 + 0.4 * s1;',
    '  if (s3 < 0.14) { b *= 0.85; glow = mix(glow, uCool, 0.65); }',
    '  return on * b;',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  /* the walls: three's own lit material, pale, flat-shaded from the mesh
     (the tiles ship no normals), and the facade drawn into it: windows that
     live, a drawn relief, the street's lamps warming the lowest metres */
  function buildingMaterialV2() {
    var m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: new THREE.Color(FACX.WALL || '#E3DDE0').convertSRGBToLinear(),
      roughness: 0.94, metalness: 0.0, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    m.onBeforeCompile = function (sh) {
      sh.uniforms.uBaseY = { value: 2544.55 };
      sh.uniforms.uT = { value: 0 };
      sh.uniforms.uReliefR = { value: 0 };
      sh.uniforms.uWarm = { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMPAGNE).convertSRGBToLinear() };
      sh.uniforms.uCool = { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMBRAY).convertSRGBToLinear() };
      /* the doors: their openings are cut out of the wall here, so the bay behind the glass is real */
      var ha = [], hb = [];
      for (var hi = 0; hi < 4; hi++) {
        var h = HOLES[hi];
        ha.push(h ? new THREE.Vector4(h[0], h[1], h[2], h[3]) : new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 0, 0));
        hb.push(h ? new THREE.Vector4(h[4], h[5], h[6], 1) : new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 0, 0));
      }
      sh.uniforms.uHoleA = { value: ha }; sh.uniforms.uHoleB = { value: hb };
      sh.vertexShader = 'varying vec3 vL; varying vec3 vWp; uniform float uBaseY;\n' +
        sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <begin_vertex>',
          '#include <begin_vertex>\n  vL = vec3(position.x, position.y - uBaseY, position.z);\n  vWp = (modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).xyz;');
      sh.uniforms.uMask = maskU; sh.uniforms.uMaskBox = maskBoxU; sh.uniforms.uMaskOn = maskOnU;
      sh.uniforms.uMat = matU; sh.uniforms.uMatBox = matBoxU; sh.uniforms.uMatOn = matOnU; sh.uniforms.uYB0 = YB0U;
      sh.uniforms.uAtD = ATU.d; sh.uniforms.uAtN = ATU.n; sh.uniforms.uAtOn = ATU.on;
      sh.fragmentShader = 'varying vec3 vL; varying vec3 vWp; uniform float uT, uReliefR; uniform vec3 uWarm, uCool; uniform vec4 uHoleA[4], uHoleB[4];\n' +
        'uniform sampler2D uAtD, uAtN; uniform float uAtOn; vec3 gTexN = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0); float gOnWall = 0.0; float gWs = 1.0; vec2 gAAw = vec2(0.01);\n' + WIN_GLSL + '\n' + MASK_GLSL + '\n' + BOGOTA_GLSL + '\n' + FACADE_GLSL + '\n' +
        sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <emissivemap_fragment>', FACADE_FRAG);
      wallShader = sh;
    };
    return m;
  }

  /* the near panes: glass, as instances, with the same windows in them */
  function paneMaterial() {
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: {
        uT: { value: 0 }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG }, uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
        uWarm: { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMPAGNE) }, uCool: { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMBRAY) }
      },
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute vec3 aCell;',
        'varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vCell; varying vec3 vW;',
        'void main(){',
        '  vUv = uv; vCell = aCell;',
        '  vec3 p = position;',
        '  #ifdef USE_INSTANCING',
        '  p = (instanceMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0)).xyz;',
        '  #endif',
        '  vec4 w = modelMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0); vW = w.xyz;',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vCell; varying vec3 vW;',
        'uniform float uT, uFog; uniform vec3 uInk, uWarm, uCool;',
        WIN_GLSL,
        'void main(){',
        /* the cell is whole numbers: a varying is not quite constant across a
           triangle, and the hash turns a millionth into a different window */
        '  vec2 cell = floor(vCell.xy + 0.5);',
        '  vec3 glow; float lit = windowLit(cell, vUv, uT, glow);',
        /* the glass: a dark pane with the sky in it, its edge a touch darker
           where it meets the frame; lit, the room behind it */
        '  vec3 dark = uInk * 1.4 + uCool * 0.10 * vUv.y;',
        '  float edge = smoothstep(0.0, 0.07, vUv.x) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.93, vUv.x) * smoothstep(0.0, 0.07, vUv.y) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.93, vUv.y);',
        '  vec3 room = glow * (0.55 + 0.45 * edge) * (0.85 + 0.15 * vCell.z);',
        '  vec3 col = mix(dark * (0.6 + 0.4 * edge), room, lit);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d * d * uFog * uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog, 0.0, 1.0)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n')
    });
    return m;
  }

  /* ---- the pieces every facade is built from, each a unit the instance
     scales: a box from y 0..1 standing on the wall plane (z 0..1 outward),
     a pediment, an arch, a pane ---- */
  function reliefGeometries() {
    var box = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1); box.translate(0, 0.5, 0.5);
    var tri = new THREE.Shape(); tri.moveTo(-0.5, 0); tri.lineTo(0.5, 0); tri.lineTo(0, 1); tri.closePath();
    var ped = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(tri, { depth: 1, bevelEnabled: false });
    var ring = new THREE.Shape();
    ring.absarc(0, 0, 0.5, 0, Math.PI, false); ring.lineTo(-0.36, 0); ring.absarc(0, 0, 0.36, Math.PI, 0, true); ring.closePath();
    var arch = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(ring, { depth: 1, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: 10 });
    var pane = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1); pane.translate(0, 0.5, 0);
    return { box: box, ped: ped, arch: arch, pane: pane };
  }

  function buildRelief() {
    var G = reliefGeometries();
    var cap = FACX.MAX_INSTANCES || 90000;
    var white = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(FACX.WALL || '#E3DDE0').convertSRGBToLinear(), roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0.0 });
    function inst(geo, n, mat, colour) {
      var m = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, n);
      m.count = 0; m.frustumCulled = false; m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true;
      m.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      if (colour) { m.instanceColor = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(n * 3), 3); m.instanceColor.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); }
      return m;
    }
    paneMat = paneMaterial();
    var panes = inst(G.pane, Math.floor(cap / 3), paneMat, false);
    panes.castShadow = false; panes.receiveShadow = false;
    panes.geometry.setAttribute('aCell', new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(Math.floor(cap / 3) * 3), 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    var bushG = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 1); bushG.translate(0, 1, 0);
    var bushM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.95, metalness: 0, flatShading: true });
    RELIEF = {
      bushes: inst(bushG, 4000, bushM, true),
      boxes: inst(G.box, cap, white, true), peds: inst(G.ped, 8000, white, true), arches: inst(G.arch, 8000, white, true), panes: panes,
      cap: cap, nb: 0, np: 0, na: 0, nw: 0, lastX: 1e9, lastZ: 1e9, radius: FACX.RADIUS || 170, built: 0
    };
    return [RELIEF.boxes, RELIEF.peds, RELIEF.arches, RELIEF.panes, RELIEF.bushes];
  }

  /* ---- laying the relief on one wall ----
     W is the wall's frame: its start, its direction t along it, its outward
     normal n, its length; every piece is placed at u along it, at height y,
     dz out of the plane, with a width w, a height h and a depth d. */
  var W = { os: 1, x1: 0, z1: 0, tx: 1, tz: 0, nx: 0, nz: 1, L: 0, tint: [1, 1, 1], seed: 0, bi: 0, wi: 0, off: 0, portal: null };
  function putInto(mesh, counter, w, h, d, u, y, dz) {
    var i = RELIEF[counter];
    if (i >= mesh.count0) return;
    var a = mesh.instanceMatrix.array, o = i * 16;
    a[o] = -W.tx * w * W.os; a[o + 1] = 0; a[o + 2] = -W.tz * w * W.os; a[o + 3] = 0;
    a[o + 4] = 0; a[o + 5] = h; a[o + 6] = 0; a[o + 7] = 0;
    a[o + 8] = W.nx * d; a[o + 9] = 0; a[o + 10] = W.nz * d; a[o + 11] = 0;
    a[o + 12] = W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * (dz + W.off); a[o + 13] = y; a[o + 14] = W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * (dz + W.off); a[o + 15] = 1;
    if (mesh.instanceColor) { var c = mesh.instanceColor.array; c[i * 3] = W.tint[0]; c[i * 3 + 1] = W.tint[1]; c[i * 3 + 2] = W.tint[2]; }
    RELIEF[counter] = i + 1;
  }
  function box(w, h, d, u, y, dz) { putInto(RELIEF.boxes, 'nb', w, h, d, u, y, dz || 0); }
  function ped(w, h, d, u, y) { putInto(RELIEF.peds, 'np', w, h, d, u, y, 0); }
  function arch(w, d, u, y) { putInto(RELIEF.arches, 'na', w, w, d, u, y, 0); }
  function pane(w, h, u, y, k) {
    var i = RELIEF.nw;
    if (i >= RELIEF.panes.count0) return;
    putInto(RELIEF.panes, 'nw', w, h, 1, u, y, 0.035);
    var c = RELIEF.panes.geometry.attributes.aCell.array;
    c[i * 3] = (Math.round(W.seed * 100) % 50000) + k * 7; c[i * 3 + 1] = W.wi * 13 + k + (W.bi % 97) * 101; c[i * 3 + 2] = (k * 0.618) % 1;
  }
  /* a span the portal owns: nothing of the facade is laid across it */
  function clearOf(u0, u1) { var p = W.portal; return !p || u1 <= p.u0 || u0 >= p.u1; }
  function courseAcross(h, d, y, dz) {
    /* a band along the whole wall, split around the portal if one is there */
    var p = W.portal;
    if (!p || y >= p.H) { box(W.L + 0.04, h, d, W.L / 2, y, dz); return; }
    if (p.u0 > 0.3) box(p.u0, h, d, p.u0 / 2, y, dz);
    if (W.L - p.u1 > 0.3) box(W.L - p.u1, h, d, (p.u1 + W.L) / 2, y, dz);
  }

  function dressWall(kind, bay, F, yb, y1, street) {
    var L = W.L, py = Math.min(street - 0.15, yb);
    if (kind === 'G') {
      if (!BLOCKS) box(L + 0.04, (yb + 0.6) - py, 0.2, L / 2, py, 0);
      box(L + 0.1, 0.5, 0.25, L / 2, y1 - 0.5, 0);
      return;
    }
    if (!BLOCKS && yb + 0.08 > py + 0.05) box(L + 0.04, (yb + 0.08) - py, 0.10, L / 2, py, 0);
    if (kind === 'B' || kind === 'E') {
      box(L + 0.12, 0.34, 0.24, L / 2, y1 - 0.34, 0);
    } else {
      box(L + 0.24, 0.14, 0.14, L / 2, y1 - 0.86, 0);
      box(L + 0.40, 0.34, 0.40, L / 2, y1 - 0.64, 0);
      box(L + 0.08, 0.5, 0.24, L / 2, y1 - 0.22, -0.24);
    }
  }

  /* planters with a bush in them, in front of the ground-floor bays that have
     no opening - the same bays the wall shader leaves blank */
  function plantWall(top) {
    var L = W.L, Bq = wallBays(L, W.ws), capY = top - 0.35, keep = W.tint;
    function planter(u) {
      if (!clearOf(u - 0.8, u + 0.8)) return;
      if (u < 0.9 || u > W.L - 0.9) return;
      var gB = groundAt(W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * 1.2, W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * 1.2);
      if (Math.min(W.f0, capY) - gB < 1.2) return;
      var pw = Math.min(1.5, Math.max(0.9, Bq.bay * 0.55));
      /* the planter's footprint, and a step in front of it, must be open pavement */
      var off = W.off + 0.04, ok = true;
      [[-0.5, 0.05], [0.5, 0.05], [-0.5, 0.6], [0.5, 0.6], [0, 1.1]].forEach(function (c) {
        var uu = u + c[0] * pw, dd = off + c[1];
        if (inBuilding(W.x1 + W.tx * uu + W.nx * dd, W.z1 + W.tz * uu + W.nz * dd, -1)) ok = false;
      });
      if (!ok) return;
      var cx = W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * (off + 0.26), cz = W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * (off + 0.26);
      for (var hq = 0; hq < HOLES.length; hq++) { var H = HOLES[hq]; if (Math.hypot(cx - H[0], cz - H[1]) < H[4] + 2.4) return; }
      SOLID.rb.push(cx, cz, W.tx, W.tz, pw / 2, 0.26);
      W.tint = [0.50, 0.50, 0.48]; box(pw, 0.44, 0.52, u, gB - 0.05, 0.04);
      var g = hashI(W.ws * 64 + Math.round(u * 10));
      W.tint = g < 0.4 ? [0.10, 0.22, 0.08] : g < 0.75 ? [0.16, 0.30, 0.10] : [0.24, 0.30, 0.12];
      putInto(RELIEF.bushes, 'nbu', pw * 0.4, 0.30 + 0.25 * g, 0.2, u, gB + 0.38, 0.3);
      W.tint = keep;
    }
    if (Bq.nb === 0) { if (L >= 2.2) planter(L / 2); return; }
    for (var k = 0; k < Bq.nb; k++) {
      var uc = Bq.m + (k + 0.5) * Bq.bay;
      var gB = groundAt(W.x1 + W.tx * uc + W.nx * 1.2, W.z1 + W.tz * uc + W.nz * 1.2);
      if (gfKind(W.ws, k) === 3 || Math.min(W.f0, capY) - gB < 2.5) planter(uc);
    }
  }

  function kindOf(style) {
    var r = (style + 0.5) / 1000;
    return r < 0.26 ? 'A' : r < 0.50 ? 'B' : r < 0.70 ? 'C' : r < 0.88 ? 'D' : 'E';
  }

  function updateRelief(px, pz, force) {
    if (!FAC || !RELIEF) return;
    if (!force && Math.hypot(px - RELIEF.lastX, pz - RELIEF.lastZ) < (LIFE && LIFE.driving ? 40 : (FACX.REBUILD || 18))) return;
    RELIEF.lastX = px; RELIEF.lastZ = pz;
    var R = FACX.RADIUS || 170;
    RELIEF.nb = RELIEF.np = RELIEF.na = RELIEF.nw = RELIEF.nbu = 0; RELIEF.bushes.count0 = 4000; SOLID.rb.length = 0;
    lifeSignsBegin();
    RELIEF.boxes.count0 = RELIEF.cap; RELIEF.peds.count0 = 8000; RELIEF.arches.count0 = 8000; RELIEF.panes.count0 = Math.floor(RELIEF.cap / 3);
    /* the buildings in reach, nearest first, so when the budget runs out it is the far ones that go without */
    var CELL = FAC.CELL, seen = {}, cand = [];
    for (var cx = Math.floor((px - R) / CELL); cx <= Math.floor((px + R) / CELL); cx++) {
      for (var cz = Math.floor((pz - R) / CELL); cz <= Math.floor((pz + R) / CELL); cz++) {
        var list = FAC.hash[cx + ',' + cz]; if (!list) continue;
        for (var q = 0; q < list.length; q++) {
          var b = list[q]; if (seen[b]) continue; seen[b] = 1;
          var ddx = Math.max(FAC.minx[b] - px, 0, px - FAC.maxx[b]), ddz = Math.max(FAC.minz[b] - pz, 0, pz - FAC.maxz[b]);
          var dist = Math.hypot(ddx, ddz);
          if (dist < R) cand.push([dist, b]);
        }
      }
    }
    cand.sort(function (a, b) { return a[0] - b[0]; });
    var achieved = R, verts = FAC.verts, laid = 0;
    for (var c = 0; c < cand.length; c++) {
      var bi = cand[c][1];
      if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at((FAC.minx[bi] + FAC.maxx[bi]) / 2, (FAC.minz[bi] + FAC.maxz[bi]) / 2) < 0.5) continue;
      var before = RELIEF.nb;
      var n = FAC.ringLen[bi], s = FAC.ringStart[bi], F = FAC.floors[bi], y1 = FAC.top[bi], yb = y1 - 3 * F;
      var st = FAC.style[bi], kind = kindFor(bi, st);
      var bay = 3.0 + 0.9 * ((st * 7.31) % 1);
      var tn = 0.9 + 0.1 * ((st * 3.7) % 1), tw = ((st * 5.3) % 1) - 0.5;
      var tint = trimTint(bi, tn, tw);
      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        var fl = FAC.flags[FAC.flagStart[bi] + i];
        if (!(fl & 1)) continue;
        var x1 = verts[(s + i) * 2], z1 = verts[(s + i) * 2 + 1], j = (i + 1) % n, x2 = verts[(s + j) * 2], z2 = verts[(s + j) * 2 + 1];
        var dx = x2 - x1, dz = z2 - z1, L = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        if (L < 1.6) continue;
        W.os = FAC.os ? FAC.os[bi] : 1;
        W.x1 = x1; W.z1 = z1; W.tx = dx / L; W.tz = dz / L; W.nx = W.os * dz / L; W.nz = -W.os * dx / L; W.L = L; W.tint = tint; W.seed = st + bi * 0.001; W.bi = bi; W.wi = i;
        W.portal = FAC.portals[bi + ':' + i] || null;
        W.off = BLOCKS ? 0.02 : (fl >> 1) / 100 + 0.01;   /* the blocks' walls are the footprint itself: nothing stands off */              /* the relief stands off where the real wall bulges past the simplified one */
        var street = groundAt((x1 + x2) / 2 + W.nx * 1.5, (z1 + z2) / 2 + W.nz * 1.5);
        W.ws = wallSeed(bi, i); W.f0 = FAC.f0 ? FAC.f0[FAC.flagStart[bi] + i] : street + GF_H;
        /* a wall that is not drawn (inside a building of its own height) carries nothing */
        if (BLOCKS && FAC.wfoot && FAC.wfoot[FAC.flagStart[bi] + i] >= y1 - 0.05) continue;
        dressWall(kind, bay, F, yb, y1, street);
        lifeWall(bi, kind, F, yb, y1, street);
        if (BLOCKS) plantWall(y1);
      }
      if (RELIEF.nb >= RELIEF.cap - 400 || RELIEF.nw >= RELIEF.panes.count0 - 40) { achieved = cand[c][0]; RELIEF.nb = before; break; }
      laid++;
    }
    RELIEF.boxes.count = RELIEF.nb; RELIEF.peds.count = RELIEF.np; RELIEF.arches.count = RELIEF.na; RELIEF.panes.count = RELIEF.nw; RELIEF.bushes.count = RELIEF.nbu;
    [RELIEF.boxes, RELIEF.peds, RELIEF.arches, RELIEF.panes, RELIEF.bushes].forEach(function (m) {
      m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; if (m.instanceColor) m.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    });
    RELIEF.panes.geometry.attributes.aCell.needsUpdate = true;
    RELIEF.radius = achieved; RELIEF.built = laid;
    lifeSignsEnd();
    if (wallShader) wallShader.uniforms.uReliefR.value = achieved;
  }

  /* ---- the door as a glass bay in the wall, in the crystal frame ---- */
  function hostWall(spot) {
    /* the wall the snapped door stands on: nearest exposed wall facing the approach */
    if (!FAC) return null;
    var best = null, CELL = FAC.CELL, cx0 = Math.floor(spot.x / CELL), cz0 = Math.floor(spot.z / CELL);
    for (var cx = cx0 - 1; cx <= cx0 + 1; cx++) for (var cz = cz0 - 1; cz <= cz0 + 1; cz++) {
      var list = FAC.hash[cx + ',' + cz]; if (!list) continue;
      for (var q = 0; q < list.length; q++) {
        var b = list[q], n = FAC.ringLen[b], s = FAC.ringStart[b];
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          if (!(FAC.flags[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] & 1)) continue;
          var x1 = FAC.verts[(s + i) * 2], z1 = FAC.verts[(s + i) * 2 + 1], j = (i + 1) % n, x2 = FAC.verts[(s + j) * 2], z2 = FAC.verts[(s + j) * 2 + 1];
          var dx = x2 - x1, dz = z2 - z1, L = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (L < 2.5) continue;
          var tx = dx / L, tz = dz / L, nx = dz / L, nz = -dx / L;
          var u = (spot.x - x1) * tx + (spot.z - z1) * tz; if (u < -0.5 || u > L + 0.5) continue;
          var off = Math.abs((spot.x - x1) * nx + (spot.z - z1) * nz); if (off > 1.6) continue;
          var face = -(spot.dx * nx + spot.dz * nz); if (face < 0.5) continue;   /* the approach runs into the wall */
          /* the longest wall the door can stand on wins; a near-collinear neighbour of a short one is cut by the same hole */
          var score = Math.min(L, 9) * face - off * 2;
          if (!best || score > best.score) best = { b: b, i: i, u: u, off: off, L: L, x1: x1, z1: z1, tx: tx, tz: tz, nx: nx, nz: nz, score: score };
        }
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  function crystalFrame(Wd, Hd, band, depth, seed) {
    var pos = [], r = seed;
    function rnd() { r = (r * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return r / 0x7fffffff; }
    function cell(x0, y0, x1, y1, spill) {
      var cx = (x0 + x1) / 2 + (rnd() - 0.5) * (x1 - x0) * 0.6, cy = (y0 + y1) / 2 + (rnd() - 0.5) * (y1 - y0) * 0.6;
      var zc = depth * (0.3 + 0.7 * rnd()), z0 = 0.05;
      var c = [[x0, y0], [x1, y0], [x1, y1], [x0, y1]];
      for (var k = 0; k < 4; k++) { var a = c[k], b = c[(k + 1) % 4]; pos.push(a[0], a[1], z0, b[0], b[1], z0, cx, cy, zc); }
      if (spill && rnd() < 0.45) {
        /* a shard spilling past the band onto the wall beside it */
        var sx = spill[0], sy = spill[1], ex = cx + sx * (0.3 + 0.5 * rnd()), ey = cy + sy * (0.3 + 0.5 * rnd());
        var a2 = c[spill[2]], b2 = c[spill[3]];
        pos.push(a2[0], a2[1], z0, b2[0], b2[1], z0, ex, ey, depth * 0.25 * rnd());
      }
    }
    var ch = 1.25, cw = 1.3, x;
    for (var y = -0.3; y < Hd + band; y += ch) {
      var y2 = Math.min(y + ch, Hd + band);
      cell(-Wd / 2 - band, y, -Wd / 2, y2, [-1, 0, 0, 3]);
      cell(Wd / 2, y, Wd / 2 + band, y2, [1, 0, 2, 1]);
    }
    for (x = -Wd / 2; x < Wd / 2; x += cw) {
      var x2 = Math.min(x + cw, Wd / 2);
      cell(x, Hd, x2, Hd + band, [0, 1, 3, 2]);
    }
    var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    g.computeVertexNormals();
    return g;
  }

  function lobbyTexture() {
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 64; c.height = 128;
    var x = c.getContext('2d');
    var gr = x.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 128);
    gr.addColorStop(0, '#F3ECE6'); gr.addColorStop(0.55, '#E8D3A6'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#6E5F4A');
    x.fillStyle = gr; x.fillRect(0, 0, 64, 128);
    var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding; return t;
  }

  function buildPortalV2(spot) {
    var shop = spot.shop;
    var gy = groundAt(spot.x, spot.z);
    var grp = new THREE.Group();
    var host = spot.snapped ? hostWall(spot) : null;
    var Wd = 7.2, Hd = 6.4, band = 1.15, depth = 0.85;
    if (host) {
      var F = FAC.floors[host.b], y1 = FAC.top[host.b], yb = y1 - 3 * F;
      Wd = Math.max(3.0, Math.min(Wd, host.L - 2 * band - 0.8));
      Hd = Math.max(3.4, Math.min(Hd, (y1 - gy) - 1.3));
      var uc = (host.L > Wd + 2 * band + 0.8) ? Math.min(host.L - Wd / 2 - band - 0.4, Math.max(Wd / 2 + band + 0.4, host.u)) : host.L / 2;
      FAC.portals[host.b + ':' + host.i] = { u0: uc - Wd / 2 - band * 0.6, u1: uc + Wd / 2 + band * 0.6, H: Hd + band + 0.2 };
      HOLES.push([host.x1 + host.tx * uc, host.z1 + host.tz * uc, host.nx, host.nz, Wd / 2, Hd, gy]);
      /* stand exactly on the wall plane, at the frame's centre */
      grp.position.set(host.x1 + host.tx * uc, gy, host.z1 + host.tz * uc);
      grp.rotation.y = Math.atan2(host.nx, host.nz);
      spot.x = grp.position.x + host.nx * 0.3; spot.z = grp.position.z + host.nz * 0.3;
    } else {
      grp.position.set(spot.x, gy, spot.z);
      grp.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-spot.dx, -spot.dz);
    }
    var pale = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(FACX.WALL || '#E3DDE0').convertSRGBToLinear(), roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0, flatShading: true, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    var frame = new THREE.Mesh(crystalFrame(Wd, Hd, band, depth, 97 + Math.round(spot.x)), pale);
    frame.castShadow = true; frame.receiveShadow = true; grp.add(frame);
    /* the bay: a lit lobby behind the glass, a stair rising in it */
    var lobby = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd - 0.1, Hd - 0.05, 5.2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: lobbyTexture(), side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false }));
    lobby.position.set(0, Hd / 2 - 0.02, -2.6); grp.add(lobby);
    var step = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(0xC9C1C4).convertSRGBToLinear(), fog: false });
    for (var k = 0; k < 7; k++) {
      var s = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd * 0.42, 0.42, 0.72), step);
      s.position.set(Wd * 0.2, 0.21 + k * 0.42, -1.3 - k * 0.72); grp.add(s);
    }
    var glass = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(Wd, Hd), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: PEARL, transparent: true, opacity: 0.16, depthWrite: false, fog: false }));
    glass.position.set(0, Hd / 2, 0.03); grp.add(glass);
    var mull = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(0xF2EDEF).convertSRGBToLinear(), fog: false });
    for (var m = 1; m < 4; m++) { var v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, Hd, 0.07), mull); v.position.set(-Wd / 2 + m * Wd / 4, Hd / 2, 0.04); grp.add(v); }
    [3.0, Hd - 0.7].forEach(function (hy) { if (hy < Hd - 0.2) { var hm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd, 0.07, 0.07), mull); hm.position.set(0, hy, 0.04); grp.add(hm); } });
    var sill = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd + 0.4, 0.14, 0.7), pale); sill.position.set(0, 0.07, 0.35); sill.castShadow = true; grp.add(sill);
    /* the name over the door */
    var sign = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(Wd * 0.9, Wd * 0.9 / 4),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: wordmarkTexture(shop.label), transparent: true, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    sign.position.set(0, Hd + band + 1.1, 0.4); grp.add(sign);
    grp.userData = { shop: shop, x: spot.x, z: spot.z, gy: gy, hall: glass, sign: sign, snapped: !!spot.snapped, host: !!host, nx: Math.sin(grp.rotation.y), nz: Math.cos(grp.rotation.y) };
    return grp;
  }

  /* the light: a moon over the walker's shoulder, the sky from above, the
     shadow map riding along */
  function buildLights() {
    var shadows = FACX.SHADOWS !== false;
    moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight(new THREE.Color(FACX.MOON || '#C9D6EC').convertSRGBToLinear(), FACX.MOON_INTENSITY != null ? FACX.MOON_INTENSITY : 1.35);
    moon.castShadow = shadows;
    if (shadows) {
      moon.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048);
      var sc = moon.shadow.camera;
      sc.left = -220; sc.right = 220; sc.top = 220; sc.bottom = -220; sc.near = 5; sc.far = 1400;
      moon.shadow.bias = -0.0006; moon.shadow.normalBias = 0.3; moon.shadow.radius = 1.5;
    }
    hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(new THREE.Color(FACX.SKY || '#6B7A96').convertSRGBToLinear(), new THREE.Color('#2A2F38').convertSRGBToLinear(), FACX.SKY_INTENSITY != null ? FACX.SKY_INTENSITY : 1.0);
    return [moon, moon.target, hemi];
  }
  function moveLights(px, py, pz) {
    if (!moon) return;
    var M = FACX.MOON_FROM || [-230, 400, 230];
    moon.position.set(px + M[0], py + M[1], pz + M[2]);
    moon.target.position.set(px, py, pz);
    moon.target.updateMatrixWorld();
  }

  /* ======================= THE PLATFORM AND THE STREETS (v3) =======================
     The city is a platform floating in the marble: it ends where the city
     ends, a pale edge drops away under it, and an invisible wall at the edge
     keeps the walker on it. The sky - the Canal Valley - goes all the way
     round, below as well as above.

     The streets are vectors: every way from the same OSM export as straight
     ribbons - pavement, then carriageway over it - and the paint as real
     strips: white edge lines, the double yellow down two-way streets, dashed
     lane lines, every line stopping where it meets another road. The paint
     is laid around the walker and laid again as they move; the ribbons are
     the whole city.

     PLATFORM in CITY_CONFIG:
       ON        false: the old open ground to the horizon
       CELL      metres per cell of the outline (20)
       REACH     how far past the last building the platform runs, metres
       DEPTH     how far the edge drops under the platform, metres
     STREETS in CITY_CONFIG:
       ON        false: the streets as the ground texture, as before
       FILE      the streets file, in city/ on the CDN
       PAINT_R   metres of paint around the walker
     ================================================================= */
  var PLX = CITY_CONFIG.PLATFORM || {};
  var STX = CITY_CONFIG.STREETS || {};
  var PLATFORM = null;       /* { tex, box, at(x,z), N } */
  var STREETS = null;        /* the parsed ways */
  var PAINT = null;          /* the paint mesh around the walker */
  var maskU = { value: null }, maskBoxU = { value: new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 0, 0) }, maskOnU = { value: 0 };

  var MASK_GLSL = [
    'uniform sampler2D uMask; uniform vec4 uMaskBox; uniform float uMaskOn;',
    'float onPlatform(vec2 p){',
    '  if (uMaskOn < 0.5) return 1.0;',
    '  vec2 m = (p - uMaskBox.xy) / uMaskBox.zw;',
    '  if (m.x < 0.0 || m.y < 0.0 || m.x > 1.0 || m.y > 1.0) return 0.0;',
    '  return texture2D(uMask, m).r;',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');
  function maskUniforms() { return { uMask: maskU, uMaskBox: maskBoxU, uMaskOn: maskOnU }; }

  /* ---- the outline: every building's footprint, grown, holes filled, the
     piece the walker stands in kept, softened so the edge is a curve ---- */
  function buildPlatform(spawn) {
    if (!FAC || PLX.ON === false) return null;
    var a = MANI.atlas, C = PLX.CELL || 20;
    var N = Math.ceil(Math.max(a.w, a.h) / C), x0 = a.x0, z0 = a.z0;
    var g = new Uint8Array(N * N);
    for (var b = 0; b < FAC.n; b++) {
      var i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((FAC.minx[b] - x0) / C)), i1 = Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor((FAC.maxx[b] - x0) / C));
      var j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((FAC.minz[b] - z0) / C)), j1 = Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor((FAC.maxz[b] - z0) / C));
      for (var j = j0; j <= j1; j++) for (var i = i0; i <= i1; i++) g[j * N + i] = 1;
    }
    function boxMax(src, r) {      /* square dilation, separable */
      var tmp = new Uint8Array(N * N), out = new Uint8Array(N * N);
      for (var j = 0; j < N; j++) for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        var v = 0; for (var k = Math.max(0, i - r); k <= Math.min(N - 1, i + r) && !v; k++) v = src[j * N + k]; tmp[j * N + i] = v;
      }
      for (var j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2++) for (var i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2++) {
