        var w = 0; for (var k2 = Math.max(0, j2 - r); k2 <= Math.min(N - 1, j2 + r) && !w; k2++) w = tmp[k2 * N + i2]; out[j2 * N + i2] = w;
      }
      return out;
    }
    function boxMin(src, r) { var inv = new Uint8Array(N * N); for (var q = 0; q < N * N; q++) inv[q] = src[q] ? 0 : 1; var d = boxMax(inv, r); for (var q2 = 0; q2 < N * N; q2++) d[q2] = d[q2] ? 0 : 1; return d; }
    function flood(src, seeds, want) {     /* cells reachable from seeds through cells equal to want */
      var seen = new Uint8Array(N * N), st = [];
      seeds.forEach(function (s) { if (src[s] === want && !seen[s]) { seen[s] = 1; st.push(s); } });
      while (st.length) {
        var c = st.pop(), ci = c % N, cj = (c / N) | 0;
        if (ci > 0 && !seen[c - 1] && src[c - 1] === want) { seen[c - 1] = 1; st.push(c - 1); }
        if (ci < N - 1 && !seen[c + 1] && src[c + 1] === want) { seen[c + 1] = 1; st.push(c + 1); }
        if (cj > 0 && !seen[c - N] && src[c - N] === want) { seen[c - N] = 1; st.push(c - N); }
        if (cj < N - 1 && !seen[c + N] && src[c + N] === want) { seen[c + N] = 1; st.push(c + N); }
      }
      return seen;
    }
    var R = Math.max(1, Math.round((PLX.REACH || 60) / C));
    var grown = boxMin(boxMax(g, R + 1), 1);
    /* holes: whatever the outside cannot reach is inside */
    var border = []; for (var e = 0; e < N; e++) { border.push(e, (N - 1) * N + e, e * N, e * N + N - 1); }
    var outside = flood(grown, border, 0);
    for (var q3 = 0; q3 < N * N; q3++) grown[q3] = outside[q3] ? 0 : 1;
    /* the piece the walker stands in */
    var si = Math.floor((spawn.x - x0) / C), sj = Math.floor((spawn.z - z0) / C);
    var keep = flood(grown, [Math.max(0, Math.min(N * N - 1, sj * N + si))], 1);
    /* softened: two box blurs, so the 0.5 line is a smooth curve */
    var f = new Float32Array(N * N); for (var q4 = 0; q4 < N * N; q4++) f[q4] = keep[q4];
    function blur(src) {
      var t = new Float32Array(N * N), o = new Float32Array(N * N);
      for (var j = 0; j < N; j++) for (var i = 0; i < N; i++) { var s = 0, n = 0; for (var k = -1; k <= 1; k++) { var ii = i + k; if (ii >= 0 && ii < N) { s += src[j * N + ii]; n++; } } t[j * N + i] = s / n; }
      for (var j2 = 0; j2 < N; j2++) for (var i2 = 0; i2 < N; i2++) { var s2 = 0, n2 = 0; for (var k2 = -1; k2 <= 1; k2++) { var jj = j2 + k2; if (jj >= 0 && jj < N) { s2 += t[jj * N + i2]; n2++; } } o[j2 * N + i2] = s2 / n2; }
      return o;
    }
    f = blur(blur(f));
    var d8 = new Uint8Array(N * N); for (var q5 = 0; q5 < N * N; q5++) d8[q5] = Math.round(f[q5] * 255);
    var tex = new THREE.DataTexture(d8, N, N, THREE.RedFormat, THREE.UnsignedByteType);
    tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; tex.generateMipmaps = false; tex.needsUpdate = true;
    /* texel centres: the box is grown half a cell each way so the GPU's sample matches at() */
    var bx = x0 - C / 2, bz = z0 - C / 2, bw = N * C, bh = N * C;
    maskU.value = tex; maskBoxU.value.set(x0, z0, N * C, N * C); maskOnU.value = 1;
    function at(x, z) {
      var fx = (x - x0) / C - 0.5, fz = (z - z0) / C - 0.5;
      if (fx < 0 || fz < 0 || fx > N - 1 || fz > N - 1) return 0;
      var i = Math.floor(fx), j = Math.floor(fz), tx = fx - i, tz = fz - j, i1 = Math.min(N - 1, i + 1), j1 = Math.min(N - 1, j + 1);
      return (f[j * N + i] * (1 - tx) + f[j * N + i1] * tx) * (1 - tz) + (f[j1 * N + i] * (1 - tx) + f[j1 * N + i1] * tx) * tz;
    }
    var area = 0; for (var q6 = 0; q6 < N * N; q6++) if (f[q6] >= 0.5) area++;
    return { tex: tex, N: N, C: C, x0: x0, z0: z0, f: f, at: at, areaKm2: area * C * C / 1e6, unused: [bx, bz, bw, bh] };
  }

  /* ---- the edge: marching squares on the softened outline, a pale wall
     dropping away under it and a thin champagne line along its lip ---- */
  function buildEdge() {
    var P = PLATFORM, N = P.N, C = P.C, f = P.f, segs = [];
    function v(i, j) { return f[j * N + i]; }
    function pt(i, j, k) {   /* point on the cell edge k of cell (i,j) where f crosses 0.5; cell corners at texel centres */
      var ax, az, bx2, bz2, fa, fb;
      if (k === 0) { ax = i; az = j; bx2 = i + 1; bz2 = j; }
      else if (k === 1) { ax = i + 1; az = j; bx2 = i + 1; bz2 = j + 1; }
      else if (k === 2) { ax = i; az = j + 1; bx2 = i + 1; bz2 = j + 1; }
      else { ax = i; az = j; bx2 = i; bz2 = j + 1; }
      fa = v(ax, az); fb = v(bx2, bz2);
      var t = (0.5 - fa) / ((fb - fa) || 1e-6);
      return [P.x0 + (ax + (bx2 - ax) * t + 0.5) * C, P.z0 + (az + (bz2 - az) * t + 0.5) * C];
    }
    var TABLE = { 1: [[3, 0]], 2: [[0, 1]], 3: [[3, 1]], 4: [[1, 2]], 5: [[3, 0], [1, 2]], 6: [[0, 2]], 7: [[3, 2]],
                  8: [[2, 3]], 9: [[2, 0]], 10: [[0, 1], [2, 3]], 11: [[2, 1]], 12: [[1, 3]], 13: [[1, 0]], 14: [[0, 3]] };
    for (var j = 0; j < N - 1; j++) for (var i = 0; i < N - 1; i++) {
      var c = (v(i, j) >= 0.5 ? 1 : 0) | (v(i + 1, j) >= 0.5 ? 2 : 0) | (v(i + 1, j + 1) >= 0.5 ? 4 : 0) | (v(i, j + 1) >= 0.5 ? 8 : 0);
      var L = TABLE[c]; if (!L) continue;
      for (var s = 0; s < L.length; s++) segs.push(pt(i, j, L[s][0]), pt(i, j, L[s][1]));
    }
    var DEPTH = PLX.DEPTH || 60;
    var pos = [], tt = [];
    for (var k = 0; k < segs.length; k += 2) {
      var A = segs[k], B = segs[k + 1];
      /* top at the ground (the shader drapes it), bottom DEPTH under */
      pos.push(A[0], 0, A[1], B[0], 0, B[1], B[0], 0, B[1], A[0], 0, A[1], B[0], 0, B[1], A[0], 0, A[1]);
      tt.push(0, 0, 1, 0, 1, 1);
    }
    var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    g.setAttribute('aT', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(tt, 1));
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({ uDepth: { value: DEPTH }, uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(INK) }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG },
        uLip: { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMPAGNE) }, uStone: { value: new THREE.Color(FACX.WALL || '#E3DDE0') } }, atlasUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute float aT; varying float vT; varying vec3 vW; uniform float uDepth;',
        ATLAS_GLSL,
        'void main(){ vT = aT; vec4 w = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); w.y = gAt(w.xz) + 0.04 - aT * uDepth; vW = w.xyz; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w; }'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying float vT; varying vec3 vW; uniform vec3 uInk, uLip, uStone; uniform float uFog, uDepth;',
        'void main(){',
        /* pale stone at the lip fading into the marble below, the lip itself a line of light */
        '  float m = vT * uDepth;',
        '  vec3 col = mix(uStone * 0.55, uInk, smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, vT));',
        '  col = mix(col, uLip, smoothstep(0.35, 0.0, m) * 0.9);',
        '  float a = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.55, 1.0, vT);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.6), a);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      transparent: true, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, m); mesh.frustumCulled = false; mesh.renderOrder = 4;
    return mesh;
  }

  /* ---- the streets file ---- */
  function loadStreets(url) {
    return fetch(url).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); return r.arrayBuffer(); }).then(function (buf) {
      var dv = new DataView(buf);
      if (String.fromCharCode(dv.getUint8(0), dv.getUint8(1), dv.getUint8(2), dv.getUint8(3)) !== 'RDS1') throw new Error('not a streets file');
      var n = dv.getUint32(4, true), q = dv.getFloat32(8, true), off = 12, ways = [];
      for (var w = 0; w < n; w++) {
        var A = dv.getUint8(off) / 4, SW = dv.getUint8(off + 1) / 4, lanes = dv.getInt8(off + 2), np = dv.getUint16(off + 3, true); off += 5;
        var x = dv.getInt32(off, true), z = dv.getInt32(off + 4, true); off += 8;
        var pts = new Float32Array(np * 2); pts[0] = x * q; pts[1] = z * q;
        for (var i = 1; i < np; i++) { x += dv.getInt16(off, true); z += dv.getInt16(off + 2, true); off += 4; pts[i * 2] = x * q; pts[i * 2 + 1] = z * q; }
        var gaps = [];
        for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) { var m = dv.getUint8(off); off += 1; var gl = []; for (var j = 0; j < m; j++) { gl.push([dv.getUint16(off, true) / 2, dv.getUint16(off + 2, true) / 2]); off += 4; } gaps.push(gl); }
        var mnx = 1e9, mxx = -1e9, mnz = 1e9, mxz = -1e9;
        for (var p = 0; p < np; p++) { var px = pts[p * 2], pz = pts[p * 2 + 1]; if (px < mnx) mnx = px; if (px > mxx) mxx = px; if (pz < mnz) mnz = pz; if (pz > mxz) mxz = pz; }
        ways.push({ A: A, SW: SW, lanes: lanes, pts: pts, gaps: gaps, box: [mnx, mnz, mxx, mxz] });
      }
      return ways;
    });
  }

  /* a ribbon along a polyline: straight segments split every STEP so the
     drape follows the ground, mitred at the corners */
  function ribbon(pts, half, step, out) {
    var n = pts.length / 2, P = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
      var ax = pts[i * 2], az = pts[i * 2 + 1], bx = pts[i * 2 + 2], bz = pts[i * 2 + 3];
      var L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az), k = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L / step));
      for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) P.push(ax + (bx - ax) * j / k, az + (bz - az) * j / k);
    }
    P.push(pts[(n - 1) * 2], pts[(n - 1) * 2 + 1]);
    var m = P.length / 2, base = out.pos.length / 3;
    for (var p = 0; p < m; p++) {
      var pi = Math.max(0, p - 1), ni = Math.min(m - 1, p + 1);
      var t1x = P[p * 2] - P[pi * 2], t1z = P[p * 2 + 1] - P[pi * 2 + 1], t2x = P[ni * 2] - P[p * 2], t2z = P[ni * 2 + 1] - P[p * 2 + 1];
      var l1 = Math.hypot(t1x, t1z) || 1, l2 = Math.hypot(t2x, t2z) || 1;
      if (p === 0) { t1x = t2x; t1z = t2z; l1 = l2; } if (p === m - 1) { t2x = t1x; t2z = t1z; l2 = l1; }
      var n1x = -t1z / l1, n1z = t1x / l1, n2x = -t2z / l2, n2z = t2x / l2;
      var mx = n1x + n2x, mz = n1z + n2z, ml = Math.hypot(mx, mz) || 1; mx /= ml; mz /= ml;
      var c = Math.max(0.4, mx * n2x + mz * n2z), h = half / c;
      out.pos.push(P[p * 2] + mx * h, 0, P[p * 2 + 1] + mz * h, P[p * 2] - mx * h, 0, P[p * 2 + 1] - mz * h);
      if (p < m - 1) { var a0 = base + p * 2; out.idx.push(a0, a0 + 2, a0 + 1, a0 + 1, a0 + 2, a0 + 3); }
    }
  }

  function streetMaterial(kind) {
    var cols = { side: '#34363D', tar: '#121418' };
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({ uCol: { value: new THREE.Color(cols[kind] || '#ffffff') }, uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(INK) }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG },
        uLift: { value: kind === 'side' ? 0.10 : 0.12 }, uTar: { value: kind === 'tar' ? 1 : 0 },
        uLP: LIGHTU.p, uLC: LIGHTU.c, uLN: LIGHTU.n, uLT: LIGHTU.tu }, atlasUniforms(), maskUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute vec3 aCol; varying vec3 vW; varying vec3 vC; uniform float uLift;',
        ATLAS_GLSL,
        'void main(){ vC = aCol; vec4 w = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); w.y = gAt(w.xz) + uLift; vW = w.xyz; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w; }'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW; varying vec3 vC; uniform vec3 uCol, uInk; uniform float uFog, uTar, uLT;',
        MASK_GLSL,
        'float h21(vec2 p){ vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031); q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33); return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z); }',
        STREET_LIGHT_GLSL,
        'void main(){',
        '  if (onPlatform(vW.xz) < 0.5) discard;',
        '  vec3 col = (length(vC) > 0.0 ? vC : uCol) * (0.92 + 0.16 * h21(floor(vW.xz * 1.9)));',
        /* a warm lamp pool every 30 m on the carriageway */
        '  if (uTar > 0.5) { vec3 vd = normalize(vW - cameraPosition); float gr = pow(1.0 - abs(vd.y), 4.0); vec2 pq = vW.xz * 0.35; vec2 pi = floor(pq), pf = fract(pq); pf = pf * pf * (3.0 - 2.0 * pf); float pn = mix(mix(h21(pi), h21(pi + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), pf.x), mix(h21(pi + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), h21(pi + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), pf.x), pf.y); float pud = smoothstep(0.55, 0.8, pn); col *= 1.0 - 0.3 * pud; col += vec3(0.20, 0.24, 0.40) * gr * (0.05 + 0.22 * pud); }',
        '  col += streetLights(vW, uTar > 0.5 ? 0.75 : 0.3, uLT);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog, 0.0, 1.0)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      depthWrite: false, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -6
    });
  }

  function buildStreets() {
    var side = { pos: [], idx: [] }, tar = { pos: [], idx: [] };
    STREETS.forEach(function (w) {
      if (PLATFORM) {
        var cx = (w.box[0] + w.box[2]) / 2, cz = (w.box[1] + w.box[3]) / 2;
        if (PLATFORM.at(cx, cz) < 0.02 && PLATFORM.at(w.pts[0], w.pts[1]) < 0.02) return;
      }
      ribbon(w.pts, w.A / 2 + w.SW, 12, side);
      if (w.A > 0.5) ribbon(w.pts, w.A / 2, 12, tar);
    });
    function mesh(o, kind, order) {
      var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(o.pos, 3));
      g.setAttribute('aCol', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(o.pos.length), 3));
      g.setIndex(o.idx.length > 65535 ? new THREE.Uint32BufferAttribute(o.idx, 1) : new THREE.Uint16BufferAttribute(o.idx, 1));
      var m = new THREE.Mesh(g, streetMaterial(kind)); m.frustumCulled = false; m.renderOrder = order; m.receiveShadow = false;
      return m;
    }
    return [mesh(side, 'side', 1), mesh(tar, 'tar', 2)];
  }

  /* the paint: real strips, laid within PAINT_R of the walker */
  var PAINT_WHITE = [0.84, 0.83, 0.80], PAINT_YELLOW = [0.93, 0.68, 0.16];
  function paintWay(w, out) {
    var A = w.A; if (A < 0.5) return;
    var lines = [];
    var e = Math.max(0.3, A / 2 - 0.3);
    if (A >= 9) { lines.push({ o: e, wd: 0.15, c: PAINT_WHITE, g: [1] }); lines.push({ o: -e, wd: 0.15, c: PAINT_WHITE, g: [2] }); }
    if (w.lanes > 0 && A >= 5) { lines.push({ o: 0.14, wd: 0.11, c: PAINT_YELLOW, g: [0] }); lines.push({ o: -0.14, wd: 0.11, c: PAINT_YELLOW, g: [0] }); }
    var n = Math.abs(w.lanes), k;
    if (w.lanes > 0) {
      var per = Math.floor(n / 2);
      if (per >= 2) { var lw = (A / 2 - 0.3) / per; for (k = 1; k < per; k++) { lines.push({ o: k * lw, wd: 0.12, c: PAINT_WHITE, g: [0, 1, 2], dash: 1 }); lines.push({ o: -k * lw, wd: 0.12, c: PAINT_WHITE, g: [0, 1, 2], dash: 1 }); } }
    } else if (n >= 2) {
      for (k = 1; k < n; k++) lines.push({ o: -A / 2 + 0.3 + k * (A - 0.6) / n, wd: 0.12, c: PAINT_WHITE, g: [0, 1, 2], dash: 1 });
    }
    var pts = w.pts, np = pts.length / 2;
    lines.forEach(function (ln) {
      var gaps = []; ln.g.forEach(function (gi) { gaps = gaps.concat(w.gaps[gi]); });
      var s0 = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < np - 1; i++) {
        var ax = pts[i * 2], az = pts[i * 2 + 1], bx = pts[i * 2 + 2], bz = pts[i * 2 + 3];
        var L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az); if (L < 0.01) continue;
        var tx = (bx - ax) / L, tz = (bz - az) / L, nx = -tz, nz = tx;
        /* the painted spans of this segment: minus the gaps, minus the dash spaces */
        var spans = [[0, L]];
        gaps.forEach(function (g) {
          var g0 = g[0] - s0, g1 = g[1] - s0, ns = [];
          spans.forEach(function (sp) { if (g1 <= sp[0] || g0 >= sp[1]) ns.push(sp); else { if (g0 > sp[0]) ns.push([sp[0], g0]); if (g1 < sp[1]) ns.push([g1, sp[1]]); } });
          spans = ns;
        });
        if (ln.dash) {
          var ds = []; spans.forEach(function (sp) { var st = Math.ceil((s0 + sp[0]) / 9) * 9 - s0; for (var d0 = st - 9; d0 < sp[1]; d0 += 9) { var a1 = Math.max(sp[0], d0), b1 = Math.min(sp[1], d0 + 3); if (b1 > a1 + 0.2) ds.push([a1, b1]); } });
          spans = ds;
        }
        spans.forEach(function (sp) {
          var len = sp[1] - sp[0]; if (len < 0.2) return;
          var pieces = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(len / 16));
          for (var q = 0; q < pieces; q++) {
            var u0 = sp[0] + len * q / pieces, u1 = sp[0] + len * (q + 1) / pieces;
            var cx0 = ax + tx * u0 + nx * ln.o, cz0 = az + tz * u0 + nz * ln.o, cx1 = ax + tx * u1 + nx * ln.o, cz1 = az + tz * u1 + nz * ln.o, h = ln.wd / 2;
            var b0 = out.pos.length / 3;
            out.pos.push(cx0 + nx * h, 0, cz0 + nz * h, cx0 - nx * h, 0, cz0 - nz * h, cx1 + nx * h, 0, cz1 + nz * h, cx1 - nx * h, 0, cz1 - nz * h);
            for (var r = 0; r < 4; r++) out.col.push(ln.c[0], ln.c[1], ln.c[2]);
            out.idx.push(b0, b0 + 2, b0 + 1, b0 + 1, b0 + 2, b0 + 3);
          }
        });
        s0 += L;
      }
    });
  }

  function updatePaint(px, pz, force) {
    if (!STREETS) return;
    if (!force && PAINT && Math.hypot(px - PAINT.userData.x, pz - PAINT.userData.z) < 60) return;
    var R = STX.PAINT_R || 450, out = { pos: [], col: [], idx: [] };
    STREETS.forEach(function (w) {
      if (w.box[0] > px + R || w.box[2] < px - R || w.box[1] > pz + R || w.box[3] < pz - R) return;
      paintWay(w, out);
    });
    var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(out.pos, 3));
    g.setAttribute('aCol', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(out.col, 3));
    g.setIndex(new THREE.Uint32BufferAttribute(out.idx, 1));
    if (!PAINT) {
      var m = streetMaterial('paint'); m.uniforms.uLift.value = 0.14; m.polygonOffsetFactor = -3; m.polygonOffsetUnits = -10;
      PAINT = new THREE.Mesh(g, m); PAINT.frustumCulled = false; PAINT.renderOrder = 3; scene.add(PAINT);
    } else { PAINT.geometry.dispose(); PAINT.geometry = g; }
    PAINT.userData.x = px; PAINT.userData.z = pz;
  }

  /* the door's wall must be long enough for the whole frame, with the street
     clear in front of all of it - otherwise the frame meets another building */
  function doorFits(spot) {
    if (!FAC || !FACADES_ON) return true;
    var h = hostWall(spot);
    if (!h || h.L < (FACX.DOOR_WALL || 10.5)) return false;
    var band = 1.15, Wd = Math.min(7.2, h.L - 2 * band - 0.8);
    var uc = Math.min(h.L - Wd / 2 - band - 0.4, Math.max(Wd / 2 + band + 0.4, h.u));
    /* the occupancy grid rounds every footprint out by up to 2 m, so the
       check starts past that */
    for (var u = uc - Wd / 2 - band; u <= uc + Wd / 2 + band; u += 1.0) {
      for (var o = 2.6; o <= 5.6; o += 1.5) {
        var x = h.x1 + h.tx * u + h.nx * o, z = h.z1 + h.tz * u + h.nz * o;
        if (blocked(x, z)) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /* Every surface that is meant to BE the ground reads the same field.
     The atlas is street level for the whole core, baked from the road
     vertices themselves, so a ribbon that samples it cannot float above the
     plate or sink into it - they are literally the same number. */
  function atlasUniforms() {
    var a = MANI.atlas;
    return {
      uAtlas:  { value: ATLAS ? ATLAS.tex : null },
      uLo:     { value: a.lo },
      uSpan:   { value: a.hi - a.lo },
      uOrigin: { value: new THREE.Vector2(a.x0, a.z0) },
      uSize:   { value: new THREE.Vector2(a.w, a.h) }
    };
  }

  /* the sampler, as vertex-shader source - shared verbatim by the ground
     plate, the roads and the parks */
  var ATLAS_GLSL = [
    'uniform sampler2D uAtlas;',
    'uniform float uLo, uSpan;',
    'uniform vec2 uOrigin, uSize;',
    'float gAt(vec2 p){',
    /* the atlas was written north row first; a texture's v runs the other
       way (flipY), so the row order is turned over here. Without this the
       plate was the terrain mirrored north-south: fine at the spawn on the
       flat, wrong by metres a kilometre away, which is what left the roads
       and the parks out of register with it. */
    '  vec2 auv = (p - uOrigin) / uSize;',
    '  vec4 t = texture2D(uAtlas, vec2(auv.x, 1.0 - auv.y));',
    /* 16-bit, high byte in red and low byte in green */
    '  return uLo + (t.r*255.0*256.0 + t.g*255.0)/65535.0 * uSpan;',
    '}',
    /* Snap to the plate where the difference is resampling noise, keep the
       geometry's own height where it is a real structure. A flyover deck
       stays a flyover; a street 40 cm out of register stops floating. */
    'float drape(float y, vec2 p, float lift){',
    '  float g = gAt(p);',
    '  float keep = smoothstep(1.5, 3.0, abs(y - g));',
    '  return mix(g, y, keep) + lift;',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  function roadMaterial() {
    /* The road exports carry more than positions, which I had been throwing
       away: TEXCOORD_0.x runs 0 to 1 ACROSS the ribbon, TEXCOORD_0.y is the
       distance ALONG it in metres, and TEXCOORD_1.x is the road's width in
       metres. That is everything the Unreal material used to draw
       sidewalk | asphalt | sidewalk with its edge lines, dashed lane lines and
       a centre line - so the same thing can be drawn here from the same
       numbers, with no new data.

       The kerb is real, not painted: the vertex shader lifts the two outer
       bands 14 cm, so the pavement stands above the carriageway the way it
       does in the street. */
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({
        uInk:   { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
        uFog:   { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG },
        uKerb:  { value: CITY_CONFIG.KERB }
      }, atlasUniforms(), lookUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW;',
        'varying vec3 vL;',
        'varying vec2 vAcross;',   /* x = 0..1 across, y = metres along */
        'varying float vWide;',
        'uniform float uKerb;',
        'attribute vec2 uv2;',
        ATLAS_GLSL,
        'void main(){',
        '  float u = clamp(uv.x, 0.0, 1.0);',
        '  float w = max(uv2.x, 4.0);',
        '  vAcross = vec2(u, uv.y);',
        '  vWide = w;',
        '  float sw = clamp(2.2 / w, 0.05, 0.30);',      /* pavement share */
        '  float onKerb = step(u, sw) + step(1.0 - sw, u);',
        '  vec3 pos = position;',
        '  vec4 world = modelMatrix * vec4(pos, 1.0);',
        /* sit the carriageway on the plate, then stand the pavement on top */
        '  world.y = drape(world.y, world.xz, onKerb * uKerb + 0.015);',
        '  vW = world.xyz; vL = pos;',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * world;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW; varying vec3 vL; varying vec2 vAcross; varying float vWide;',
        'uniform vec3 uInk; uniform float uFog; uniform float uKerb;',
        LOOK_GLSL,
        'float h21(vec2 p){',
        '  vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);',
        '  q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33);',
        '  return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z);',
        '}',
        'void main(){',
        '  float u = vAcross.x;',
        '  float sAlong = vAcross.y;',
        '  float w = vWide;',
        '  float sw = clamp(2.2 / w, 0.05, 0.30);',
        '  float edge = min(u, 1.0 - u);',

        /* pavement, then carriageway */
        '  vec3 PAVE  = vec3(0.115, 0.122, 0.140);',
        '  vec3 TAR   = vec3(0.052, 0.058, 0.070);',
        '  float isPave = step(edge, sw);',
        '  vec3 col = mix(TAR, PAVE, isPave);',

        /* a little grain so neither reads as flat plastic */
        '  col *= 0.88 + 0.24 * h21(floor(vL.xz * 1.7));',

        /* white edge line just inside the kerb */
        '  float lineW = 0.16 / w;',
        '  float edgeLine = smoothstep(lineW, 0.0, abs(edge - sw - lineW*1.4));',

        /* dashed lane lines: 3 m of paint, 5 m of gap, straight off the
           along-road distance the exporter already wrote */
        '  float lanes = max(1.0, floor((w - 4.4) / 3.4));',
        '  float lanePos = fract((u - sw) / max(0.001, 1.0 - 2.0*sw) * lanes);',
        '  float laneEdge = min(lanePos, 1.0 - lanePos);',
        '  float dash = step(fract(sAlong / 8.0), 0.375);',
        '  float laneLine = smoothstep(lineW*0.9, 0.0, laneEdge) * dash * step(2.0, lanes);',

        /* a solid centre line on anything with room for two directions */
        '  float centre = smoothstep(lineW*1.1, 0.0, abs(u - 0.5)) * step(9.0, w);',

        '  vec3 paint  = uPost > 0.5 ? uPaper  : vec3(0.78,0.80,0.84);',
        '  vec3 middle = uPost > 0.5 ? uAccent : vec3(0.86,0.72,0.34);',
        '  col = mix(col, paint,  clamp(edgeLine + laneLine, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.85);',
        '  col = mix(col, middle, centre * 0.80);',

        /* sodium lamps along the kerb every 30 m, as the film had them */
        '  vec2 lamp = floor(vL.xz / 30.0);',
        '  vec2 f = abs(fract(vL.xz / 30.0) - 0.5);',
        '  float pool = smoothstep(0.5, 0.0, length(f)) * step(0.62, h21(lamp));',
        '  col += vec3(0.95,0.78,0.48) * pool * 0.42;',

        '  col = poster(col);',
        '  col = dots(col);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog,0.0,1.0)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    return m;
  }

  function greenMaterial() {
    /* The OSM green: parks, the verges along the avenues, the river edges.
       At night these are the darkest things on the ground, so they are drawn
       as a deep, slightly uneven green that reads as planting against the
       asphalt without pretending to be lit. */
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({
        uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
        uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG }
      }, atlasUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW; varying vec3 vL;',
        ATLAS_GLSL,
        'void main(){ vec4 w = modelMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);',
        ' w.y = drape(w.y, w.xz, 0.10);',
        ' vW=w.xyz; vL=position;',
        ' gl_Position = projectionMatrix*viewMatrix*w; }'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW; varying vec3 vL;',
        'uniform vec3 uInk; uniform float uFog;',
        'float h21(vec2 p){',
        '  vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);',
        '  q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33);',
        '  return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z);',
        '}',
        'void main(){',
        /* canopy mottle, so a park is not one flat slab */
        '  float n = h21(floor(vL.xz/7.0))*0.5 + h21(floor(vL.xz/23.0))*0.5;',
        '  vec3 col = mix(vec3(0.035,0.072,0.046), vec3(0.075,0.125,0.080), n);',
        '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
        '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col, uInk, clamp(fog,0.0,1.0)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
  }

  /* ---------------- the sketch, as a texture ----------------
     hydra renders into its own offscreen canvas; three.js reads that canvas
     as the sky texture. hydra is driven by OUR frame loop (autoLoop off, we
     call tick) so the sketch is always rendered before the texture is read,
     whatever order the two libraries happened to load in. The chain below
     is the footer's buildHydraBg, line for line. */
  var CANAL = null;

  function loadHydra(url) {
    return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
      if (window.Hydra) { res(window.Hydra); return; }
      var existing = document.getElementById('lala-hydra-lib');
      if (existing) {
        existing.addEventListener('load', function () { window.Hydra ? res(window.Hydra) : rej(new Error('no Hydra global')); });
        existing.addEventListener('error', function () { rej(new Error('hydra did not load')); });
        setTimeout(function () { window.Hydra ? res(window.Hydra) : rej(new Error('hydra timed out')); }, 15000);
        return;
      }
      var sc = document.createElement('script');
      sc.id = 'lala-hydra-lib'; sc.src = url; sc.async = true;
      var done = false;
      sc.onload = function () { done = true; window.Hydra ? res(window.Hydra) : rej(new Error('no Hydra global')); };
      sc.onerror = function () { done = true; rej(new Error('hydra did not load')); };
      setTimeout(function () { if (!done) rej(new Error('hydra timed out')); }, 15000);
      document.head.appendChild(sc);
    });
  }

  function startCanal() {
    var C = CITY_CONFIG.CANAL;
    if (!C || !C.URL || reduceMotion) return Promise.resolve(null);
    return loadHydra(C.URL).then(function (Hydra) {
      var cv = document.createElement('canvas');
      cv.width = SMALL ? C.W / 2 : C.W;
      cv.height = SMALL ? C.H / 2 : C.H;
      var h = new Hydra({ canvas: cv, detectAudio: false, makeGlobal: false,
                          autoLoop: false, enableStreamCapture: false });
      var y = h.synth;
      var PEARL_F = [0.871, 0.839, 0.847], CHAMP_F = [0.910, 0.827, 0.651],
          CHAMB_F = [0.620, 0.706, 0.827], SHELL_F = [0.929, 0.902, 0.910],
          INK_F = [0.031, 0.035, 0.047];

      /* ---- the footer's chain ---- */
      function bands() {
        return y.osc(C.BANDS, 0.035, 0.9).rotate(1.05)
                .modulate(y.osc(2.2, 0.02, 0).rotate(-0.4), 0.22);
      }
      function valleyFromScan() {
        return y.src(y.s0).scale(C.ZOOM).contrast(1.15).posterize(C.TERRAC, 0.55)
                .modulateScale(y.osc(0.6, 0.02, 0), 0.03);
      }
      function valleyProcedural() {
        return y.noise(1.8, 0.05).add(y.noise(3.6, 0.08), 0.35)
                .posterize(C.TERRAC, 0.55).contrast(1.15);
      }
      /* the music, when the footer's ear is on (the note on the player):
         bass and kicks in the flow, the smear and the crawl - the noise and
         the lattice - mids and highs in the three colours. Ear off, every
         value is what it always was. */
      var NIL = { active: false, level: 0, bass: 0, mid: 0, high: 0, kick: 0 };
      function A() { var a = window.LALA_AUDIO; return (a && a.active) ? a : NIL; }
      function run(valley, label) {
        bands()
          .modulate(valley(), function () { return C.FLOW + 0.035 * Math.sin(y.time * 0.19) + A().bass * 0.12; })
          .modulateScale(valley().rotate(function () { return y.time * C.CRAWL + A().kick * 0.05; }), function () { return 0.05 + A().bass * 0.04; })
          .modulate(y.src(y.o0).scale(1.006), function () { return C.SMEAR + A().kick * 0.04; })
          .blend(y.src(y.o0), 0.42)
          .mult(y.solid(function () { return PEARL_F[0] * (1 + 0.22 * A().high); }, function () { return PEARL_F[1] * (1 + 0.10 * A().mid); }, function () { return PEARL_F[2] * (1 + 0.30 * A().high); }))
          .add(bands().rotate(1.05).modulate(valley(), C.FLOW).luma(0.55, 0.35)
                .mult(y.solid(function () { return CHAMP_F[0] * (1 + 0.35 * A().mid); }, function () { return CHAMP_F[1] * (1 + 0.25 * A().mid); }, function () { return CHAMP_F[2] * (1 + 0.10 * A().high); })), function () { return 0.42 + A().mid * 0.25; })
          .add(valley().invert().luma(0.60, 0.40)
                .mult(y.solid(function () { return CHAMB_F[0] * (1 + 0.10 * A().high); }, function () { return CHAMB_F[1] * (1 + 0.20 * A().high); }, function () { return CHAMB_F[2] * (1 + 0.45 * A().high); })), function () { return 0.30 + A().high * 0.25; })
          .add(y.gradient(0).rotate(1.5708).luma(0.35, 0.5)
                .mult(y.solid(SHELL_F[0], SHELL_F[1], SHELL_F[2])), 0.16)
          .add(y.solid(INK_F[0], INK_F[1], INK_F[2]), 0.55)
          .contrast(function () { return 1.12 + A().level * 0.15; })
          .out(y.o0);
        y.render(y.o0);
        /* the sketch feeds back on itself and takes a few seconds to settle
           into the valley; run those seconds now so the sky arrives settled
           rather than showing the raw scan while it gets there */
        for (var w = 0; w < (SMALL ? 90 : 240); w++) { try { h.tick(33.3); } catch (e) { break; } }
        if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] sky: canal valley running (' + label + ')');
      }

      var tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);
      tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping; tex.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
      tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
      tex.generateMipmaps = false;
      CANAL = { tex: tex, hydra: h, acc: 0, every: 1000 / (C.FPS || 30),
                tick: function (dtMs) {
                  this.acc += dtMs;
                  if (this.acc < this.every) return false;
                  try { h.tick(this.acc); } catch (e) {}
                  this.acc = 0;
                  tex.needsUpdate = true;
                  return true;
                } };

      /* the scan first; the procedural valley if it is missing or CORS-blocked */
      return new Promise(function (res) {
        var settled = false;
        function once(fn, label) { if (settled) return; settled = true;
          try { run(fn, label); res(CANAL); } catch (err) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] sky pipeline failed:', err); res(null); } }
        if (C.SCAN) {
          var img = new Image();
          img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
          img.onload = function () {
            try { y.s0.init({ src: img, dynamic: false }); } catch (err) { return once(valleyProcedural, 'procedural'); }
            once(valleyFromScan, 'scan');
          };
          img.onerror = function () { once(valleyProcedural, 'procedural'); };
          img.src = C.SCAN;
          setTimeout(function () { once(valleyProcedural, 'procedural'); }, 6000);
        } else {
          once(valleyProcedural, 'procedural');
        }
      });
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] sky stays plain:', e && e.message);
      return null;
    });
  }

  function buildSky() {
    var g = new THREE.SphereGeometry(CITY_CONFIG.FAR * 0.96, 64, 40);
    var C = CITY_CONFIG.CANAL || {};
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: {
        uT:     { value: 0 },
        uCanal: { value: null },
        uMode:  { value: 0 },                 /* 0 ink + stars, 1 the sketch */
        uGain:  { value: C.GAIN != null ? C.GAIN : 0.6 },
        uTile:  { value: C.TILE || 1 },
        uPost:  { value: (CITY_CONFIG.LOOK || {}).POSTER || 0 },
        uMarble: { value: 0 },
        uInk:   { value: new THREE.Color(INK) }
      },
      vertexShader: [
        'varying vec3 vD;',
        'void main(){ vD = position; gl_Position = projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0); }'
      ].join('\n'),
      /* The sketch is a flat frame; a dome is not. It is draped with the
         frame's poles on the horizon - due east and due west, where the city
         stands in front of them - so straight up is clean, and its two ends
         are cross-faded where they meet so there is no seam. */
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vD;',
        'uniform float uT, uMode, uGain, uTile, uPost, uMarble;',
        'uniform sampler2D uCanal;',
        'uniform vec3 uInk;',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec3 d = normalize(vD);',
        '  float el = d.y;',
        '  float a = atan(d.z, d.x), e = asin(clamp(el, -1.0, 1.0));',
        '  vec3 col = uInk * 0.9;',
        '  if (uMode > 0.5) {',
        '    float cu = atan(d.y, d.z) / 6.2831853 + 0.5;',
        '    float cv = asin(clamp(d.x, -1.0, 1.0)) / 3.1415927 + 0.5;',
        '    float u0 = fract(cu * uTile);',
        '    float w0 = smoothstep(0.0, 0.17, min(u0, 1.0 - u0));',
        '    float vy = clamp(cv, 0.003, 0.997);',
        '    vec3 A = texture2D(uCanal, vec2(u0, vy)).rgb;',
        '    vec3 B = texture2D(uCanal, vec2(fract(u0 + 0.5), vy)).rgb;',
        '    vec3 sk = mix(B, A, w0);',
        '    if (uPost > 0.5) sk = floor(sk * uPost + 0.5) / uPost;',
        '    col = mix(uInk, sk, 0.9) * uGain;',
        '  } else {',
        /* a few stars, until the sketch is up */
        '    vec2 sp = floor(vec2(a*140.0, e*140.0));',
        '    vec3 q = fract(vec3(sp.xyx)*0.1031); q += dot(q, q.yzx+33.33);',
        '    col += vec3(step(0.996, fract((q.x+q.y)*q.z)) * smoothstep(0.05,0.3,el)) * 0.55;',
        '  }',
        /* keep the ground half dark so the skyline reads against it */
        '  float sky = smoothstep(-0.10, 0.12, el);',
        '  col = mix(uInk*0.55, col, max(sky, uMarble));',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(max(col, 0.0), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      side: THREE.BackSide, depthWrite: false, fog: false
    });
    return new THREE.Mesh(g, m);
  }

  function wordmarkTexture(text) {
    var c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = 2048; c.height = 512;
    var x = c.getContext('2d');
    x.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    x.font = '300 300px LalaTitle, "Bebas Neue", sans-serif';
    x.textAlign = 'center'; x.textBaseline = 'middle';
    x.shadowColor = 'rgba(158,180,211,0.9)'; x.shadowBlur = 54;
    x.fillStyle = 'rgba(237,230,232,0.92)';
    x.fillText(text, c.width / 2, c.height / 2);
    x.shadowBlur = 0;
    x.lineWidth = 2;
    x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(232,211,166,0.75)';
    x.strokeText(text, c.width / 2, c.height / 2);
    var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
    t.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    if (FACADES_ON) t.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding;
    return t;
  }

  function hoodTexture() {
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 1024; c.height = 776;
    var x = c.getContext('2d');
    x.clearRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
    x.font = '300 190px LalaTitle, "Bebas Neue", sans-serif';
    x.textAlign = 'center'; x.textBaseline = 'middle';
    var tw = x.measureText('LALAGUNA').width, sc = Math.min(1, 900 / tw);
    x.font = '300 ' + Math.round(190 * sc) + 'px LalaTitle, "Bebas Neue", sans-serif';
    x.shadowColor = 'rgba(232,211,166,0.9)'; x.shadowBlur = 30;
    x.fillStyle = 'rgba(240,234,236,1)'; x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(240,234,236,1)'; x.lineWidth = 9; x.lineJoin = 'round';
    x.strokeText('LALAGUNA', c.width / 2, c.height / 2);
    x.fillText('LALAGUNA', c.width / 2, c.height / 2);
    x.shadowBlur = 0; x.lineWidth = 3; x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(232,211,166,0.8)';
    x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(c.width * 0.22, c.height / 2 + 120 * sc); x.lineTo(c.width * 0.78, c.height / 2 + 120 * sc); x.stroke();
    var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.anisotropy = 4;
    if (FACADES_ON) t.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding;
    return t;
  }

  function buildLogo(spawn) {
    var L = CITY_CONFIG.LOGO;
    var yawRad = (MANI.spawn.yawDeg || 0) * Math.PI / 180;
    var dx = Math.cos(yawRad), dz = Math.sin(yawRad);
    var g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(L.width, L.width / 4);
    var m = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      map: wordmarkTexture(L.text), transparent: true, depthWrite: false,
      blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, m);
    mesh.position.set(spawn.x + dx * L.dist, groundAt(spawn.x, spawn.z) + L.height, spawn.z + dz * L.dist);
    mesh.lookAt(spawn.x, mesh.position.y * 0.82, spawn.z);
    mesh.renderOrder = 2;
    return mesh;
  }

  /* Where the three doors go.

     They used to stand in the street at bearings picked by hand, which in a
     grid this dense meant they could end up behind a block with no way to
     see them. Instead each one is walked out from the spawn until the
     occupancy grid says a building starts: that cell is a real frontage, the
     door goes on it facing back the way you came, and the street in front is
     by definition walkable because that is how we arrived. */
  function findFrontage(spawn, bearingDeg, minD, maxD) {
    var r = bearingDeg * Math.PI / 180;
    var dx = Math.cos(r), dz = Math.sin(r);
    var step = 1.5, lastFree = null;
    for (var dist = 4; dist < maxD; dist += step) {
      var x = spawn.x + dx * dist, z = spawn.z + dz * dist;
      if (blocked(x, z)) {
        if (dist >= minD && lastFree) {
          /* Walk the last 1.5 m back down in halves to find where the facade
             actually starts. The door then stands against the wall instead of
             floating a cell and a half out in the road, which is what made
             them read as loose furniture. */
          var a = dist - step, b = dist;
          for (var it = 0; it < 7; it++) {
            var mid = (a + b) / 2;
            if (blocked(spawn.x + dx * mid, spawn.z + dz * mid)) b = mid; else a = mid;
          }
          var FACE = 0.45;                 /* frame depth, so it reads as set in */
          return { x: spawn.x + dx * (a - FACE), z: spawn.z + dz * (a - FACE),
                   dx: dx, dz: dz, dist: a };
        }
        while (dist < maxD && blocked(spawn.x + dx * dist, spawn.z + dz * dist)) dist += step;
        lastFree = null;
        continue;
      }
      lastFree = { x: x, z: z };
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* Pick one frontage per shop, as far apart around the compass as the
     streets allow, so you are never looking at two of them at once. */
  function placeShops(spawn) {
    var cands = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < 360; b += 10) {
      var f = findFrontage(spawn, b, 22, 150);
      if (f) { f.bearing = b; cands.push(f); }
    }
    if (!cands.length) return [];
    var picked = [];
    CITY_CONFIG.SHOPS.forEach(function (shop, n) {
      var want = (n * 360 / CITY_CONFIG.SHOPS.length);
      var scored = [];
      cands.forEach(function (c) {
        if (picked.indexOf(c) > -1) return;
        var da = Math.abs(((c.bearing - want + 540) % 360) - 180);   /* 0 = dead on target */
        var sep = 1e9;
        picked.forEach(function (p) {
          var s2 = Math.abs(((c.bearing - p.bearing + 540) % 360) - 180);
          sep = Math.min(sep, 180 - s2);
        });
        var score = -da * 1.0 + Math.min(sep, 90) * 1.2 - Math.abs(c.dist - 45) * 1.1;
        scored.push({ c: c, score: score });
      });
      scored.sort(function (a, b) { return b.score - a.score; });
      /* best first, but only a spot where the door actually lands on a wall
         with the street in front of it; a frontage the grid liked that the
         geometry does not back up goes to the next one */
      var best = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < scored.length && !best; i++) {
        if (snapToWall(scored[i].c) && doorFits(scored[i].c)) best = scored[i].c;
      }
      if (!best && scored.length) best = scored[0].c;
      if (best) {
        best.shop = shop; picked.push(best);
        clearDoorstep(best.x, best.z, -best.dx, -best.dz);
      }
    });
    return picked;
  }

  /* The occupancy grid is one cell per two metres, so a door found with it
     can stand anything up to two metres out in the road - which is "floating"
     as far as anyone looking at it is concerned. So once a frontage is chosen
     the actual wall is found with a ray against the building geometry
     itself: the hit point is where the door goes, and the hit normal is the
     way it faces, so it sits flat on a diagonal facade as well as a square
     one. If the ray finds nothing (an occupancy cell with no mesh in it) the
     grid position stands. */
  function snapToWall(spot) {
    var meshes = [];
    TILES.forEach(function (t) {
      if (!t._city) return;
      t._city.traverse(function (o) { if (o.isMesh) meshes.push(o); });
    });
    if (!meshes.length) return false;
    /* nothing has rendered yet when this runs, so the tiles' world matrices
       are still identity - bring them up to date or the ray tests the mesh
       at the origin instead of where the tile stands */
    scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    var gy = groundAt(spot.x, spot.z);
    /* Sweep from the spot itself: the grid may have stopped us beside a
       building rather than in front of one (a street corner, the flank of a
       block), in which case the nearest wall is off to the side. Whichever
       wall is closest, within reach, is the one the door goes on. */
    var base = Math.atan2(spot.dz, spot.dx), hit = null;
    var o = new THREE.Vector3(spot.x, gy + 1.4, spot.z);
    [0, 30, -30, 60, -60, 90, -90, 120, -120, 150, -150, 180].forEach(function (deg) {
      var ang = base + deg * Math.PI / 180;
      var dir = new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(ang), 0, Math.sin(ang));
      var hits = new THREE.Raycaster(o, dir, 0.05, 9.0).intersectObjects(meshes, false);
      for (var i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) {
        var n = hits[i].face && hits[i].face.normal;
        if (!n) continue;
        var wn = n.clone().transformDirection(hits[i].object.matrixWorld);
        if (Math.abs(wn.y) > 0.5) continue;              /* a wall, not a roof edge or a slab */
        /* and a wall with room in front of it: a door in a metre-wide gap
           between two blocks is a door nobody can stand at */
        var out = wn.clone(); out.y = 0; out.normalize();
        if (out.dot(dir) > 0) out.negate();
        var ahead = new THREE.Raycaster(hits[i].point.clone().addScaledVector(out, 0.3), out, 0.0, 4.5)
                      .intersectObjects(meshes, false);
        var clear = true;
        for (var k = 0; k < ahead.length; k++) {
          var an = ahead[k].face && ahead[k].face.normal;
          if (an && Math.abs(an.clone().transformDirection(ahead[k].object.matrixWorld).y) < 0.5) { clear = false; break; }
        }
        if (!clear) break;
        if (!hit || hits[i].distance < hit.d) hit = { d: hits[i].distance, p: hits[i].point, n: wn, dir: dir };
        break;
      }
    });
    if (!hit) return false;
    var n2 = hit.n.clone(); n2.y = 0; n2.normalize();
    /* the normal should point out of the building, back toward the street */
    if (n2.dot(hit.dir) > 0) n2.negate();
    var OUT = 0.16;                                   /* half the frame's depth, so the back of the frame is on the wall */
    spot.x = hit.p.x + n2.x * OUT;
    spot.z = hit.p.z + n2.z * OUT;
    spot.dx = -n2.x; spot.dz = -n2.z;                 /* the approach is straight into the wall now */
    spot.snapped = true;
    return true;
  }

  function buildPortal(spot) {
    var shop = spot.shop;
    var gy = groundAt(spot.x, spot.z);
    var grp = new THREE.Group();
    grp.position.set(spot.x, gy, spot.z);
    /* face back down the street, toward where the viewer is standing */
    grp.rotation.y = Math.atan2(-spot.dx, -spot.dz);

    var lit = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: CHAMPAGNE, fog: false });
    var W = 3.4, H = 5.6, T = 0.26;
    [[-W / 2, H / 2], [W / 2, H / 2]].forEach(function (p) {
      var b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(T, H, T), lit);
      b.position.set(p[0], p[1], 0); grp.add(b);
    });
    var top = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(W + T, T, T), lit);
    top.position.set(0, H, 0); grp.add(top);

    /* the doorway itself: light spilling out of the building */
    var hall = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(W, H),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: MIST, transparent: true, opacity: 0.18,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    hall.position.set(0, H / 2, 0.02);
    grp.add(hall);

    /* the name on the building above the door */
    var sign = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(W * 2.0, W * 2.0 / 4),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: wordmarkTexture(shop.label), transparent: true,
        depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    sign.position.set(0, H + 2.0, 0.05);
    grp.add(sign);

    grp.userData = { shop: shop, x: spot.x, z: spot.z, gy: gy, hall: hall, sign: sign, snapped: !!spot.snapped };
    return grp;
  }

  /* A pin you can see from anywhere, drawn through the buildings. Without it
     a doorway one block over simply does not exist as far as the viewer is
     concerned, and the whole point is that these are the way out. */
  function buildMarker(spot) {
    var gy = groundAt(spot.x, spot.z);
    var grp = new THREE.Group();
    grp.position.set(spot.x, gy, spot.z);

    var beam = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.22, 150, 6, 1, true),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: CHAMPAGNE, transparent: true, opacity: 0.16,
        depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    beam.position.y = 75;
    beam.renderOrder = 990;
    grp.add(beam);

    var tag = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.PlaneGeometry(34, 8.5),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: wordmarkTexture(spot.shop.label), transparent: true,
        depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, fog: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    tag.position.y = 26;
    tag.renderOrder = 991;
    grp.add(tag);

    grp.userData = { tag: tag, x: spot.x, z: spot.z };
    return grp;
  }

  function buildGround() {
    /* The plate used to be tessellated on the CPU at roughly 23 m and then
       dropped a metre so the roads would clear it. That is why the roads
       floated: two different descriptions of the same ground, one of them
       coarse. Now the mesh samples the ground atlas in its own vertex shader,
       so it IS the terrain at whatever density the mesh has, and the roads -
       which the atlas was built from - sit on it. No drop, no lip. */
    var a = MANI.atlas;
    var g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(a.w, a.h, PLATE_SEG, PLATE_SEG);
    g.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);

    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: Object.assign({
        uInk:    { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
        uFog:    { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG },
        uRoads:  { value: ROADS },
        uHasRoads: { value: ROADS ? 1 : 0 },
        uPearl:  { value: new THREE.Color(PEARL) },
        uVec:    { value: STREETS ? 1 : 0 }
      }, atlasUniforms(), lookUniforms(), maskUniforms(), groundExtraUniforms()),
      vertexShader: [
        'varying vec3 vW; varying vec2 vUv;',
        ATLAS_GLSL,
        'void main(){',
        '  vec4 w = modelMatrix * vec4(position,1.0);',
        '  w.y = gAt(w.xz);',
        '  vW = w.xyz;',
        '  vUv = (w.xz - uOrigin) / uSize;',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: GROUND_FRAG.join('\n')
    });
    var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, m);
    mesh.position.set(a.x0 + a.w / 2, 0, a.z0 + a.h / 2);
    mesh.frustumCulled = false;
    return mesh;
  }

  /* ======================= THE STREET LIFE (v4) =======================
     Bogota as it is at night, with Midnight Club in its veins.

     THE WALLS   every building gets a material of its own, read from a map
                 rasterised at load from the cadastre: the red brick that is
                 most of the city, painted stucco in the colonial colours,
                 board-formed concrete, glass curtain walls on the towers,
                 white render. Low roofs are clay tile. The relief takes the
                 trim of its building (concrete on brick, white on stucco,
                 dark aluminium on glass) and the towers get mullions.
     THE NEON    shopfronts on the street carry neon signs - panaderia,
                 drogueria, billares, the bars - some of them failing; every
                 light glows, and the wet asphalt throws a streak of it
                 back at you.
     THE STREET  lampposts the whole length of every street (and the far
                 ones as a carpet of lights), SITP bus stops with backlit
                 posters, cars parked along the kerbs - one in four a yellow
                 taxi - traffic driving the real streets with light trails,
                 people on the pavements, some of them dancing outside the
                 bars to whatever is playing.
     THE CAR     walk up to a parked car and press F: it is yours. W/S
                 throttle and brake, A/D steer, Space the handbrake, F to
                 get out where you stopped.
     THE FINDS   tokens hidden around the city; walk into one and it gives
                 up a FYMA code or a free print (REWARDS in LIFE).
     QUALITY     'auto' reads the graphics card and the machine and picks
                 low / mid / high, then steps down by itself if the first
                 seconds of walking run slow. Anyone with a browser gets a
                 city that moves.
     ================================================================= */
  var LIFEX = CITY_CONFIG.LIFE || {};
  var LIFE_ON = LIFEX.ON !== false;
  var LIFE = null;
  var QUAL = null;
  var matU = { value: null }, matBoxU = { value: new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 1) }, matOnU = { value: 0 };
  /* the lights the street shader sees: the nearest NL lamps, signs and car lights, every frame */
  var NL = 24;
  var LIGHTU = { p: { value: [] }, c: { value: [] }, n: { value: 0 }, tu: { value: 0 } };
  for (var lu = 0; lu < NL; lu++) { LIGHTU.p.value.push(new THREE.Vector4(0, -1e4, 0, 0)); LIGHTU.c.value.push(new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 0, 0)); }
  var STREET_LIGHT_GLSL = [
    'uniform vec4 uLP[' + NL + ']; uniform vec4 uLC[' + NL + ']; uniform float uLN;',
    /* pools under every light, and the wet street throwing each one back as a streak toward the eye */
    'vec3 streetLights(vec3 P, float wet, float t){',
    '  vec3 acc = vec3(0.0);',
    '  vec3 V = normalize(cameraPosition - P); vec3 R = vec3(-V.x, V.y, -V.z);',
    '  vec2 rh = normalize(R.xz + 1e-5);',
    '  for (int k = 0; k < ' + NL + '; k++) {',
    '    if (float(k) >= uLN) break;',
    '    vec4 lp = uLP[k], lc = uLC[k];',
    '    vec3 d = lp.xyz - P; float d2 = dot(d.xz, d.xz);',
    '    acc += lc.rgb * exp(-d2 / (lc.w * lc.w)) * 0.5;',
    '    vec3 L = normalize(d); vec2 lh = normalize(L.xz + 1e-5);',
    '    float dh = 1.0 - dot(rh, lh), dv = R.y - L.y;',
    '    float rip = 0.55 + 0.45 * sin(dot(P.xz, vec2(3.1, 2.3)) + t * 1.7 + lp.x);',
    '    acc += lc.rgb * exp(-dh * 1400.0) * exp(-dv * dv * 7.0) * wet * rip * 1.3;',
    '  }',
    '  return acc;',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');
  var CL_BRICK = 0, CL_STUCCO = 1, CL_CONCRETE = 2, CL_GLASS = 3, CL_WHITE = 4;

  function hh(a, b) { var x = Math.sin(a * 127.1 + b * 311.7) * 43758.5453; return x - Math.floor(x); }
  function lin(hex) { return new THREE.Color(hex).convertSRGBToLinear(); }

  /* ---- quality ---- */
  var TIERS = [
    { name: 'low',  px: 1.0,  shadows: false, relief: 110, inst: 45000, cars: 44,  peds: 70,  parkR: 140, lampR: 210, stopR: 260, signs: 110, streaks: 140 },
    { name: 'mid',  px: 1.5,  shadows: true,  relief: 150, inst: 72000, cars: 80,  peds: 130, parkR: 200, lampR: 290, stopR: 360, signs: 200, streaks: 260 },
    { name: 'high', px: 2.0,  shadows: true,  relief: 170, inst: 90000, cars: 120, peds: 200, parkR: 260, lampR: 360, stopR: 440, signs: 300, streaks: 380 }
  ];
  function pickQuality() {
    var want = String(LIFEX.QUALITY || 'auto').toLowerCase(), t = 1, gpu = '';
    if (want === 'low') t = 0; else if (want === 'mid') t = 1; else if (want === 'high') t = 2;
    else {
      try {
        var c = document.createElement('canvas'), gl = c.getContext('webgl');
        var ext = gl && gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
        gpu = String(ext ? gl.getParameter(ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL) : '');
        var lose = gl && gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context'); if (lose) lose.loseContext();
      } catch (e) {}
      var cores = navigator.hardwareConcurrency || 4, mem = navigator.deviceMemory || 8;
      if (/swiftshader|llvmpipe|software|basic render|microsoft basic/i.test(gpu)) t = 0;
      else if (/nvidia|geforce|rtx|gtx|radeon rx|radeon pro|apple m[1-9]|apple gpu/i.test(gpu)) t = cores >= 6 ? 2 : 1;
      else if (/intel|uhd|iris|mali|adreno|powervr/i.test(gpu)) t = (cores <= 4 || mem <= 4) ? 0 : 1;
      else if (!gpu || /^brave$/i.test(gpu.trim())) t = TOUCH ? 0 : 1;     /* the name is hidden (Brave): assume a desktop GPU */
      else t = (cores <= 4 || mem <= 4) ? 0 : 1;
      if (TOUCH) t = Math.min(t, 1);
    }
    QUAL = Object.assign({ tier: t, gpu: gpu }, TIERS[t]);
    FACX.RADIUS = Math.min(FACX.RADIUS || 170, QUAL.relief);
    FACX.MAX_INSTANCES = Math.min(FACX.MAX_INSTANCES || 90000, QUAL.inst);
    if (!QUAL.shadows) FACX.SHADOWS = false;
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] quality: ' + QUAL.name + (gpu ? ' (' + gpu + ')' : ''));
  }
  function dropQuality() {
    if (!QUAL || QUAL.tier === 0) return;
    var t = QUAL.tier - 1;
    QUAL = Object.assign({ tier: t, gpu: QUAL.gpu, dropped: true }, TIERS[t]);
    try { rend.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, QUAL.px)); resize(); } catch (e) {}
    if (!QUAL.shadows && moon) moon.castShadow = false;
    if (LIFE) { LIFE.lastX = 1e9; }
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] running slow - quality down to ' + QUAL.name);
  }

  /* ---- the material of every building ---- */
  function buildingClasses() {
    var n = FAC.n;
    FAC.cls = new Uint8Array(n); FAC.pal = new Uint8Array(n); FAC.roof = new Uint8Array(n); FAC.sd = new Uint8Array(n);
    for (var b = 0; b < n; b++) {
      var F = FAC.floors[b], r = hh(FAC.minx[b] * 0.013, FAC.minz[b] * 0.017), c;
      if (F >= 12)     c = r < 0.55 ? CL_GLASS : r < 0.85 ? CL_CONCRETE : CL_BRICK;
      else if (F >= 5) c = r < 0.58 ? CL_BRICK : r < 0.78 ? CL_CONCRETE : r < 0.90 ? CL_STUCCO : r < 0.95 ? CL_GLASS : CL_WHITE;
      else             c = r < 0.52 ? CL_BRICK : r < 0.88 ? CL_STUCCO : CL_WHITE;
      FAC.cls[b] = c;
      FAC.pal[b] = Math.floor(hh(b, 7.7) * 8) & 7;
      FAC.roof[b] = (F <= 3 && c !== CL_GLASS && hh(b, 3.3) > 0.3) ? 1 : 0;
      FAC.sd[b] = Math.floor(hh(b, 1.9) * 255);
    }
  }
  /* the map: one texel per ~3 m over the whole core, every footprint filled
     with its building's material, scanline by scanline */
  function buildMatMap() {
    var a = MANI.atlas, N = LIFEX.MATMAP || 2048, D = new Uint8Array(N * N * 4);
    var sx = N / a.w, sz = N / a.h, V = FAC.verts;
    /* the base of every building, so the shader knows where its floors are: whole metres in G over a floor YB0, the fraction in B under the roof bit, a seed in A */
    var ybs = [], lo = 1e9, hi = -1e9;
    for (var bq = 0; bq < FAC.n; bq++) { var yv = FAC.top[bq] - 3 * FAC.floors[bq]; ybs.push(yv); if (yv < lo) lo = yv; if (yv > hi) hi = yv; }
    var sorted = ybs.slice().sort(function (p, q) { return p - q; }), YB0 = Math.floor(sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.002)]);
    YB0U.value = YB0;
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] building bases ' + lo.toFixed(1) + '..' + hi.toFixed(1) + ' m, stored from ' + YB0);
    /* OWN BLOCKS: the cadastre is full of slivers - a strip a metre wide
       between two houses, a 6 m2 shed against a wall - and every one of them
       used to get a material of its own, so a wall changed from brick to
       stucco a metre from its corner. Each of those now belongs to the
       biggest building it touches: same material, same colour, same floor
       lines. The ordinary houses keep theirs. */
    var host = new Int32Array(FAC.n), CELLH = FAC.CELL || 64, fused = 0;
    for (var hb = 0; hb < FAC.n; hb++) {
      host[hb] = hb;
      var w0 = FAC.maxx[hb] - FAC.minx[hb], d0 = FAC.maxz[hb] - FAC.minz[hb];
      if (LIFEX.FUSE === false || (Math.min(w0, d0) >= 4.5 && w0 * d0 >= 45)) continue;
      var best = -1, bestA = w0 * d0 * 1.5, tol = 0.6;
      for (var hx = Math.floor((FAC.minx[hb] - tol) / CELLH); hx <= Math.floor((FAC.maxx[hb] + tol) / CELLH); hx++) {
        for (var hz = Math.floor((FAC.minz[hb] - tol) / CELLH); hz <= Math.floor((FAC.maxz[hb] + tol) / CELLH); hz++) {
          var list = FAC.hash[hx + ',' + hz]; if (!list) continue;
          for (var li = 0; li < list.length; li++) {
            var o2 = list[li]; if (o2 === hb) continue;
            if (FAC.minx[o2] > FAC.maxx[hb] + tol || FAC.maxx[o2] < FAC.minx[hb] - tol || FAC.minz[o2] > FAC.maxz[hb] + tol || FAC.maxz[o2] < FAC.minz[hb] - tol) continue;
            var A2 = (FAC.maxx[o2] - FAC.minx[o2]) * (FAC.maxz[o2] - FAC.minz[o2]);
            if (A2 > bestA && Math.abs(ybs[o2] - ybs[hb]) < 2.0) { bestA = A2; best = o2; }
          }
        }
      }
      if (best >= 0) { host[hb] = best; fused++; }
    }
    FAC.host = host;
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] own blocks: ' + fused + ' slivers joined to the building beside them');
    for (var b = 0; b < FAC.n; b++) {
      var n = FAC.ringLen[b], s = FAC.ringStart[b], hm = host[b];
      var ybv = Math.max(0, Math.min(255.99, ybs[hm] - YB0)), gi = Math.floor(ybv), fr = Math.min(127, Math.round((ybv - gi) * 127));
      var r = FAC.cls[hm] * 16 + FAC.pal[hm], g = gi, bl = (FAC.roof[hm] ? 128 : 0) + fr, al = 128 + (FAC.sd[hm] & 127);
      var j0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((FAC.minz[b] - a.z0) * sz - 0.5)), j1 = Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor((FAC.maxz[b] - a.z0) * sz - 0.5));
      var xs = [];
      for (var j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
        var zc = a.z0 + (j + 0.5) / sz;
        xs.length = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          var k = (i + 1) % n;
          var x1 = V[(s + i) * 2], z1 = V[(s + i) * 2 + 1], x2 = V[(s + k) * 2], z2 = V[(s + k) * 2 + 1];
          if ((z1 <= zc && z2 > zc) || (z2 <= zc && z1 > zc)) xs.push(x1 + (zc - z1) / (z2 - z1) * (x2 - x1));
        }
        xs.sort(function (p, q) { return p - q; });
        for (var e = 0; e + 1 < xs.length; e += 2) {
          var i0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((xs[e] - a.x0) * sx - 0.5)), i1 = Math.min(N - 1, Math.floor((xs[e + 1] - a.x0) * sx - 0.5));
          for (var ii = i0; ii <= i1; ii++) { var o = (j * N + ii) * 4; D[o] = r; D[o + 1] = g; D[o + 2] = bl; D[o + 3] = al; }
        }
      }
    }
    var tex = new THREE.DataTexture(D, N, N, THREE.RGBAFormat);
    tex.magFilter = THREE.NearestFilter; tex.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter; tex.generateMipmaps = false; tex.flipY = false;
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
    matU.value = tex; matBoxU.value.set(a.x0, a.z0, a.w, a.h); matOnU.value = 1;
  }

  var BOGOTA_GLSL = [
    'uniform sampler2D uMat; uniform vec4 uMatBox; uniform float uMatOn;',
    'vec3 linC(vec3 c){ return pow(c, vec3(2.2)); }',
    'float vn(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);',
    '  return mix(mix(h21(i), h21(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x), mix(h21(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), h21(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y); }',
    'vec3 bogPal(float i){',
    '  if (i < 0.5) return vec3(0.90, 0.85, 0.72);',
    '  if (i < 1.5) return vec3(0.80, 0.62, 0.33);',
    '  if (i < 2.5) return vec3(0.72, 0.38, 0.25);',
    '  if (i < 3.5) return vec3(0.30, 0.49, 0.66);',
    '  if (i < 4.5) return vec3(0.55, 0.66, 0.53);',
    '  if (i < 5.5) return vec3(0.80, 0.55, 0.56);',
    '  if (i < 6.5) return vec3(0.64, 0.79, 0.71);',
    '  return vec3(0.92, 0.90, 0.86);',
    '}',
    'vec2 atUV(float i, vec2 uv){ return (vec2(mod(i, 4.0), floor(i / 4.0)) * 512.0 + 16.0 + fract(uv) * 480.0) / 2048.0; }',
    'vec4 atS(sampler2D t, float i, vec2 uv){',
    '  vec2 g = uv * (480.0 / 2048.0);',
    '#if __VERSION__ >= 300',
    '  return textureGrad(t, atUV(i, uv), dFdx(g), dFdy(g));',
    '#else',
    '  return texture2D(t, atUV(i, uv));',
    '#endif',
    '}',
    'float lumC(vec3 c){ return dot(c, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)); }',
    'vec3 atTex(float i, vec2 uv, float sz, vec3 avg, float fw, out vec3 nrm){',
    '  float nearF = 1.0 - smoothstep(sz / 110.0, sz / 28.0, fw);',
    '  nrm = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);',
    '  if (nearF < 0.01) return avg;',
    '  vec3 t = atS(uAtD, i, uv).rgb;',
    '  vec3 nn = atS(uAtN, i, uv).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0;',
    '  nrm = normalize(mix(vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0), vec3(nn.xy * 0.9, nn.z), nearF));',
    '  return mix(avg, t, nearF);',
    '}',
    'vec3 bogWallP(float cls, float pal, float sd, float u, float y);',
    'vec3 bogWall(float cls, float pal, float sd, float u, float y){',
    '  gTexN = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);',
    '  if (uAtOn < 0.5 || (cls > 2.5 && cls < 3.5)) return bogWallP(cls, pal, sd, u, y);',
    '  float grime = 0.86 + 0.14 * vn(vec2(u * 0.15, y * 0.09) + sd * 40.0);',
    '  float fw = max(fwidth(u), fwidth(y));',
    '  vec2 off = vec2(sd * 3.7, sd * 1.3);',
    '  vec3 c, nrm;',
    '  if (cls < 0.5) {',
    '    vec3 b0 = mix(vec3(0.50, 0.20, 0.12), vec3(0.70, 0.34, 0.20), sd);',
    '    bool b4 = sd > 0.42; vec3 av = b4 ? vec3(0.588, 0.435, 0.314) : vec3(0.416, 0.325, 0.294);',
    '    vec3 t = b4 ? atTex(1.0, vec2(u, -y) / 1.3 + off, 1.3, av, fw, nrm) : atTex(0.0, vec2(u, -y) / 1.15 + off, 1.15, av, fw, nrm);',
    '    c = b0 * mix(vec3(lumC(t) / lumC(av)), t / av, 0.45) * (0.95 + 0.1 * fract(sd * 7.0));',
    '  } else if (cls < 1.5) {',
    '    bool worn = fract(sd * 5.3) < 0.26;',
    '    vec3 av = worn ? vec3(0.733, 0.682, 0.608) : vec3(0.671, 0.639, 0.627);',
    '    vec3 t = atTex(worn ? 5.0 : 2.0, vec2(u, -y) / (worn ? 2.2 : 2.6) + off, worn ? 2.2 : 2.6, av, fw, nrm);',
    '    c = bogPal(pal) * mix(1.0, lumC(t) / lumC(av), worn ? 0.95 : 0.8);',
    '  } else if (cls < 2.5) {',
    '    if (fract(sd * 3.1) < 0.33) c = atTex(6.0, vec2(u, -y) / 2.4 + off, 2.4, vec3(0.212, 0.22, 0.235), fw, nrm) * 1.35;',
    '    else {',
    '      vec3 av = vec3(0.808, 0.722, 0.616);',
    '      vec3 t = atTex(4.0, vec2(u, -y) / 3.2 + off, 3.2, av, fw, nrm);',
    '      vec2 pn = vec2(fract(u / 1.2), fract(y / 0.6));',
    '      float ln = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.025, pn.y)) * 0.06 * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.02, 0.08, fw));',
    '      c = vec3(0.55, 0.54, 0.52) * (0.88 + 0.22 * sd) * (1.0 + (lumC(t) / lumC(av) - 1.0) * 0.7 - ln) + (t / av - lumC(t) / lumC(av)) * 0.09;',
    '    }',
    '  } else {',
    '    vec3 av = vec3(0.557, 0.529, 0.478);',
    '    vec3 t = atTex(3.0, vec2(u, -y) / 2.0 + off, 2.0, av, fw, nrm);',
    '    c = vec3(0.90, 0.88, 0.84) * mix(1.0, lumC(t) / lumC(av), 0.85);',
    '  }',
    '  gTexN = nrm;',
    '  return linC(clamp(c, 0.0, 1.0) * grime);',
    '}',
    'vec3 bogWallP(float cls, float pal, float sd, float u, float y){',
    '  float grime = 0.84 + 0.16 * vn(vec2(u * 0.15, y * 0.09) + sd * 40.0);',
    '  if (cls < 0.5) {',
    '    vec3 b0 = mix(vec3(0.50, 0.20, 0.12), vec3(0.70, 0.34, 0.20), sd);',
    '    float bv = y / 0.075, row = floor(bv);',
    '    float bu = u / 0.25 + 0.5 * mod(row, 2.0);',
    '    vec2 g = vec2(fract(bu), fract(bv));',
    '    vec2 fw = fwidth(vec2(bu, bv));',
    '    float brick = smoothstep(0.0, 0.07 + fw.x, g.x) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.93 - fw.x, g.x) * smoothstep(0.0, 0.15 + fw.y, g.y) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.85 - fw.y, g.y);',
    '    float near = clamp(1.6 - max(fw.x, fw.y) * 2.2, 0.0, 1.0);',
    '    vec3 one = b0 * (0.82 + 0.34 * h21(vec2(floor(bu), row) + sd * 17.0));',
    '    vec3 mort = vec3(0.60, 0.55, 0.49);',
    '    vec3 c = mix(mort, one, brick);',
    '    vec3 avg = mix(b0, mort, 0.2);',
    '    return linC(mix(avg, c, near) * grime);',
    '  }',
    '  if (cls < 1.5) return linC(bogPal(pal) * grime * (0.93 + 0.07 * vn(vec2(u, y) * 0.8)));',
    '  if (cls < 2.5) {',
    '    vec2 pn = vec2(fract(u / 1.2), fract(y / 0.6));',
    '    float ln = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.025, pn.y)) * 0.08 + (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.012, pn.x)) * 0.05;',
    '    float streak = vn(vec2(u * 2.0, y * 0.12)) * 0.12;',
    '    return linC(vec3(0.53, 0.52, 0.50) * (0.88 + 0.22 * sd) * (grime - ln - streak));',
    '  }',
    '  if (cls < 3.5) return linC(vec3(0.06, 0.09, 0.12));',
    '  return linC(vec3(0.89, 0.87, 0.83) * grime);',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  /* what the relief is made of, by building: the trim of each material */
  function trimTint(bi, tn, tw) {
    var c = FAC.cls ? FAC.cls[bi] : -1, v;
    if (c === CL_BRICK)         v = [0.86, 0.82, 0.76];
    else if (c === CL_STUCCO)   v = [1.02, 1.00, 0.96];
    else if (c === CL_CONCRETE) v = [0.60, 0.59, 0.57];
    else if (c === CL_GLASS)    v = [0.14, 0.15, 0.17];
    else if (c === CL_WHITE)    v = [0.95, 0.94, 0.92];
    else return [tn * (1 + 0.05 * tw), tn, tn * (1 - 0.06 * tw)];
    return [v[0] * tn, v[1] * tn, v[2] * tn];
  }
  function kindFor(bi, st) {
    if (!FAC.cls) return kindOf(st);
    var c = FAC.cls[bi], r = (st + 0.5) / 1000;
    if (c === CL_GLASS) return 'G';
    if (c === CL_CONCRETE) return r < 0.5 ? 'B' : 'E';
    if (c === CL_STUCCO) return r < 0.45 ? 'A' : r < 0.75 ? 'C' : 'D';
    if (c === CL_WHITE) return r < 0.5 ? 'B' : 'D';
    return r < 0.35 ? 'D' : r < 0.6 ? 'A' : r < 0.8 ? 'E' : 'C';
  }
  /* the towers: a curtain wall - mullions, transoms, a crown; the glass
     itself is drawn by the wall shader at every distance */
  function dressCurtain(F, yb, y1, street) {
    var L = W.L, py = Math.min(street - 0.15, yb);
    box(L + 0.04, (yb + 0.6) - py, 0.2, L / 2, py, 0);
    var nm = Math.max(1, Math.round(L / 1.5));
    for (var m = 0; m <= nm; m++) { var u = L * m / nm; if (!clearOf(u - 0.1, u + 0.1)) continue; box(0.07, y1 - yb, 0.12, u, yb, 0); }
    for (var j = 1; j < F; j++) courseAcross(0.12, 0.10, yb + 3 * j - 0.06, 0);
    box(L + 0.1, 0.5, 0.25, L / 2, y1 - 0.5, 0);
  }

  /* ---- shared geometry helpers ---- */
  function mergeParts(parts) {
    /* parts: [{ g: geometry, m: Matrix4, c: [r,g,b], a: {name: value}, }] -> one non-indexed geometry with colour + extra attributes */
    var pos = [], nor = [], col = [], extra = {};
    parts.forEach(function (p) { Object.keys(p.a || {}).forEach(function (k) { extra[k] = extra[k] || []; }); });
    parts.forEach(function (p) {
      var g = p.g.index ? p.g.toNonIndexed() : p.g.clone();
      if (p.m) g.applyMatrix4(p.m);
      g.computeVertexNormals();
      var P = g.attributes.position.array, Nn = g.attributes.normal.array, cnt = P.length / 3;
      for (var i = 0; i < cnt; i++) {
        pos.push(P[i * 3], P[i * 3 + 1], P[i * 3 + 2]); nor.push(Nn[i * 3], Nn[i * 3 + 1], Nn[i * 3 + 2]);
        col.push(p.c[0], p.c[1], p.c[2]);
        Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (k) {
          var v = (p.a && p.a[k] != null) ? p.a[k] : 0;
          if (Array.isArray(v)) extra[k].push(v[0], v[1], v[2]); else extra[k].push(v);
        });
      }
    });
    var out = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(nor, 3));
    out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(col, 3));
    Object.keys(extra).forEach(function (k) { var arr = extra[k]; out.setAttribute(k, new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(arr, arr.length === pos.length ? 3 : 1)); });
    return out;
  }
  function mt(x, y, z, rx, ry, rz, sx, sy, sz) {
    var m = new THREE.Matrix4();
    m.compose(new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z), new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(rx || 0, ry || 0, rz || 0)), new THREE.Vector3(sx || 1, sy || 1, sz || 1));
    return m;
  }
  /* an instance matrix standing at (x, y, z), its local +z along (fx, fz) */
  function placeAt(arr, i, x, y, z, fx, fz, s) {
    var o = i * 16; s = s || 1;
    arr[o] = fz * s; arr[o + 1] = 0; arr[o + 2] = -fx * s; arr[o + 3] = 0;
    arr[o + 4] = 0; arr[o + 5] = s; arr[o + 6] = 0; arr[o + 7] = 0;
    arr[o + 8] = fx * s; arr[o + 9] = 0; arr[o + 10] = fz * s; arr[o + 11] = 0;
    arr[o + 12] = x; arr[o + 13] = y; arr[o + 14] = z; arr[o + 15] = 1;
  }
  function placeTilt(arr, i, x, y, z, fx, fz, hl, hw) {
    /* hl / hw: half the wheelbase / track. y comes back: the height the
       middle of the axles sits at, so the wheels touch on a slope */
    var gf = groundAt(x + fx * hl, z + fz * hl), gb = groundAt(x - fx * hl, z - fz * hl);
    var gr = groundAt(x + fz * hw, z - fx * hw), gl = groundAt(x - fz * hw, z + fx * hw);
    var yy = Math.min((gf + gb) / 2, (gr + gl) / 2) + (y || 0);
    var Fx = fx, Fy = (gf - gb) / (2 * hl), Fz = fz, fl = Math.hypot(Fx, Fy, Fz); Fx /= fl; Fy /= fl; Fz /= fl;
    var Xx = fz, Xy = (gr - gl) / (2 * hw), Xz = -fx;
    /* Y = F x X, then X = Y x F so the three are square */
    var Yx = Fy * Xz - Fz * Xy, Yy = Fz * Xx - Fx * Xz, Yz = Fx * Xy - Fy * Xx, yl = Math.hypot(Yx, Yy, Yz); Yx /= yl; Yy /= yl; Yz /= yl;
    Xx = Yy * Fz - Yz * Fy; Xy = Yz * Fx - Yx * Fz; Xz = Yx * Fy - Yy * Fx;
    var o = i * 16;
    arr[o] = Xx; arr[o + 1] = Xy; arr[o + 2] = Xz; arr[o + 3] = 0;
    arr[o + 4] = Yx; arr[o + 5] = Yy; arr[o + 6] = Yz; arr[o + 7] = 0;
    arr[o + 8] = Fx; arr[o + 9] = Fy; arr[o + 10] = Fz; arr[o + 11] = 0;
    arr[o + 12] = x; arr[o + 13] = yy; arr[o + 14] = z; arr[o + 15] = 1;
    return yy;
  }
  function instGeometry(base, attrs, cap) {
    var g = new THREE.InstancedBufferGeometry();
    g.index = base.index; g.setAttribute('position', base.attributes.position);
    if (base.attributes.uv) g.setAttribute('uv', base.attributes.uv);
    Object.keys(attrs).forEach(function (k) {
      var a = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap * attrs[k]), attrs[k]);
      a.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); g.setAttribute(k, a);
    });
    g.instanceCount = 0;
    return g;
  }

  /* ---- light: glows, pools on the ground, streaks in the wet ---- */
  var FOGC = 'float fogOf(vec3 w){ float d = length(w - cameraPosition); return clamp(1.0 - exp(-d * d * uFog * uFog), 0.0, 1.0); }';
  function glowLayer(cap) {
    var g = instGeometry(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), { iPos: 3, iCol: 3, iSize: 1, iVel: 3 }, cap);
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG }, uFar: { value: 900 } },
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute vec3 iPos; attribute vec3 iCol; attribute float iSize; attribute vec3 iVel;',
        'varying vec2 vQ; varying vec3 vC; varying float vF;',
        'uniform float uFar;',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec4 mv = viewMatrix * vec4(iPos, 1.0);',
        '  vec3 vv = (viewMatrix * vec4(iVel, 0.0)).xyz;',
        '  float L = length(vv.xy);',
        '  vec2 ax = vec2(1.0, 0.0), ay = vec2(0.0, 1.0);',
        '  if (L > 0.01) { ax = vv.xy / L; ay = vec2(-ax.y, ax.x); }',
        '  vec2 q = position.xy;',
        '  mv.xy += ax * (q.x * (iSize + L * 0.5) + L * 0.5) + ay * q.y * iSize;',
        '  vQ = q; vC = iCol;',
        '  vF = 1.0 - smoothstep(uFar * 0.7, uFar, -mv.z);',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec2 vQ; varying vec3 vC; varying float vF;',
        'void main(){',
        '  float r2 = dot(vQ, vQ);',
        '  float a = exp(-r2 * 5.0) * 0.85 + exp(-r2 * 28.0) * 1.2;',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(vC * a * vF, 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      transparent: true, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    });
    var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, m); mesh.frustumCulled = false; mesh.renderOrder = 8;
    return { mesh: mesh, n: 0, cap: cap, a: g.attributes };
  }
  function glowPut(L, x, y, z, r, g, b, size, vx, vy, vz) {
    var i = L.n; if (i >= L.cap) return; L.n++;
    L.a.iPos.array[i * 3] = x; L.a.iPos.array[i * 3 + 1] = y; L.a.iPos.array[i * 3 + 2] = z;
    L.a.iCol.array[i * 3] = r; L.a.iCol.array[i * 3 + 1] = g; L.a.iCol.array[i * 3 + 2] = b;
    L.a.iSize.array[i] = size;
    L.a.iVel.array[i * 3] = vx || 0; L.a.iVel.array[i * 3 + 1] = vy || 0; L.a.iVel.array[i * 3 + 2] = vz || 0;
  }
  function layerDone(L) {
    L.mesh.geometry.instanceCount = L.n;
    Object.keys(L.a).forEach(function (k) { if (L.a[k].isInstancedBufferAttribute) L.a[k].needsUpdate = true; });
  }
  /* ---- the neon ---- */
  var SIGNS = [
    { t: 'PANADERÍA',   c: '#FFB347', f: 'italic 700 92px Georgia, serif' },
    { t: 'DROGUERÍA',   c: '#4BFF9E', f: '700 88px Arial, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'BILLARES',    c: '#3FE6FF', f: '900 96px Impact, Arial Black, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'BAR LA 19',   c: '#FF3FA4', f: 'italic 800 92px Arial, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'TINTO · CAFÉ', c: '#FFB347', f: '700 84px Georgia, serif' },
    { t: 'HOTEL',       c: '#FF4258', f: '900 100px Arial Black, Impact, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'FERRETERÍA',  c: '#3FE6FF', f: '700 80px Arial, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'ASADERO',     c: '#FF6A2B', f: 'italic 900 96px Arial Black, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'DISCOTECA',   c: '#A86BFF', f: '900 90px Impact, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'PELUQUERÍA',  c: '#FF3FA4', f: 'italic 700 80px Georgia, serif' },
    { t: 'ABIERTO 24H', c: '#4BFF9E', f: '800 84px Arial, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'MISCELÁNEA',  c: '#FFD84A', f: '700 80px Arial, sans-serif' },
    { t: 'F.Y.M.A.',    c: '#CDEDFB', f: "300 100px 'LalaTitle', Arial, sans-serif" },
    { t: 'CEDAIN',      c: '#E8D3A6', f: "300 100px 'LalaTitle', Arial, sans-serif" },
    { t: 'LA LAGUNA',   c: '#9EB4D3', f: "300 96px 'LalaTitle', Arial, sans-serif" },
    { t: 'EMPANADAS',   c: '#FFB347', f: 'italic 800 88px Arial, sans-serif' }
  ];
  function signAtlas() {
    var cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = 1024; cv.height = 1024;
    var g = cv.getContext('2d');
    g.fillStyle = '#000'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 1024, 1024);
    SIGNS.forEach(function (s, i) {
      var cx = (i % 2) * 512 + 256, cy = Math.floor(i / 2) * 128 + 64;
      g.save();
      g.beginPath(); g.rect(cx - 256, cy - 64, 512, 128); g.clip();
      g.font = s.f; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
      var w = g.measureText(s.t).width, k = Math.min(1, 470 / w);
      g.translate(cx, cy); g.scale(k, Math.min(1, k * 1.15));
      g.shadowColor = s.c; g.shadowBlur = 30; g.fillStyle = s.c; g.globalAlpha = 0.9; g.fillText(s.t, 0, 4);
      g.globalAlpha = 1; g.shadowBlur = 8; g.fillText(s.t, 0, 4);
      var cc = new THREE.Color(s.c).lerp(new THREE.Color(0xffffff), 0.55);
      g.shadowBlur = 0; g.lineWidth = 2.0; g.strokeStyle = '#' + cc.getHexString(); g.strokeText(s.t, 0, 4);
      g.restore();
    });
    var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv); t.anisotropy = 4;
    return t;
  }
  function signLayer(cap) {
    var base = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1); base.translate(0, 0.5, 0);
    var g = new THREE.InstancedBufferGeometry();
    g.index = base.index; g.setAttribute('position', base.attributes.position); g.setAttribute('uv', base.attributes.uv);
    ['iA', 'iB', 'iC', 'iD'].forEach(function (k) { var a = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap * 4), 4); a.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); g.setAttribute(k, a); });
    g.instanceCount = 0;
    var m = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uTex: { value: signAtlas() }, uT: { value: 0 }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG } },
      vertexShader: [
        /* iA: origin xyz + cell; iB: along (tx,tz) * width, height, seed; iC: out (nx,nz), unused x2 */
        'attribute vec4 iA; attribute vec4 iB; attribute vec4 iC; attribute vec4 iD;',
        'varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vW; varying float vSeed;',
        'void main(){',
        '  float cell = iA.w;',
        '  vec2 c0 = vec2(mod(cell, 2.0) * 0.5, 1.0 - (floor(cell / 2.0) + 1.0) * 0.125);',
        '  vUv = c0 + vec2(uv.x * 0.5, uv.y * 0.125);',
        '  vec3 w = iA.xyz + vec3(iB.x, 0.0, iB.y) * position.x + vec3(0.0, iB.z, 0.0) * position.y;',
        '  vW = w; vSeed = iB.w;',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * vec4(w, 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'uniform sampler2D uTex; uniform float uT, uFog; varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vW; varying float vSeed;', FOGC,
        'float hs(float n){ return fract(sin(n) * 43758.5453); }',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec3 c = texture2D(uTex, vUv).rgb;',
        '  float on = 1.0;',
        '  if (vSeed > 0.86) on = step(0.35, hs(floor(uT * 9.0) + vSeed * 100.0));',          /* a failing tube */
        '  else if (vSeed > 0.7) on = 0.75 + 0.25 * step(0.5, sin(uT * 1.3 + vSeed * 40.0));',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(c * 1.05 * on * (1.0 - fogOf(vW)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      transparent: true, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
    var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, m); mesh.frustumCulled = false; mesh.renderOrder = 7;
    return { mesh: mesh, n: 0, cap: cap, a: g.attributes, list: [] };
  }
  function hexRGB(h) { var c = new THREE.Color(h); return [c.r, c.g, c.b]; }

  /* called by updateRelief for every dressed wall: a sign on the shopfronts that face a street */
  function lifeSignsBegin() { if (!LIFE) return; LIFE.signs.n = 0; LIFE.signs.list.length = 0; }
  function lifeWall(bi, kind, F, yb, y1, street) {
    if (!LIFE || kind === 'G' || kind === 'B') return;
    var S = LIFE.signs, L = W.L;
    if (S.n >= Math.min(S.cap, QUAL ? QUAL.signs : 200) || L < 5.5 || y1 - yb < 5.0) return;
