    var r = hh(bi * 0.71, W.wi * 1.3);
    if (r > (LIFEX.SIGN_CHANCE || 0.45)) return;
    var mx = W.x1 + W.tx * L / 2 + W.nx * 4, mz = W.z1 + W.tz * L / 2 + W.nz * 4;
    if (!nearRoad(mx, mz)) return;
    var sw = Math.min(5.2, L * 0.62), shh = sw * 0.25, u = L / 2, gS = null;
    if (BLOCKS) {
      var Bq = wallBays(L, W.ws), shops = [];
      for (var kq = 0; kq < Bq.nb; kq++) if (gfKind(W.ws, kq) === 0) shops.push(kq);
      if (!shops.length) return;
      var ks = shops[Math.floor(hh(bi, W.wi + 2.2) * shops.length) % shops.length];
      u = Bq.m + (ks + 0.5) * Bq.bay; sw = Math.min(5.2, Bq.bay * 0.86); shh = Math.min(0.6, sw * 0.22);
      gS = groundAt(W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * 1.2, W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * 1.2);
      if (Math.min(W.f0, y1 - 0.35) - gS < 2.95 + shh) return;
    }
    if (!clearOf(u - sw / 2 - 0.2, u + sw / 2 + 0.2)) return;
    var cell = Math.floor(hh(bi, W.wi + 5.1) * SIGNS.length) % SIGNS.length;
    var y = gS != null ? gS + 2.47 : yb + 2.72, out = W.off + 0.34;
    var ox = W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * out, oz = W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * out;
    var i = S.n++, A = S.a;
    A.iA.array[i * 4] = ox; A.iA.array[i * 4 + 1] = y; A.iA.array[i * 4 + 2] = oz; A.iA.array[i * 4 + 3] = cell;
    A.iB.array[i * 4] = -W.tx * sw; A.iB.array[i * 4 + 1] = -W.tz * sw; A.iB.array[i * 4 + 2] = shh; A.iB.array[i * 4 + 3] = hh(bi, 9.1);
    var col = hexRGB(SIGNS[cell].c);
    S.list.push({ x: W.x1 + W.tx * u + W.nx * (out + 0.6), y: y + shh / 2, z: W.z1 + W.tz * u + W.nz * (out + 0.6), w: sw, c: col, g: street,
                  nx: W.nx, nz: W.nz });
  }
  function lifeSignsEnd() {
    if (!LIFE) return;
    LIFE.signs.mesh.geometry.instanceCount = LIFE.signs.n;
    ['iA', 'iB'].forEach(function (k) { LIFE.signs.a[k].needsUpdate = true; });
    LIFE.staticDirty = true; LIFE.dancersDirty = true;
  }

  /* ---- the streets as a network ---- */
  function nkey(x, z) { return Math.round(x / 1.5) + ',' + Math.round(z / 1.5); }
  function buildNetwork() {
    var nodes = {}, W2 = STREETS;
    W2.forEach(function (w, wi) {
      var n = w.pts.length / 2, acc = 0;
      w.len = [0];
      for (var i = 1; i < n; i++) { acc += Math.hypot(w.pts[i * 2] - w.pts[i * 2 - 2], w.pts[i * 2 + 1] - w.pts[i * 2 - 1]); w.len.push(acc); }
      w.L = acc;
      w.on = !PLATFORM || PLATFORM.at((w.box[0] + w.box[2]) / 2, (w.box[1] + w.box[3]) / 2) > 0.3 || PLATFORM.at(w.pts[0], w.pts[1]) > 0.3;
      w.park = (w.A >= 8 && w.A < 14 && w.L > 40) ? 1 : 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < n; j++) { var k = nkey(w.pts[j * 2], w.pts[j * 2 + 1]); (nodes[k] || (nodes[k] = [])).push(wi, j); }
    });
    return nodes;
  }
  /* road proximity: 8 m cells near any carriageway */
  function buildRoadGrid() {
    var a = MANI.atlas, C = 8, N = Math.ceil(a.w / C), grid = new Uint8Array(N * N);
    STREETS.forEach(function (w) {
      if (w.A < 2.5) return;
      var n = w.pts.length / 2, r = Math.ceil((w.A / 2 + w.SW + 5) / C);
      for (var i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
        var ax = w.pts[i * 2], az = w.pts[i * 2 + 1], bx = w.pts[i * 2 + 2], bz = w.pts[i * 2 + 3];
        var L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az), k = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L / (C * 0.5)));
        for (var j = 0; j <= k; j++) {
          var x = ax + (bx - ax) * j / k, z = az + (bz - az) * j / k;
          var ci = Math.floor((x - a.x0) / C), cj = Math.floor((z - a.z0) / C);
          for (var di = -r; di <= r; di++) for (var dj = -r; dj <= r; dj++) {
            var ii = ci + di, jj = cj + dj; if (ii < 0 || jj < 0 || ii >= N || jj >= N) continue; grid[jj * N + ii] = 1;
          }
        }
      }
    });
    return { grid: grid, N: N, C: C, x0: a.x0, z0: a.z0 };
  }
  function nearRoad(x, z) {
    var R = LIFE && LIFE.road; if (!R) return true;
    var i = Math.floor((x - R.x0) / R.C), j = Math.floor((z - R.z0) / R.C);
    return i >= 0 && j >= 0 && i < R.N && j < R.N && R.grid[j * R.N + i] === 1;
  }
  /* a point on a way: segment i, distance s into it, lateral offset (right of the polyline's own direction) */
  function wayPoint(w, i, s, off, out) {
    var ax = w.pts[i * 2], az = w.pts[i * 2 + 1], bx = w.pts[i * 2 + 2], bz = w.pts[i * 2 + 3];
    var L = (w.len[i + 1] - w.len[i]) || 1, dx = (bx - ax) / L, dz = (bz - az) / L;
    out.x = ax + dx * s - dz * off; out.z = az + dz * s + dx * off; out.dx = dx; out.dz = dz;
    return out;
  }
  /* things laid along every way at a spacing: [x, z, fx, fz, way, extra] in a flat array, and a hash */
  function hashOf(arr, stride, CELL) {
    var h = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length / stride; i++) {
      var k = Math.floor(arr[i * stride] / CELL) + ',' + Math.floor(arr[i * stride + 1] / CELL);
      (h[k] || (h[k] = [])).push(i);
    }
    return h;
  }
  function eachNear(set, x, z, R, fn) {
    var C = set.CELL;
    for (var cx = Math.floor((x - R) / C); cx <= Math.floor((x + R) / C); cx++)
      for (var cz = Math.floor((z - R) / C); cz <= Math.floor((z + R) / C); cz++) {
        var l = set.hash[cx + ',' + cz]; if (!l) continue;
        for (var q = 0; q < l.length; q++) {
          var i = l[q], dx = set.d[i * set.S] - x, dz = set.d[i * set.S + 1] - z, dd = dx * dx + dz * dz;
          if (dd < R * R) fn(i, Math.sqrt(dd));
        }
      }
  }
  function alongWays(minA, maxA, spacing, sideFn, offFn, keep) {
    var out = [], P = {};
    STREETS.forEach(function (w, wi) {
      if (!w.on || w.A < minA || w.A >= maxA || w.L < spacing * 0.8) return;
      var sp = typeof spacing === 'function' ? spacing(w) : spacing;
      var n = w.pts.length / 2, k = 0;
      for (var s = sp * 0.5 + hh(wi, 2) * sp * 0.5; s < w.L - 6; s += sp, k++) {
        var i = 0; while (i < n - 2 && w.len[i + 1] < s) i++;
        var sides = sideFn(w, k);
        for (var si = 0; si < sides.length; si++) {
          var side = sides[si], off = offFn(w) * side;
          wayPoint(w, i, s - w.len[i], off, P);
          if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at(P.x, P.z) < 0.6) continue;
          if (blocked(P.x, P.z)) continue;
          if (keep && !keep(P, w, wi, s)) continue;
          out.push(P.x, P.z, -P.dz * side * -1, P.dx * side * -1, wi, side, P.dx, P.dz);
        }
      }
    });
    return out;   /* stride 8: x, z, toward-road fx, fz, way, side, along dx, dz */
  }

  /* ---- the street furniture: lamps ---- */
  function lampGeometry() {
    var P = [], dark = [0.11, 0.12, 0.13], head = [0.85, 0.85, 0.82];
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.11, 8, 6), m: mt(0, 4, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.2, 0.5, 6), m: mt(0, 0.25, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.045, 0.045, 1.6, 5), m: mt(0, 7.85, 0.75, Math.PI / 2 - 0.12, 0, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.34, 0.12, 0.7), m: mt(0, 7.92, 1.55), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.26, 0.03, 0.56), m: mt(0, 7.85, 1.55), c: head });
    return mergeParts(P);
  }
  /* ---- bus stops (SITP) ---- */
  function stopGeometry() {
    var P = [], metal = [0.16, 0.18, 0.2], roof = [0.55, 0.58, 0.62], bench = [0.32, 0.33, 0.35], blue = [0.05, 0.27, 0.62];
    [-1.6, 1.6].forEach(function (x) { P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 2.5, 0.08), m: mt(x, 1.25, -0.6), c: metal }); P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 2.5, 0.08), m: mt(x, 1.25, 0.55), c: metal }); });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.08, 1.6), m: mt(0, 2.54, -0.02), c: roof });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 0.14, 0.12), m: mt(0, 2.45, 0.75), c: blue });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.06, 0.42), m: mt(-0.4, 0.48, -0.32), c: bench });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.4, 0.05), m: mt(-0.4, 0.75, -0.53), c: bench });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.06, 2.9, 0.06), m: mt(2.1, 1.45, 0.75), c: metal });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.7, 0.04), m: mt(2.1, 2.7, 0.75), c: blue });
    return mergeParts(P);
  }
  function stopGlassGeometry() {
    var g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.1, 2.0); g.translate(0, 1.35, -0.6);
    return g;
  }
  function adAtlas() {
    var cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = 1024; cv.height = 512;
    var g = cv.getContext('2d');
    function poster(i, bg1, bg2, title, sub, tcol, font) {
      var x = i * 256;
      var gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 512); gr.addColorStop(0, bg1); gr.addColorStop(1, bg2);
      g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(x, 0, 256, 512);
      g.save(); g.translate(x + 128, 0);
      g.fillStyle = tcol; g.textAlign = 'center';
      g.font = font || "300 46px 'LalaTitle', Arial, sans-serif"; g.fillText(title, 0, 250);
      g.font = "600 14px Arial, sans-serif"; g.globalAlpha = 0.85;
      sub.forEach(function (l, k) { g.fillText(l, 0, 300 + k * 22); });
      g.restore(); g.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    poster(0, '#050304', '#3E5F6A', 'F.Y.M.A.', ['COLLECTION 01', 'THE WIND', '', 'FYMA.STORE'], '#CDEDFB');
    poster(1, '#08090C', '#4F0609', 'CEDAIN', ['SETS · LIVE', 'BOGOTÁ', '', 'LALAGUNA.STUDIO/CEDAIN'], '#E8D3A6');
    poster(2, '#EDE6E8', '#9EB4D3', 'LALAGUNA', ['STUDIO', 'ARCHITECTURE · CAPTURE', '', 'LALAGUNA.STUDIO'], '#08090C');
    g.fillStyle = '#0B4DA2'; g.fillRect(768, 0, 256, 512); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.fillRect(768, 90, 256, 4);
    g.font = '900 54px Arial, sans-serif'; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillText('SITP', 896, 70);
    var routes = ['C19  CHAPINERO', 'K23  LA CANDELARIA', '17-4  USAQUÉN', 'T25  SUBA', '560  CALLE 72'];
    g.font = '700 17px Arial, sans-serif'; g.textAlign = 'left';
    routes.forEach(function (r, k) { g.fillStyle = ['#F2C200', '#E2231A', '#69BE28', '#00A3E0', '#F28C00'][k]; g.fillRect(790, 130 + k * 64, 20, 20); g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.fillText(r, 822, 147 + k * 64); });
    var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv); return t;
  }
  function adMaterial() {
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uTex: { value: adAtlas() }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG } },
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute float aAd; varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vW;',
        'void main(){ vUv = vec2((uv.x + aAd) * 0.25, uv.y); vec4 w = modelMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); vW = w.xyz; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w; }'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'uniform sampler2D uTex; uniform float uFog; varying vec2 vUv; varying vec3 vW;', FOGC,
        'void main(){ vec3 c = texture2D(uTex, vUv).rgb * 1.15; gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(c, vec3(0.031, 0.035, 0.047), fogOf(vW)), 1.0); }'
      ].join('\n')
    });
  }

  /* ---- cars ---- */
  var CAR_TYPES = [
    { k: 'hatch', L: 3.6, Wd: 1.62, H: 1.48 },
    { k: 'sedan', L: 4.5, Wd: 1.78, H: 1.42 },
    { k: 'suv',   L: 4.6, Wd: 1.86, H: 1.74 },
    { k: 'bus',   L: 11.0, Wd: 2.5, H: 3.1 }
  ];
  function carGeometry(T) {
    var P = [], body = [1, 1, 1], glass = [0.035, 0.04, 0.05], tyre = [0.03, 0.03, 0.03], tail = [0.5, 0.02, 0.02], head = [1.3, 1.25, 1.1];
    var L = T.L, Wd = T.Wd, H = T.H, wr = T.k === 'bus' ? 0.5 : 0.31;
    if (T.k === 'bus') {
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd, H - 0.4, L), m: mt(0, 0.4 + (H - 0.4) / 2, 0), c: body });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd + 0.02, 1.1, L - 1.2), m: mt(0, 1.85, -0.2), c: glass });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd - 0.2, 1.3, 0.05), m: mt(0, 1.8, L / 2 + 0.01), c: glass });
    } else {
      var cabL = T.k === 'hatch' ? L * 0.62 : T.k === 'suv' ? L * 0.66 : L * 0.5, cabZ = T.k === 'hatch' ? -L * 0.12 : T.k === 'suv' ? -L * 0.1 : -L * 0.04;
      var lowH = T.k === 'suv' ? 0.8 : 0.62;
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd, lowH, L), m: mt(0, 0.28 + lowH / 2, 0), c: body });
      var cabH = H - 0.28 - lowH - 0.05;
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd * 0.9, cabH, cabL), m: mt(0, 0.28 + lowH + cabH / 2, cabZ), c: glass });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(Wd * 0.88, 0.06, cabL * 0.86), m: mt(0, 0.28 + lowH + cabH + 0.03, cabZ), c: body });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, cabH, 0.1), m: mt(-Wd * 0.44, 0.28 + lowH + cabH / 2, cabZ + cabL / 2 - 0.05), c: body });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, cabH, 0.1), m: mt(Wd * 0.44, 0.28 + lowH + cabH / 2, cabZ + cabL / 2 - 0.05), c: body });
    }
    [[-1, 1], [1, 1], [-1, -1], [1, -1]].forEach(function (q) {
      P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(wr, wr, 0.24, 10), m: mt(q[0] * (Wd / 2 - 0.1), wr, q[1] * (L / 2 - (T.k === 'bus' ? 2.0 : 0.78)), 0, 0, Math.PI / 2), c: tyre });
    });
    [-1, 1].forEach(function (s) {
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.34, 0.12, 0.04), m: mt(s * (Wd / 2 - 0.26), T.k === 'bus' ? 0.9 : 0.72, L / 2 + 0.02), c: head });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.3, 0.12, 0.04), m: mt(s * (Wd / 2 - 0.24), T.k === 'bus' ? 1.0 : 0.82, -L / 2 - 0.02), c: tail });
    });
    return mergeParts(P);
  }
  function taxiSignGeometry() { return mergeParts([{ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.62, 0.16, 0.22), m: mt(0, 1.56, -0.4), c: [1.4, 1.3, 0.9] }]); }
  var CARPAINT = null;
  function carPaints() {
    return {
      std: ['#F2F2F0', '#B8BCC2', '#15171A', '#4A4E55', '#A3151C', '#1F3F7A', '#5A1A22', '#C8B89A', '#DADCDE', '#2E3A2F'].map(lin),
      taxi: lin('#F2C200'), sitp: lin('#0E5AA7'), tm: lin('#C8102E'), lala: lin('#111216')
    };
  }

  /* ---- people ---- */
  function personGeometry() {
    var P = [], I = new THREE.Matrix4();
    function part(g, sel, partId, pivot) { P.push({ g: g, m: I, c: [1, 1, 1], a: { aPart: partId, aSel: sel, aPivot: pivot } }); }
    function cyl(rt, rb, h, seg, x, y, z, sx, sz) { var g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rt, rb, h, seg, 1); g.scale(sx || 1, 1, sz || 1); g.translate(x, y, z); return g; }
    function ball(r, ws, hs, x, y, z, sx, sy, sz, tl) { var g = new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, ws, hs, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, tl || Math.PI); g.scale(sx || 1, sy || 1, sz || 1); g.translate(x, y, z); return g; }
    part(cyl(0.205, 0.168, 0.34, 8, 0, 1.30, 0, 1, 0.62), 1, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(cyl(0.168, 0.158, 0.20, 8, 0, 1.03, 0, 1, 0.66), 1, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(cyl(0.162, 0.150, 0.15, 8, 0, 0.875, 0, 1, 0.70), 2, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(cyl(0.048, 0.056, 0.10, 6, 0, 1.50, 0), 0, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(ball(0.105, 8, 6, 0, 1.635, 0.012, 0.92, 1.15, 1.0), 0, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(ball(0.114, 8, 4, 0, 1.652, -0.006, 0.94, 1.12, 1.05, Math.PI * 0.56), 3, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.03, 0.045, 0.03).translate(0, 1.625, 0.115), 0, 0, [0, 0, 0]);
    [-1, 1].forEach(function (s) {
      var id = s < 0 ? 1 : 2, hx = s * 0.092;
      part(cyl(0.078, 0.062, 0.44, 7, hx, 0.62, 0), 2, id, [hx, 0.86, 0]);
      part(cyl(0.060, 0.046, 0.42, 7, hx, 0.24, 0), 2, id, [hx, 0.86, 0]);
      part(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.105, 0.085, 0.25).translate(hx, 0.043, 0.045), 4, id, [hx, 0.86, 0]);
    });
    [-1, 1].forEach(function (s) {
      var id = s < 0 ? 3 : 4, ax = s * 0.235;
      part(ball(0.068, 6, 4, ax - s * 0.01, 1.44, 0), 1, id, [ax, 1.45, 0]);
      part(cyl(0.056, 0.048, 0.30, 6, ax, 1.30, 0), 1, id, [ax, 1.45, 0]);
      part(cyl(0.046, 0.040, 0.27, 6, ax + s * 0.005, 1.03, 0), 0, id, [ax, 1.45, 0]);
      part(ball(0.045, 6, 4, ax + s * 0.008, 0.86, 0, 0.8, 1.2, 0.9), 0, id, [ax, 1.45, 0]);
    });
    return mergeParts(P);
  }
  function personMaterial() {
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uT: { value: 0 }, uBeat: { value: 0 }, uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG }, uInk: { value: new THREE.Color(INK) },
                  uSky: { value: new THREE.Color(0x6B7A96) }, uWarm: { value: new THREE.Color(CHAMPAGNE) } },
      vertexShader: [
        'attribute float aPart; attribute float aSel; attribute vec3 aPivot;',
        'attribute vec3 iShirt; attribute vec3 iPants; attribute vec4 iAnim;',
        'uniform float uT, uBeat;',
        'varying vec3 vCol; varying vec3 vN; varying vec3 vW;',
        'mat3 rx(float a){ float c = cos(a), s = sin(a); return mat3(1.0,0.0,0.0, 0.0,c,s, 0.0,-s,c); }',
        'mat3 rz(float a){ float c = cos(a), s = sin(a); return mat3(c,s,0.0, -s,c,0.0, 0.0,0.0,1.0); }',
        'mat3 ry(float a){ float c = cos(a), s = sin(a); return mat3(c,0.0,-s, 0.0,1.0,0.0, s,0.0,c); }',
        'void main(){',
        '  vec3 p = position, n = normal;',
        '  float ph = iAnim.x + uT * iAnim.y * 6.2831853, mode = iAnim.z, sw = sin(ph), bob = 0.0;',
        '  mat3 R = mat3(1.0);',
        '  if (mode < 0.5) {',
        '    float a = 0.0;',
        '    if (aPart > 0.5 && aPart < 1.5) a = 0.55 * sw; else if (aPart > 1.5 && aPart < 2.5) a = -0.55 * sw;',
        '    else if (aPart > 2.5 && aPart < 3.5) a = -0.45 * sw; else if (aPart > 3.5) a = 0.45 * sw;',
        '    float la = 0.55 * sw; R = rx(a); bob = 0.012 * abs(cos(ph)) - (0.86 * (1.0 - cos(la)) - 0.075 * abs(sin(la)));',
        '  } else if (mode < 1.5) {',
        '    float beat = uBeat;',
        '    if (aPart > 2.5 && aPart < 3.5) R = rz(-2.5 - 0.35 * sin(ph * 2.0)) * rx(0.3 * sin(ph));',
        '    else if (aPart > 3.5) R = rz(2.5 + 0.35 * sin(ph * 2.0 + 1.0)) * rx(0.3 * cos(ph));',
        '    else if (aPart > 0.5 && aPart < 1.5) R = rx(0.18 * sin(ph * 2.0));',
        '    else if (aPart > 1.5 && aPart < 2.5) R = rx(-0.18 * sin(ph * 2.0));',
        '    bob = beat * 0.11 + 0.03 * abs(sin(ph * 2.0)) - 0.86 * (1.0 - cos(0.18 * sin(ph * 2.0)));',
        '  } else if (mode < 2.5) {',
        '    if (aPart > 0.5 && aPart < 1.5) R = rx(-1.1); else if (aPart > 1.5 && aPart < 2.5) R = rx(0.9);',
        '    else if (aPart > 2.5) R = rz(aPart < 3.5 ? -2.2 : 2.2);',
        '    bob = 0.25;',
        '  } else {',
        '    if (aPart > 2.5 && aPart < 3.5) R = rx(-0.15 * sin(ph * 0.5)); else if (aPart > 3.5) R = rx(0.1 * sin(ph * 0.5));',
        '    bob = 0.01 * sin(ph);',
        '  }',
        '  p = aPivot + R * (p - aPivot); n = R * n;',
        '  if (mode > 0.5 && mode < 1.5) { mat3 T = ry(0.45 * sin(ph)); p = T * p; n = T * n; }',
        '  p.y += bob; p *= iAnim.w;',
        '  float s1 = fract(iAnim.x * 7.31);',
        '  vec3 skin = mix(vec3(0.36, 0.22, 0.14), vec3(0.78, 0.58, 0.44), s1);',
        '  vec3 hair = mix(vec3(0.03, 0.025, 0.02), vec3(0.25, 0.15, 0.08), step(0.8, fract(iAnim.x * 3.1)));',
        '  vCol = aSel < 0.5 ? skin : aSel < 1.5 ? iShirt : aSel < 2.5 ? iPants : aSel < 3.5 ? hair : vec3(0.05);',
        '  vec4 w = modelMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0);',
        '  vW = w.xyz; vN = normalize(mat3(instanceMatrix) * n);',
        '  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * w;',
        '}'
      ].join('\n'),
      fragmentShader: [
        'varying vec3 vCol; varying vec3 vN; varying vec3 vW; uniform float uFog; uniform vec3 uInk, uSky, uWarm;', FOGC,
        'void main(){',
        '  vec3 L = normalize(vec3(-0.4, 0.75, 0.4));',
        '  float d = 0.5 + 0.5 * dot(vN, L);',
        '  vec3 V = normalize(cameraPosition - vW);',
        '  float rim = pow(1.0 - abs(dot(vN, V)), 2.5);',
        '  vec3 c = vCol * (0.22 + 0.62 * d) + vCol * uWarm * 0.18 * (1.0 - vN.y) + vec3(0.55, 0.2, 0.7) * rim * 0.35 + uSky * rim * 0.15;',
        '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(c, uInk, fogOf(vW)), 1.0);',
        '}'
      ].join('\n')
    });
  }
  var SHIRTS = ['#E8D3A6', '#9EB4D3', '#B8262E', '#1D1F24', '#F2F2F0', '#3E5F6A', '#CDEDFB', '#6B2C91', '#E86A1A', '#2A6E3F', '#F2C200', '#4F0609', '#D9447A', '#2E2F33'];
  var PANTS = ['#1A1C22', '#2B3A55', '#3A3530', '#14161A', '#5B5F66', '#2F3E2C', '#0E0F12'];

  /* ======================= build ======================= */
  function buildLife(spawn) {
    if (!LIFE_ON || !STREETS || !FAC) return null;
    var t0 = performance.now();
    var nodes = buildNetwork();
    LIFE = { nodes: nodes, road: buildRoadGrid(), lastX: 1e9, lastZ: 1e9, staticDirty: true, dancersDirty: true,
             cars: [], peds: [], parkedNear: [], driving: false, t: 0, ui: false, beat: 0, slow: { t: 0, n: 0, done: false } };
    var group = new THREE.Group();
    LIFE.sigs = buildSignals(nodes); LIFE.nearSigs = [];
    LIFE.sigMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(signalGeometry(), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.55, metalness: 0.35 }), 400);
    LIFE.sigMesh.count = 0; LIFE.sigMesh.frustumCulled = false; group.add(LIFE.sigMesh);

    /* lamps: one every ~30 m, both sides of the wide roads */
    var lamps = alongWays(3, 999, function (w) { return w.A >= 9 ? 26 : 32; },
      function (w, k) { return w.A >= 12 ? [1, -1] : [k % 2 ? 1 : -1]; },
      function (w) { return w.A / 2 + Math.min(0.55, w.SW * 0.3); });
    LIFE.lamps = { d: Float32Array.from(lamps), S: 8, CELL: 64 }; LIFE.lamps.hash = hashOf(LIFE.lamps.d, 8, 64);
    LIFE.lamps.n = lamps.length / 8;
    LIFE.lampMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(lampGeometry(), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.6, metalness: 0.3 }), 1800);
    LIFE.lampMesh.count = 0; LIFE.lampMesh.frustumCulled = false; LIFE.lampMesh.castShadow = false;
    group.add(LIFE.lampMesh);
    /* the far lamps: a carpet of points */
    var lp = new Float32Array(LIFE.lamps.n * 3), lc = new Float32Array(LIFE.lamps.n * 3);
    for (var li = 0; li < LIFE.lamps.n; li++) {
      var L8 = LIFE.lamps.d, ls = STREETS[L8[li * 8 + 4]].A >= 12 ? 1.1 : 1, lx = L8[li * 8] + L8[li * 8 + 2] * 1.55 * ls, lz = L8[li * 8 + 1] + L8[li * 8 + 3] * 1.55 * ls;
      var amber = hh(L8[li * 8 + 4], li) < 0.62;
      lp[li * 3] = lx; lp[li * 3 + 1] = groundAt(L8[li * 8], L8[li * 8 + 1]) - 0.06 + 7.82 * ls; lp[li * 3 + 2] = lz;
      var col = amber ? [1.0, 0.62, 0.3] : [0.78, 0.86, 1.0];
      lc[li * 3] = col[0]; lc[li * 3 + 1] = col[1]; lc[li * 3 + 2] = col[2];
    }
    LIFE.lampHead = lp; LIFE.lampCol = lc;
    var pg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); pg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(lp, 3)); pg.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(lc, 3));
    var pts = new THREE.Points(pg, new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      uniforms: { uFog: { value: CITY_CONFIG.FOG }, uNear: { value: 150 } },
      vertexShader: 'attribute vec3 color; varying vec3 vC; varying float vA; uniform float uNear, uFog; void main(){ vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); float d = -mv.z; vC = color; vA = smoothstep(uNear * 0.8, uNear, d) * (1.0 - clamp(1.0 - exp(-d * d * uFog * uFog * 0.35), 0.0, 1.0)); gl_PointSize = clamp(2600.0 / d, 1.5, 7.0); gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv; }',
      fragmentShader: 'varying vec3 vC; varying float vA; void main(){ vec2 q = gl_PointCoord * 2.0 - 1.0; float a = exp(-dot(q, q) * 3.0); gl_FragColor = vec4(vC * a * vA * 1.4, 1.0); }',
      transparent: true, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    pts.frustumCulled = false; pts.renderOrder = 4; group.add(pts); LIFE.points = pts;

    /* bus stops on the bigger roads */
    var stops = alongWays(7, 999, 300, function (w, k) { return [k % 2 ? 1 : -1]; }, function (w) { return w.A / 2 + Math.max(0.9, w.SW * 0.55); });
    LIFE.stops = { d: Float32Array.from(stops), S: 8, CELL: 128 }; LIFE.stops.hash = hashOf(LIFE.stops.d, 8, 128); LIFE.stops.n = stops.length / 8;
    LIFE.stopMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(stopGeometry(), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.3 }), 120);
    var gm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9EB4D3, transparent: true, opacity: 0.22, roughness: 0.05, metalness: 0.0, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    LIFE.stopGlass = new THREE.InstancedMesh(stopGlassGeometry(), gm, 120);
    var adG = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.2, 1.8); adG.translate(1.0, 1.3, 0.62);
    adG.setAttribute('aAd', new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(120), 1));
    LIFE.adMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(adG, adMaterial(), 120);
    [LIFE.stopMesh, LIFE.stopGlass, LIFE.adMesh].forEach(function (m) { m.count = 0; m.frustumCulled = false; group.add(m); });

    /* parking: the kerbs of the in-between streets, kept clear of the junctions */
    var park = alongWays(8, 14, 6.1, function () { return [1, -1]; }, function (w) { return w.A / 2 - 1.05; }, function (P, w, wi, s) {
      if (s < 12 || s > w.L - 12) return false;
      if (hh(wi * 0.37 + s * 0.11, P.x * 0.01) > (LIFEX.PARKED || 0.52)) return false;
      for (var dx = -8; dx <= 8; dx += 8) for (var dz = -8; dz <= 8; dz += 8) {
        var nl = nodes[nkey(P.x + dx, P.z + dz)]; if (!nl) continue;
        for (var q = 0; q < nl.length; q += 2) if (nl[q] !== wi) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
    LIFE.park = { d: Float32Array.from(park), S: 8, CELL: 64 }; LIFE.park.hash = hashOf(LIFE.park.d, 8, 64); LIFE.park.n = park.length / 8;
    LIFE.parkTaken = {};
    /* about one parked car in fifty is a LALAGUNA car - and always the one nearest where you start */
    LIFE.lala = new Uint8Array(LIFE.park.n);
    var pd = LIFE.park.d, bestP = -1, bestD = 1e9;
    for (var pi = 0; pi < LIFE.park.n; pi++) {
      var ppx = pd[pi * 8], ppz = pd[pi * 8 + 1];
      if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at(ppx, ppz) < 0.6) continue;
      if (hh(pi, 9.9) < (LIFEX.LALA_CARS || 0.02)) LIFE.lala[pi] = 1;
      var dsp = Math.hypot(ppx - spawn.x, ppz - spawn.z);
      if (dsp > 8 && dsp < bestD) { bestD = dsp; bestP = pi; }
    }
    if (bestP >= 0) LIFE.lala[bestP] = 1;
    /* and one at the kerb right where you start, on the street you are standing on */
    LIFE.left = LIFE.left || [];
    try {
      var bw = null, bdw = 1e9;
      STREETS.forEach(function (w) {
        if (!w.on || w.A < 6 || w.box[2] < spawn.x - 120 || w.box[0] > spawn.x + 120 || w.box[3] < spawn.z - 120 || w.box[1] > spawn.z + 120) return;
        for (var k = 0; k + 3 < w.pts.length; k += 2) {
          var ax = w.pts[k], az = w.pts[k + 1], bx = w.pts[k + 2], bz = w.pts[k + 3], sx = bx - ax, sz = bz - az, ll = sx * sx + sz * sz || 1;
          var t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((spawn.x - ax) * sx + (spawn.z - az) * sz) / ll)), qx = ax + sx * t, qz = az + sz * t, d = Math.hypot(spawn.x - qx, spawn.z - qz);
          if (d < bdw) { bdw = d; bw = { w: w, qx: qx, qz: qz, tx: sx / Math.sqrt(ll), tz: sz / Math.sqrt(ll) }; }
        }
      });
      if (bw) {
        var nx0 = -bw.tz, nz0 = bw.tx, sideS = ((spawn.x - bw.qx) * nx0 + (spawn.z - bw.qz) * nz0) >= 0 ? 1 : -1, off = bw.w.A / 2 - 1.1;
        for (var ahead = 12; ahead <= 40; ahead += 4) {
          var fwd = ((MANI.spawn.yawDeg != null) ? 1 : 1) * ahead;
          var cxs = bw.qx + bw.tx * fwd + nx0 * off * sideS, czs = bw.qz + bw.tz * fwd + nz0 * off * sideS;
          if (blocked(cxs, czs) || blocked(cxs + bw.tx * 2.4, czs + bw.tz * 2.4) || blocked(cxs - bw.tx * 2.4, czs - bw.tz * 2.4)) continue;
          LIFE.left.push({ type: 1, col: lin('#111216'), taxi: false, lala: 1, x: cxs, z: czs, hx: bw.tx, hz: bw.tz });
          if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] a LALAGUNA car parked ' + Math.round(Math.hypot(cxs - spawn.x, czs - spawn.z)) + ' m from the start');
          break;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    CARPAINT = carPaints();
    LIFE.carMeshes = CAR_TYPES.map(function (T) {
      var cap = T.k === 'bus' ? 40 : 700;
      var m = new THREE.InstancedMesh(carGeometry(T), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.26, metalness: 0.2 }), cap);
      m.count = 0; m.frustumCulled = false; m.castShadow = false; m.receiveShadow = false; m.cap = cap;
      m.instanceColor = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(cap * 3), 3);
      m.instanceColor.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage); m.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      group.add(m); return m;
    });
    LIFE.taxiSign = new THREE.InstancedMesh(taxiSignGeometry(), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ vertexColors: true }), 300);
    /* the wordmark on the hood of a LALAGUNA car (a sedan: the hood runs from the windscreen at 0.95 m to the nose at 2.25 m, 0.9 m up) */
    var dg = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.45, 1.1); dg.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); dg.translate(0, 0.912, 1.62);
    LIFE.lalaDecal = new THREE.InstancedMesh(dg, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: hoodTexture(), transparent: true, depthWrite: false,
      polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -2, toneMapped: false }), 64);
    LIFE.lalaDecal.count = 0; LIFE.lalaDecal.frustumCulled = false; LIFE.lalaDecal.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
    group.add(LIFE.lalaDecal);
    LIFE.taxiSign.count = 0; LIFE.taxiSign.frustumCulled = false; group.add(LIFE.taxiSign);

    /* people */
    var pgeo = personGeometry(), PCAP = 260;
    ['iShirt', 'iPants'].forEach(function (k) { pgeo.setAttribute(k, new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(PCAP * 3), 3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage)); });
    pgeo.setAttribute('iAnim', new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(PCAP * 4), 4).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    LIFE.pedMat = personMaterial();
    LIFE.pedMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(pgeo, LIFE.pedMat, PCAP);
    LIFE.pedMesh.count = 0; LIFE.pedMesh.frustumCulled = false; LIFE.pedMesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
    group.add(LIFE.pedMesh);

    /* light */
    LIFE.glowS = glowLayer(4200); LIFE.glowD = glowLayer(1400);
    LIFE.signs = signLayer(420);
    [LIFE.glowS, LIFE.glowD, LIFE.signs].forEach(function (L) { group.add(L.mesh); });

    /* the car you drive */
    LIFE.me = null;
    LIFE.rewards = buildRewards(spawn, group);

    LIFE.group = group;
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] street life: ' + LIFE.lamps.n + ' lamps, ' + LIFE.stops.n + ' stops, ' + LIFE.park.n + ' parked cars, ' + LIFE.sigs.length + ' signals, ' + LIFE.rewards.list.length + ' finds, in ' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms');
    wireLife();
    return group;
  }

  /* ---- the near sets, rebuilt as you move ---- */
  /* SOLIDS: what you cannot walk or drive through, near you - circles (poles) and boxes
     (shelters, planters, parked cars) as flat footprints; moving cars are checked live */
  var SOLID = { c: [], b: [], rb: [] };
  function solidBox(arr, x, z, fx, fz, lx, lz, hu, hv) {
    /* a box in an object's frame: forward (fx, fz), across (fz, -fx); (lx, lz) its centre in that frame */
    arr.push(x + fz * lx + fx * lz, z - fx * lx + fz * lz, fz, -fx, hu, hv);
  }
  function inBoxes(B, x, z, r) {
    for (var i = 0; i < B.length; i += 6) {
      var dx = x - B[i], dz = z - B[i + 1], u = dx * B[i + 2] + dz * B[i + 3], v = -dx * B[i + 3] + dz * B[i + 2];
      if (Math.abs(u) < B[i + 4] + r && Math.abs(v) < B[i + 5] + r) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function propHit(x, z, r, skipCar) {
    var C = SOLID.c;
    for (var i = 0; i < C.length; i += 3) { var dx = x - C[i], dz = z - C[i + 1], rr = C[i + 2] + r; if (dx * dx + dz * dz < rr * rr) return true; }
    if (inBoxes(SOLID.b, x, z, r) || inBoxes(SOLID.rb, x, z, r)) return true;
    var L = LIFE;
    if (L && L.cars) for (var k = 0; k < L.cars.length; k++) {
      var c = L.cars[k]; if (c === skipCar) continue;
      var T = CAR_TYPES[c.type], ex = x - c.x, ez = z - c.z;
      if (ex * ex + ez * ez > 64) continue;
      var u = ex * c.hx + ez * c.hz, v = -ex * c.hz + ez * c.hx;
      if (Math.abs(u) < T.L / 2 + r && Math.abs(v) < T.Wd / 2 + r) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function solidsRebuild(px, pz) {
    var L = LIFE, R = 140, i;
    SOLID.c.length = 0; SOLID.b.length = 0;
    (L.nearLamps || []).forEach(function (i) {
      var D = L.lamps.d, x = D[i * 8], z = D[i * 8 + 1];
      if (Math.abs(x - px) < R && Math.abs(z - pz) < R) SOLID.c.push(x, z, 0.2);
    });
    (L.nearSigs || []).forEach(function (si) {
      var sg = L.sigs[si]; if (Math.abs(sg.x - px) > R || Math.abs(sg.z - pz) > R) return;
      sg.arms.forEach(function (a) { armPole(sg, a, AP); SOLID.c.push(AP.x, AP.z, 0.2); });
    });
    var S = L.stops.d;
    (L.nearStops || []).forEach(function (i) {
      var x = S[i * 8], z = S[i * 8 + 1], fx = S[i * 8 + 2], fz = S[i * 8 + 3];
      if (Math.abs(x - px) > R || Math.abs(z - pz) > R) return;
      solidBox(SOLID.b, x, z, fx, fz, 0, -0.62, 1.8, 0.12);     /* the back panel */
      solidBox(SOLID.b, x, z, fx, fz, -0.4, -0.32, 1.2, 0.24);  /* the bench */
      solidBox(SOLID.b, x, z, fx, fz, 2.1, 0.75, 0.27, 0.08);   /* the timetable on its pole */
      [[-1.6, 0.55], [1.6, 0.55]].forEach(function (p) { var w = [x + fz * p[0] + fx * p[1], z - fx * p[0] + fz * p[1]]; SOLID.c.push(w[0], w[1], 0.08); });
    });
    var P = L.park.d;
    (L.parkedNear || []).forEach(function (e) {
      var x, z, fx, fz, type;
      if (typeof e === 'object') { x = e.x; z = e.z; fx = e.hx; fz = e.hz; type = e.type; }
      else {
        var r = hh(e, 1.7), side = P[e * 8 + 5]; type = (L.lala && L.lala[e]) ? 1 : r < 0.36 ? 0 : r < 0.74 ? 1 : 2;
        x = P[e * 8]; z = P[e * 8 + 1]; fx = P[e * 8 + 6] * side; fz = P[e * 8 + 7] * side;
      }
      if (Math.abs(x - px) > R || Math.abs(z - pz) > R) return;
      var T = CAR_TYPES[type];
      SOLID.b.push(x, z, fx, fz, T.L / 2, T.Wd / 2);
    });
  }

  function lifeRebuild(px, pz) {
    var L = LIFE, Q = QUAL || TIERS[1];
    /* lamps */
    var lm = L.lampMesh, a = lm.instanceMatrix.array, n = 0, D = L.lamps.d;
    L.nearLamps = [];
    eachNear(L.lamps, px, pz, Q.lampR, function (i, d) {
      if (n >= 1800) return;
      var x = D[i * 8], z = D[i * 8 + 1], y = groundAt(x, z) - 0.06;
      placeAt(a, n++, x, y, z, D[i * 8 + 2], D[i * 8 + 3], STREETS[D[i * 8 + 4]].A >= 12 ? 1.1 : 1);
      L.nearLamps.push(i);
    });
    lm.count = n; lm.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    var sgm = L.sigMesh, sga = sgm.instanceMatrix.array, nsg = 0; L.nearSigs = [];
    L.sigs.forEach(function (sg, si) {
      if (Math.abs(sg.x - px) > Q.lampR || Math.abs(sg.z - pz) > Q.lampR) return;
      sg.arms.forEach(function (a) { if (nsg >= 400) return; armPole(sg, a, AP); placeAt(sga, nsg++, AP.x, groundAt(AP.x, AP.z) - 0.06, AP.z, AP.fx, AP.fz); });
      L.nearSigs.push(si);
    });
    sgm.count = nsg; sgm.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    L.points.material.uniforms.uNear.value = Q.lampR * 0.9;
    /* stops */
    var sm = L.stopMesh, sa = sm.instanceMatrix.array, ga = L.stopGlass.instanceMatrix.array, aa = L.adMesh.instanceMatrix.array, ad = L.adMesh.geometry.attributes.aAd;
    var S = L.stops.d, ns = 0;
    L.nearStops = [];
    eachNear(L.stops, px, pz, Q.stopR, function (i) {
      if (ns >= 120) return;
      var x = S[i * 8], z = S[i * 8 + 1], y = groundAt(x, z) - 0.04;
      /* the shelter's open side (+z) faces the road */
      placeAt(sa, ns, x, y, z, S[i * 8 + 2], S[i * 8 + 3]); placeAt(ga, ns, x, y, z, S[i * 8 + 2], S[i * 8 + 3]); placeAt(aa, ns, x, y, z, S[i * 8 + 2], S[i * 8 + 3]);
      ad.array[ns] = Math.floor(hh(i, 4.4) * 4) % 4;
      L.nearStops.push(i); ns++;
    });
    [sm, L.stopGlass, L.adMesh].forEach(function (m) { m.count = ns; m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; });
    ad.needsUpdate = true;
    /* parked cars */
    L.parkedNear.length = 0;
    var P = L.park.d;
    eachNear(L.park, px, pz, Q.parkR, function (i) { if (!L.parkTaken[i]) L.parkedNear.push(i); });
    (L.left || []).forEach(function (c) { if (Math.hypot(c.x - px, c.z - pz) < Q.parkR) L.parkedNear.push(c); });
    try { solidsRebuild(px, pz); } catch (e) {}
    /* the ways near you, for traffic and people to use */
    L.nearWays = []; L.nearWalks = [];
    STREETS.forEach(function (w, wi) {
      if (!w.on) return;
      if (w.box[2] < px - 450 || w.box[0] > px + 450 || w.box[3] < pz - 450 || w.box[1] > pz + 450) return;
      if (w.A >= 4.5) L.nearWays.push(wi);
      if (w.SW >= 0.9 || w.A < 0.5) L.nearWalks.push(wi);
    });
    L.staticDirty = true;
  }

  function lifeStatic() {
    /* everything that glows and does not move: lamp heads, signs, stop ads */
    var L = LIFE, G = L.glowS, D = L.lamps.d, cx = pos.x, cz = pos.z, Q = QUAL || TIERS[1];
    G.n = 0;
    L.nearLamps.forEach(function (i) {
      var hx = L.lampHead[i * 3], hy = L.lampHead[i * 3 + 1], hz = L.lampHead[i * 3 + 2];
      var r = L.lampCol[i * 3], g = L.lampCol[i * 3 + 1], b = L.lampCol[i * 3 + 2];
      glowPut(G, hx, hy - 0.03, hz, r * 1.3, g * 1.3, b * 1.3, 0.48);
      glowPut(G, hx, hy - 0.35, hz, r * 0.15, g * 0.15, b * 0.15, 3.0);
    });
    L.signs.list.forEach(function (s) {
      glowPut(G, s.x, s.y, s.z, s.c[0] * 0.22, s.c[1] * 0.22, s.c[2] * 0.22, s.w * 0.75);
    });
    var SD = L.stops.d;
    L.nearStops.forEach(function (i) {
      var x = SD[i * 8], z = SD[i * 8 + 1], fx = SD[i * 8 + 2], fz = SD[i * 8 + 3], y = groundAt(x, z);
      var ax = x + fz * 1.0 + fx * 0.7, az = z - fx * 1.0 + fz * 0.7;
      glowPut(G, ax, y + 1.4, az, 0.16, 0.18, 0.22, 2.0);
    });
    (L.rewards ? L.rewards.list : []).forEach(function (r) {
      if (r.found || Math.hypot(r.x - cx, r.z - cz) > 90) return;
      glowPut(G, r.x, r.y + 1.2, r.z, 0.35, 0.3, 0.18, 1.6);
    });
    layerDone(G);
    L.staticDirty = false;
  }

  /* ---- traffic ---- */
  function spawnCar(c, px, pz, ringMin) {
    var L = LIFE;
    if (!L.nearWays.length) return false;
    for (var tries = 0; tries < 10; tries++) {
      var wi = L.nearWays[Math.floor(Math.random() * L.nearWays.length)], w = STREETS[wi];
      var s = Math.random() * w.L, n = w.pts.length / 2, i = 0;
      while (i < n - 2 && w.len[i + 1] < s) i++;
      var P = wayPoint(w, i, s - w.len[i], 0, {});
      var d = Math.hypot(P.x - px, P.z - pz);
      if (d < ringMin || d > (ringMin < 60 ? 230 : 330)) continue;
      var oneway = w.lanes < 0;
      c.w = wi; c.i = i; c.s = s - w.len[i]; c.dir = oneway ? 1 : (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
      var half = w.A / 2 - (w.park ? 2.1 : 0);
      c.off = oneway ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.max(0, half - 1.3) : Math.max(0.9, half / 2);
      c.type = (w.A >= 10 && Math.random() < 0.1) ? 3 : (Math.random() < 0.3 ? 0 : Math.random() < 0.6 ? 1 : 2);
      c.taxi = c.type === 0 && Math.random() < 0.7;
      c.col = c.type === 3 ? (Math.random() < 0.6 ? CARPAINT.sitp : CARPAINT.tm) : c.taxi ? CARPAINT.taxi : CARPAINT.std[Math.floor(Math.random() * CARPAINT.std.length)];
      c.vmax = (w.A >= 12 ? 13 : w.A >= 8 ? 10.5 : 7.5) * (0.85 + Math.random() * 0.3) * (c.type === 3 ? 0.8 : 1);
      c.v = c.vmax * 0.8; c.brake = 0; c.bump = 0; c.vr = Math.random();
      c.hx = P.dx * c.dir; c.hz = P.dz * c.dir; c.x = P.x - P.dz * c.off * c.dir; c.z = P.z + P.dx * c.off * c.dir;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function stepCar(c, dt) {
    var w = STREETS[c.w], n = w.pts.length / 2;
    c.s += c.v * dt * c.dir;
    var guard = 0;
    while (guard++ < 8) {
      var segL = w.len[c.i + 1] - w.len[c.i];
      if (c.s >= 0 && c.s <= segL) break;
      var atEnd = c.s > segL, vi = atEnd ? c.i + 1 : c.i;
      var over = atEnd ? c.s - segL : -c.s;
      var last = atEnd ? vi === n - 1 : vi === 0;
      var pv = atEnd ? vi - 1 : vi + 1;
      var hx = w.pts[vi * 2] - w.pts[pv * 2], hz = w.pts[vi * 2 + 1] - w.pts[pv * 2 + 1], hl = Math.hypot(hx, hz) || 1; hx /= hl; hz /= hl;
      var opts = [], tot = 0;
      if (!last) {
        var nj = vi + (atEnd ? 1 : -1), ox = w.pts[nj * 2] - w.pts[vi * 2], oz = w.pts[nj * 2 + 1] - w.pts[vi * 2 + 1], ol = Math.hypot(ox, oz) || 1;
        var dd = (ox * hx + oz * hz) / ol; opts.push([c.w, vi, c.dir, dd > 0.8 ? 5 : 2]); tot += opts[0][3];
      }
      var nl = LIFE.nodes[nkey(w.pts[vi * 2], w.pts[vi * 2 + 1])];
      if (nl) {
        for (var q = 0; q < nl.length; q += 2) {
          var ow = nl[q], oj = nl[q + 1], W2 = STREETS[ow];
          if (ow === c.w || W2.A < 4.5 || !W2.on) continue;
          var m = W2.pts.length / 2;
          for (var sd2 = -1; sd2 <= 1; sd2 += 2) {
            var tj = oj + sd2; if (tj < 0 || tj >= m) continue;
            if (sd2 < 0 && W2.lanes < 0) continue;
            var ex = W2.pts[tj * 2] - W2.pts[oj * 2], ez = W2.pts[tj * 2 + 1] - W2.pts[oj * 2 + 1], el = Math.hypot(ex, ez) || 1, d2 = (ex * hx + ez * hz) / el;
            if (d2 < -0.25) continue;
            var wt = d2 > 0.8 ? 4 : 1.2; opts.push([ow, oj, sd2, wt]); tot += wt;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!opts.length) return false;
      var rr = Math.random() * tot, pick = opts[0];
      for (var k = 0; k < opts.length; k++) { rr -= opts[k][3]; if (rr <= 0) { pick = opts[k]; break; } }
      if (pick[0] === c.w && pick[2] === c.dir) {
        c.i += atEnd ? 1 : -1;
        c.s = atEnd ? over : (w.len[c.i + 1] - w.len[c.i]) - over;
        continue;
      }
      var W3 = STREETS[pick[0]];
      c.w = pick[0]; w = W3; n = W3.pts.length / 2; c.dir = pick[2];
      c.i = c.dir > 0 ? pick[1] : pick[1] - 1;
      c.s = c.dir > 0 ? over : (W3.len[c.i + 1] - W3.len[c.i]) - over;
      var half = W3.A / 2 - (W3.park ? 2.1 : 0);
      c.off = W3.lanes < 0 ? Math.max(-half + 1.3, Math.min(half - 1.3, c.off)) : Math.max(0.9, half / 2);
      c.vmax = (W3.A >= 12 ? 13 : W3.A >= 8 ? 10.5 : 7.5) * (0.85 + (c.vr || 0.5) * 0.3) * (c.type === 3 ? 0.8 : 1);
    }
    var P = wayPoint(w, c.i, c.s, c.off * c.dir, TMP);
    var fx = P.dx * c.dir, fz = P.dz * c.dir;
    var kp = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 6), kh = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 5);
    var nx = c.x + (P.x - c.x) * kp, nz = c.z + (P.z - c.z) * kp;
    if (Math.hypot(P.x - c.x, P.z - c.z) > 12) { nx = P.x; nz = P.z; }
    c.hx += (fx - c.hx) * kh; c.hz += (fz - c.hz) * kh;
    var hl2 = Math.hypot(c.hx, c.hz) || 1; c.hx /= hl2; c.hz /= hl2;
    c.vx = (nx - c.x) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3); c.vz = (nz - c.z) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3);
    c.x = nx; c.z = nz;
    return true;
  }
  var TMP = {}, AP = {}, SGT = {};

  /* ---- people ---- */
  function spawnPed(p, px, pz, ringMin) {
    var L = LIFE;
    if (!L.nearWalks.length) return false;
    for (var tries = 0; tries < 10; tries++) {
      var wi = L.nearWalks[Math.floor(Math.random() * L.nearWalks.length)], w = STREETS[wi];
      var s = Math.random() * w.L, n = w.pts.length / 2, i = 0;
      while (i < n - 2 && w.len[i + 1] < s) i++;
      var side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
      var off = w.A < 0.5 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2 : (w.A / 2 + Math.max(0.5, w.SW * (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4))) * side;
      var P = wayPoint(w, i, s - w.len[i], off, {});
      var d = Math.hypot(P.x - px, P.z - pz);
      if (d < ringMin || d > (ringMin < 30 ? 120 : 175) || blocked(P.x, P.z)) continue;
      p.w = wi; p.i = i; p.s = s - w.len[i]; p.off = off; p.dir = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1;
      p.v = 1.05 + Math.random() * 0.6; p.x = P.x; p.z = P.z; p.hx = P.dx * p.dir; p.hz = P.dz * p.dir;
      p.mode = 0; p.dodge = 0; p.dx = 0; p.dz = 0;
      p.shirt = lin(SHIRTS[Math.floor(Math.random() * SHIRTS.length)]); p.pants = lin(PANTS[Math.floor(Math.random() * PANTS.length)]);
      p.ph = Math.random() * 6.28; p.sc = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function stepPed(p, dt) {
    var w = STREETS[p.w], n = w.pts.length / 2;
    p.s += p.v * dt * p.dir;
    var segL = w.len[p.i + 1] - w.len[p.i];
    if (p.s > segL) { if (p.i < n - 2) { p.s -= segL; p.i++; } else { p.dir = -1; p.s = segL; } }
    else if (p.s < 0) { if (p.i > 0) { p.i--; p.s += w.len[p.i + 1] - w.len[p.i]; } else { p.dir = 1; p.s = 0; } }
    var P = wayPoint(w, p.i, p.s, p.off, TMP);
    var k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 6);
    p.hx += (P.dx * p.dir - p.hx) * k; p.hz += (P.dz * p.dir - p.hz) * k;
    var hl = Math.hypot(p.hx, p.hz) || 1; p.hx /= hl; p.hz /= hl;
    p.x = P.x; p.z = P.z;
  }

  /* ---- the frame ---- */
  function stepLife(dt, t) {
    var L = LIFE; if (!L) return;
    var Q = QUAL || TIERS[1];
    L.t = t;
    var px = pos.x, pz = pos.z;
    if (Math.hypot(px - L.lastX, pz - L.lastZ) > (L.driving ? 60 : 30)) { L.lastX = px; L.lastZ = pz; lifeRebuild(px, pz); }
    if (L.staticDirty) lifeStatic();

    /* the beat: the music if the site is listening, otherwise 118 bpm */
    var A = window.LALA_AUDIO, beat;
    if (A && A.active && typeof A.kick === 'number') beat = Math.min(1, A.kick * 1.4 + (A.level || 0) * 0.3);
    else beat = Math.pow(Math.max(0, Math.cos(t * Math.PI * 2 * 118 / 60)), 10);
    L.beat += (beat - L.beat) * Math.min(1, dt * 18);
    L.pedMat.uniforms.uT.value = t; L.pedMat.uniforms.uBeat.value = L.beat;
    LIGHTU.tu.value = t;
    L.signs.mesh.material.uniforms.uT.value = t;

    /* traffic: keep the count, spawn out of sight, drop what falls behind */
    var want = Q.cars;
    for (var sa = 0; sa < 24 && L.cars.length < want; sa++) { var c = {}; if (spawnCar(c, px, pz, L.cars.length < want * 0.6 ? 25 : 150)) L.cars.push(c); }
    if (L.cars.length > want) L.cars.length = want;
    var me = L.me;
    for (var i = 0; i < L.cars.length; i++) {
      var c1 = L.cars[i];
      /* follow: slow behind whatever is in front, stop for a walker in the lane */
      var target = c1.vmax, fx = c1.hx, fz = c1.hz;
      for (var j = 0; j < L.cars.length; j++) {
        if (j === i) continue;
        var c2 = L.cars[j], rx = c2.x - c1.x, rz = c2.z - c1.z, ahead = rx * fx + rz * fz;
        if (ahead <= 0 || ahead > 16) continue;
        var lat = Math.abs(rx * fz - rz * fx);
        if (lat < 1.6 && fx * c2.hx + fz * c2.hz > 0.3) target = Math.min(target, Math.max(0, (ahead - 6) * 0.9));
      }
      var ox = (me ? me.x : px) - c1.x, oz = (me ? me.z : pz) - c1.z, oa = ox * fx + oz * fz;
      if (oa > 0 && oa < 14 && Math.abs(ox * fz - oz * fx) < 1.8) target = Math.min(target, Math.max(0, (oa - 5) * 0.8));
      var sgA = aheadSignal(c1, SGT);
      if (sgA) {
        var stA = sigState(sgA.s, c1.hx, c1.hz, t), sdA = sgA.d - (sgA.s.rad + 6.3);
        if (stA !== 2 && sdA > -1.0 && !(stA === 1 && sdA < c1.v * 0.9)) target = Math.min(target, Math.max(0, sdA * 0.85));
      }
      c1.brake = target < c1.v - 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
      c1.v += (target - c1.v) * Math.min(1, dt * (target < c1.v ? 3.5 : 0.8));
      if (c1.bump > 0) { c1.bump -= dt; c1.v *= 0.9; }
      var dcam = Math.hypot(c1.x - px, c1.z - pz);
      if (!stepCar(c1, dt) || dcam > 360) { if (!spawnCar(c1, px, pz, 150)) { L.cars.splice(i, 1); i--; } }
    }

    /* people */
    var wantP = Q.peds;
    for (var sp2 = 0; sp2 < 30 && L.peds.length < wantP; sp2++) { var p0 = {}; if (spawnPed(p0, px, pz, L.peds.length < wantP * 0.7 ? 6 : 80)) L.peds.push(p0); }
    if (L.peds.length > wantP) L.peds.length = wantP;
    for (var pi = 0; pi < L.peds.length; pi++) {
      var p = L.peds[pi];
      if (p.mode === 0) stepPed(p, dt);
      /* get out of the way of anything fast: a little jump to the side */
      var threat = me && Math.abs(me.v) > 4 ? me : null;
      if (threat && p.dodge <= 0 && Math.hypot(threat.x - p.x, threat.z - p.z) < 7) {
        var sx = -(threat.hz), sz = threat.hx, sgn = ((p.x - threat.x) * sx + (p.z - threat.z) * sz) >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
        p.ddx = sx * sgn * 3.2; p.ddz = sz * sgn * 3.2; p.dodge = 0.8;
      }
      if (p.dodge > 0) { p.dodge -= dt; p.dx += p.ddx * dt; p.dz += p.ddz * dt; }
      else { p.dx *= 1 - Math.min(1, dt * 0.8); p.dz *= 1 - Math.min(1, dt * 0.8); }
      if (Math.hypot(p.x - px, p.z - pz) > 190) { if (!spawnPed(p, px, pz, 80)) { L.peds.splice(pi, 1); pi--; } }
    }
    placeDancers();
    drawLife(dt);
    gatherLights();
    if (L.driving) stepDrive(dt); else nearCar();
    stepRewards(dt, t);

    /* the first seconds of walking: if they run slow, step the quality down */
    if (running && !L.slow.done) {
      L.slow.t += dt; L.slow.n++;
      if (L.slow.t > 6) { L.slow.done = true; if (L.slow.t / L.slow.n > 0.034) dropQuality(); }
    }
  }

  function placeDancers() {
    var L = LIFE;
    if (!L.dancersDirty) return;
    L.dancersDirty = false;
    L.dancers = [];
    L.signs.list.forEach(function (s, i) {
      if (L.dancers.length >= 14 || hh(s.x * 0.013, s.z * 0.017) > 0.3) return;
      var k = 1 + Math.floor(hh(i, 3) * 3);
      for (var q = 0; q < k; q++) {
        var along = (q - (k - 1) / 2) * 0.9, out = 1.6 + hh(i, q) * 0.8;
        var x = s.x + s.nx * (out - 0.6) + (-s.nz) * along, z = s.z + s.nz * (out - 0.6) + s.nx * along;
        if (blocked(x, z)) continue;
        L.dancers.push({ x: x, z: z, hx: -s.nx, hz: -s.nz, ph: hh(i, q + 7) * 6.28, sc: 0.92 + hh(q, i) * 0.16,
          shirt: lin(SHIRTS[Math.floor(hh(i + q, 2) * SHIRTS.length)]), pants: lin(PANTS[Math.floor(hh(q, i + 3) * PANTS.length)]), mode: 1 });
      }
    });
    (L.nearStops || []).forEach(function (i, k) {
      if (L.dancers.length >= 22 || hh(i, 8) > 0.6) return;
      var SD = L.stops.d, x = SD[i * 8] + SD[i * 8 + 3] * 0.4 - SD[i * 8 + 2] * 0.1, z = SD[i * 8 + 1] - SD[i * 8 + 2] * 0.4 - SD[i * 8 + 3] * 0.1;
      L.dancers.push({ x: x, z: z, hx: SD[i * 8 + 2], hz: SD[i * 8 + 3], ph: hh(i, 5) * 6.28, sc: 0.95, shirt: lin(SHIRTS[k % SHIRTS.length]), pants: lin(PANTS[k % PANTS.length]), mode: 3 });
    });
  }

  function drawLife(dt) {
    var L = LIFE, GD = L.glowD;
    GD.n = 0;
    var counts = [0, 0, 0, 0], ts = 0, cm = L.carMeshes, nd = 0;
    function decal(type) {
      var m = cm[type], i = counts[type] - 1; if (i < 0 || nd >= 64) return;
      var da = L.lalaDecal.instanceMatrix.array, so = i * 16, to = (nd++) * 16;
      for (var k = 0; k < 16; k++) da[to + k] = m.instanceMatrix.array[so + k];
    }
    function car(type, x, z, fx, fz, col, taxi, roll) {
      var m = cm[type], i = counts[type];
      if (i >= m.cap) return;
      var T = CAR_TYPES[type], y = placeTilt(m.instanceMatrix.array, i, x, -0.02, z, fx, fz, T.L / 2 - (T.k === 'bus' ? 2.0 : 0.78), T.Wd / 2 - 0.1);
      m.instanceColor.array[i * 3] = col.r; m.instanceColor.array[i * 3 + 1] = col.g; m.instanceColor.array[i * 3 + 2] = col.b;
      counts[type]++;
      if (taxi && ts < 300) { var ta = L.taxiSign.instanceMatrix.array, so = i * 16, to = (ts++) * 16; for (var k = 0; k < 16; k++) ta[to + k] = m.instanceMatrix.array[so + k]; }
      return y;
    }
    /* parked */
    var P = L.park.d;
    for (var q = 0; q < L.parkedNear.length; q++) {
      var e = L.parkedNear[q];
      if (typeof e === 'object') { car(e.type, e.x, e.z, e.hx, e.hz, e.col, e.taxi); if (e.lala) decal(e.type); continue; }
      var r = hh(e, 1.7), type = r < 0.36 ? 0 : r < 0.74 ? 1 : 2, taxi = type === 0 && hh(e, 2.9) < 0.5;
      var side = P[e * 8 + 5], fx = P[e * 8 + 6] * side, fz = P[e * 8 + 7] * side;
      var col = taxi ? CARPAINT.taxi : CARPAINT.std[Math.floor(hh(e, 6.1) * CARPAINT.std.length)];
      if (L.lala && L.lala[e]) { type = 1; taxi = false; col = CARPAINT.lala; }
      car(type, P[e * 8], P[e * 8 + 1], fx, fz, col, taxi);
      if (L.lala && L.lala[e]) decal(1);
    }
    /* moving */
    var cx = pos.x, cz = pos.z;
    L.cars.forEach(function (c) {
      var T = CAR_TYPES[c.type], y = car(c.type, c.x, c.z, c.hx, c.hz, c.col, c.taxi);
      if (y == null) return;
      var d = Math.hypot(c.x - cx, c.z - cz); if (d > 420) return;
      var sx = -c.hz, sz = c.hx, fl = T.L / 2;
      var hb = c.type === 3 ? 0.9 : 0.72, tb = c.type === 3 ? 1.0 : 0.82;
      for (var s = -1; s <= 1; s += 2) {
        var ox = sx * s * (T.Wd / 2 - 0.26), oz = sz * s * (T.Wd / 2 - 0.26);
        glowPut(GD, c.x + c.hx * (fl + 0.05) + ox, y + hb, c.z + c.hz * (fl + 0.05) + oz, 1.5, 1.38, 1.15, 0.42);
        var tr = c.brake ? 1.9 : 1.05;
        glowPut(GD, c.x - c.hx * (fl + 0.05) + ox, y + tb, c.z - c.hz * (fl + 0.05) + oz, tr, 0.07, 0.09, 0.32, -c.vx * 0.22, 0, -c.vz * 0.22);
      }
    });
    /* the signals: the lit lamp of each head, seen from the front */
    var cpx = cam.position.x, cpz = cam.position.z;
    (L.nearSigs || []).forEach(function (si) {
      var sg = L.sigs[si]; if (Math.hypot(sg.x - cpx, sg.z - cpz) > 260) return;
      sg.arms.forEach(function (a) {
        armPole(sg, a, AP);
        if ((cpx - AP.x) * a.dx + (cpz - AP.z) * a.dz < 0) return;
        var st = sigState(sg, -a.dx, -a.dz, L.t), gy = groundAt(AP.x, AP.z) - 0.06;
        var col = st === 2 ? [0.15, 1.5, 0.75] : st === 1 ? [1.7, 0.85, 0.05] : [1.8, 0.07, 0.05], dy = st === 2 ? -0.36 : st === 1 ? 0 : 0.36;
        var Xx = a.dz, Xz = -a.dx;
        glowPut(GD, AP.x + Xx * -3.2 + a.dx * 0.2, gy + 4.95 + dy, AP.z + Xz * -3.2 + a.dz * 0.2, col[0], col[1], col[2], 0.34);
        glowPut(GD, AP.x + Xx * -3.2 + a.dx * 0.2, gy + 4.95 + dy, AP.z + Xz * -3.2 + a.dz * 0.2, col[0] * 0.12, col[1] * 0.12, col[2] * 0.12, 1.6);
        glowPut(GD, AP.x + a.dx * 0.4, gy + 3.1 + dy * 0.89, AP.z + a.dz * 0.4, col[0] * 0.8, col[1] * 0.8, col[2] * 0.8, 0.26);
      });
    });
    /* yours */
    if (L.me) {
      var me = L.me;
      car(me.type, me.x, me.z, Math.sin(me.h), Math.cos(me.h), me.col, me.taxi);
      if (me.lala) decal(me.type);
      var T2 = CAR_TYPES[me.type], hx = Math.sin(me.h), hz = Math.cos(me.h), y2 = groundAt(me.x, me.z);
      if (L.driving) {
        for (var s2 = -1; s2 <= 1; s2 += 2) {
          var o2x = -hz * s2 * (T2.Wd / 2 - 0.26), o2z = hx * s2 * (T2.Wd / 2 - 0.26);
          glowPut(GD, me.x + hx * (T2.L / 2 + 0.05) + o2x, y2 + 0.72, me.z + hz * (T2.L / 2 + 0.05) + o2z, 1.6, 1.5, 1.25, 0.45);
          glowPut(GD, me.x - hx * (T2.L / 2 + 0.05) + o2x, y2 + 0.82, me.z - hz * (T2.L / 2 + 0.05) + o2z, me.braking ? 2.0 : 1.1, 0.06, 0.08, 0.34, -me.vx * 0.22, 0, -me.vz * 0.22);
        }
      }
    }
    cm.forEach(function (m, k) { m.count = counts[k]; m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; m.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true; });
    L.taxiSign.count = ts; L.taxiSign.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    L.lalaDecal.count = nd; L.lalaDecal.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    for (var tsi = 0; tsi < ts; tsi++) { var a2 = L.taxiSign.instanceMatrix.array, o = tsi * 16; glowPut(GD, a2[o + 12], a2[o + 13] + 1.56, a2[o + 14], 0.3, 0.26, 0.12, 0.5); }

    /* people */
    var pm = L.pedMesh, pa = pm.instanceMatrix.array, at = pm.geometry.attributes, np = 0;
    function person(x, z, hx, hz, shirt, pants, ph, speed, mode, sc) {
      if (np >= 260) return;
      placeAt(pa, np, x, groundAt(x, z) - 0.015, z, hx, hz);
      at.iShirt.array[np * 3] = shirt.r; at.iShirt.array[np * 3 + 1] = shirt.g; at.iShirt.array[np * 3 + 2] = shirt.b;
      at.iPants.array[np * 3] = pants.r; at.iPants.array[np * 3 + 1] = pants.g; at.iPants.array[np * 3 + 2] = pants.b;
      at.iAnim.array[np * 4] = ph; at.iAnim.array[np * 4 + 1] = speed; at.iAnim.array[np * 4 + 2] = mode; at.iAnim.array[np * 4 + 3] = sc;
      np++;
    }
    L.peds.forEach(function (p) {
      person(p.x + p.dx, p.z + p.dz, p.hx, p.hz, p.shirt, p.pants, p.ph, p.dodge > 0 ? 0 : p.v * 0.62, p.dodge > 0 ? 2 : 0, p.sc);
    });
    (L.dancers || []).forEach(function (d) { person(d.x, d.z, d.hx, d.hz, d.shirt, d.pants, d.ph, 118 / 60 / 2, d.mode, d.sc); });
    pm.count = np; pm.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    at.iShirt.needsUpdate = true; at.iPants.needsUpdate = true; at.iAnim.needsUpdate = true;

    layerDone(GD);
  }

  /* the nearest lights, for the street shader */
  var LCAND = [];
  function gatherLights() {
    var L = LIFE, cx = cam.position.x, cz = cam.position.z, n = 0;
    LCAND.length = 0;
    function add(x, y, z, r, g, b, rad, w) { var d = Math.hypot(x - cx, z - cz); if (d < 160) LCAND.push([d * (w || 1), x, y, z, r, g, b, rad]); }
    (L.nearLamps || []).forEach(function (i) { add(L.lampHead[i * 3], L.lampHead[i * 3 + 1], L.lampHead[i * 3 + 2], L.lampCol[i * 3] * 0.32, L.lampCol[i * 3 + 1] * 0.32, L.lampCol[i * 3 + 2] * 0.32, 7.5); });
    L.signs.list.forEach(function (s) { add(s.x, s.y, s.z, s.c[0] * 0.35, s.c[1] * 0.35, s.c[2] * 0.35, 3.6, 0.8); });
    var me = L.me;
    L.cars.concat(me && L.driving ? [me] : []).forEach(function (c) {
      var hx = c.hx, hz = c.hz, T = CAR_TYPES[c.type], y = groundAt(c.x, c.z);
      add(c.x + hx * (T.L / 2 + 5), y + 0.8, c.z + hz * (T.L / 2 + 5), 0.28, 0.26, 0.21, 5.5, 1.3);
      add(c.x - hx * (T.L / 2 + 0.2), y + 0.8, c.z - hz * (T.L / 2 + 0.2), c.brake || c.braking ? 0.5 : 0.24, 0.02, 0.03, 1.6, 1.3);
    });
    LCAND.sort(function (a, b) { return a[0] - b[0]; });
    var cap = Math.min(NL, QUAL && QUAL.tier === 0 ? 14 : NL);
    for (var k = 0; k < cap && k < LCAND.length; k++) {
      var c = LCAND[k];
      LIGHTU.p.value[k].set(c[1], c[2], c[3], 0); LIGHTU.c.value[k].set(c[4], c[5], c[6], c[7]); n++;
    }
    LIGHTU.n.value = n;
  }

  /* ======================= the car you drive ======================= */
  function nearCar() {
    var L = LIFE, best = null, bd = 3.6;
    if (!running) { L.canDrive = null; showDrive(false); return; }
    var P = L.park.d;
    L.parkedNear.forEach(function (e) {
      var x, z;
      if (typeof e === 'object') { if (!e.lala) return; x = e.x; z = e.z; }
      else { if (!L.lala || !L.lala[e]) return; x = P[e * 8]; z = P[e * 8 + 1]; }
      var d = Math.hypot(x - pos.x, z - pos.z);
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = e; }
    });
    L.canDrive = best;
    showDrive(!!best);
  }
  function enterCar() {
    var L = LIFE, e = L.canDrive; if (e == null) return;
    var me;
    if (typeof e === 'object') {
      me = { type: e.type, col: e.col, taxi: e.taxi, lala: e.lala, x: e.x, z: e.z, h: Math.atan2(e.hx, e.hz) };
      L.left.splice(L.left.indexOf(e), 1);
    } else {
      var P = L.park.d, r = hh(e, 1.7), type = r < 0.36 ? 0 : r < 0.74 ? 1 : 2, taxi = type === 0 && hh(e, 2.9) < 0.5;
      var side = P[e * 8 + 5];
      me = { type: type, taxi: taxi, col: taxi ? CARPAINT.taxi : CARPAINT.std[Math.floor(hh(e, 6.1) * CARPAINT.std.length)],
             x: P[e * 8], z: P[e * 8 + 1], h: Math.atan2(P[e * 8 + 6] * side, P[e * 8 + 7] * side) };
      if (L.lala && L.lala[e]) { me.type = 1; me.taxi = false; me.col = CARPAINT.lala; me.lala = 1; }
      L.parkTaken[e] = 1;
    }
    me.v = 0; me.vx = 0; me.vz = 0; me.steer = 0; me.shake = 0; me.roll = 0; me.pitch = 0;
    me.hx = Math.sin(me.h); me.hz = Math.cos(me.h);
    L.me = me; L.driving = true; L.canDrive = null;
    /* the car you got into is no longer an obstacle */
    for (var sb = SOLID.b.length - 6; sb >= 0; sb -= 6) if (Math.hypot(SOLID.b[sb] - me.x, SOLID.b[sb + 1] - me.z) < 1.5) SOLID.b.splice(sb, 6);
    L.camP = null; L.fov = cam.fov;
    L.lastX = 1e9;
    lifeRebuild(me.x, me.z);
    showDrive(false); setSpeedo(true); root.classList.add('ct-driving');
  }
  function exitCar() {
    var L = LIFE, me = L.me; if (!me) return;
    var hx = Math.sin(me.h), hz = Math.cos(me.h), sx = -hz, sz = hx;
    var spots = [[-sx * 2.4, -sz * 2.4], [sx * 2.4, sz * 2.4], [-hx * 4, -hz * 4], [hx * 4, hz * 4]];
    for (var k = 0; k < spots.length; k++) {
      var x = me.x + spots[k][0], z = me.z + spots[k][1];
      if (!blocked(x, z)) { pos.x = x; pos.z = z; break; }
    }
    pos.y = groundAt(pos.x, pos.z) + CITY_CONFIG.EYE; vel.set(0, 0, 0);
    yaw = me.h + Math.PI; pitch = -0.05;
    L.left = L.left || [];
    L.left.push({ type: me.type, col: me.col, taxi: me.taxi, lala: me.lala, x: me.x, z: me.z, hx: hx, hz: hz });
    L.me = null; L.driving = false;
    cam.fov = 72; cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    L.lastX = 1e9; setSpeedo(false); root.classList.remove('ct-driving');
  }
  function carHits(x, z, hx, hz, T) {
    var sx = -hz, sz = hx, fl = T.L / 2 - 0.2, hw = T.Wd / 2 - 0.1;
    var pts = [[fl, hw], [fl, -hw], [-fl, hw], [-fl, -hw], [fl + 0.15, 0]];
    for (var k = 0; k < pts.length; k++) {
      var qx = x + hx * pts[k][0] + sx * pts[k][1], qz = z + hz * pts[k][0] + sz * pts[k][1];
      if (blocked(qx, qz) || propHit(qx, qz, 0.05, LIFE && LIFE.me)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function stepDrive(dt) {
    var L = LIFE, me = L.me, T = CAR_TYPES[me.type];
    var thr = 0, brk = 0, st = 0;
    if (!L.ui) {
      if (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp']) thr = 1;
      if (keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown']) brk = 1;
      if (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft']) st += 1;
      if (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight']) st -= 1;
      if (stickId !== null) { thr = Math.max(thr, -stickVec.y); if (stickVec.y > 0.2) brk = 1; st -= stickVec.x; }
    }
    var hand = !L.ui && keys['Space'];
    var VMAX = LIFEX.TOP_SPEED || 46;
    if (thr > 0) me.v += (me.v < 0 ? 22 : 10.5 * (1 - Math.max(0, me.v) / VMAX)) * thr * dt;
    if (brk) me.v += (me.v > 0.5 ? -22 : -7) * dt;
    if (!thr && !brk) me.v -= Math.sign(me.v) * Math.min(Math.abs(me.v), (1.6 + Math.abs(me.v) * 0.12) * dt);
    me.v = Math.max(-11, Math.min(VMAX, me.v));
    me.braking = brk && me.v > 0.5;
    me.steer += (st - me.steer) * Math.min(1, dt * 7);
    var rate = me.steer * (me.v / 3.1) * (0.9 / (1 + Math.abs(me.v) / 20));
    if (hand) { rate *= 1.75; me.v *= 1 - Math.min(1, dt * 0.9); }
    me.h += rate * dt;
    var hx = Math.sin(me.h), hz = Math.cos(me.h);
    var grip = hand ? 1.6 : 8.5, kk = Math.min(1, grip * dt);
    me.vx += (hx * me.v - me.vx) * kk; me.vz += (hz * me.v - me.vz) * kk;
    var nx = me.x + me.vx * dt, nz = me.z + me.vz * dt;
    if (carHits(nx, nz, hx, hz, T)) {
      if (!carHits(nx, me.z, hx, hz, T)) { me.x = nx; me.vz *= -0.2; }
      else if (!carHits(me.x, nz, hx, hz, T)) { me.z = nz; me.vx *= -0.2; }
      else { me.vx *= -0.3; me.vz *= -0.3; }
      me.v *= -0.25; me.shake = Math.min(0.6, me.shake + 0.4);
    } else { me.x = nx; me.z = nz; }
    /* parked and moving cars push back */
    var P = L.park.d, R = (T.L + 4.3) / 4;
    for (var q = 0; q < L.parkedNear.length; q++) {
      var e = L.parkedNear[q], ex = typeof e === 'object' ? e.x : P[e * 8], ez = typeof e === 'object' ? e.z : P[e * 8 + 1];
      var dx = me.x - ex, dz = me.z - ez, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (d < R * 1.05 && d > 0.01) { me.x += dx / d * (R * 1.05 - d); me.z += dz / d * (R * 1.05 - d); me.v *= 0.55; me.shake = Math.min(0.6, me.shake + 0.25); }
    }
    L.cars.forEach(function (c) {
      var dx = me.x - c.x, dz = me.z - c.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz), RR = (T.L + CAR_TYPES[c.type].L) / 4 + 0.4;
      if (d < RR && d > 0.01) { me.x += dx / d * (RR - d) * 0.6; me.z += dz / d * (RR - d) * 0.6; c.bump = 1.2; me.v *= 0.6; me.shake = Math.min(0.6, me.shake + 0.3); }
    });
    me.hx = hx; me.hz = hz;
    me.roll += ((-rate * me.v * 0.004) - me.roll) * Math.min(1, dt * 6);
    pos.x = me.x; pos.z = me.z; pos.y = groundAt(me.x, me.z) + CITY_CONFIG.EYE;
    setSpeedo(true, Math.abs(me.v) * 3.6);
  }
  /* the chase camera, after the walker's camera has been set: it overrides it */
  function driveCamera(dt) {
    var L = LIFE, me = L.me; if (!me || !L.driving) return;
    var hx = Math.sin(me.h), hz = Math.cos(me.h), gy = groundAt(me.x, me.z);
    var back = 6.6 + Math.min(3, Math.abs(me.v) * 0.05), up = 2.35;
    var tx = me.x - hx * back, tz = me.z - hz * back, ty = Math.max(gy + up, groundAt(tx, tz) + 1.4);
    if (!L.camP) L.camP = new THREE.Vector3(tx, ty, tz);
    var k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 7);
    L.camP.x += (tx - L.camP.x) * k; L.camP.y += (ty - L.camP.y) * k; L.camP.z += (tz - L.camP.z) * k;
    me.shake *= Math.exp(-dt * 6);
    cam.position.set(L.camP.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * me.shake * 0.3, L.camP.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * me.shake * 0.3, L.camP.z);
    cam.up.set(Math.cos(me.h) * me.roll, 1, -Math.sin(me.h) * me.roll).normalize();
    cam.lookAt(me.x + hx * 5, gy + 1.15, me.z + hz * 5);
    var f = 70 + Math.min(22, Math.abs(me.v) * 0.42);
    L.fov += (f - L.fov) * Math.min(1, dt * 3);
    if (Math.abs(cam.fov - L.fov) > 0.05) { cam.fov = L.fov; cam.updateProjectionMatrix(); }
    cam.up.set(0, 1, 0);
  }

  /* ======================= the finds ======================= */
  function buildRewards(spawn, group) {
    var R = { list: [], meshes: [] };
    var cfg = LIFEX.REWARDS || [];
    if (!cfg.length) return R;
    var found = {};
    try { (JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('lala-city-found') || '[]') || []).forEach(function (id) { found[id] = 1; }); } catch (e) {}
    /* spots: on pavements, 140 - 1500 m from where you land, never two within 220 m */
    var cands = [];
    STREETS.forEach(function (w, wi) {
      if (!w.on || w.L < 30) return;
      for (var k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
        var s = w.L * (0.2 + 0.6 * hh(wi, k + 0.5)), n = w.pts.length / 2, i = 0;
        while (i < n - 2 && w.len[i + 1] < s) i++;
        var side = hh(wi, k + 9) < 0.5 ? 1 : -1, off = w.A < 0.5 ? 0 : (w.A / 2 + Math.max(0.6, w.SW * 0.5)) * side;
        var P = wayPoint(w, i, s - w.len[i], off, {});
        var d = Math.hypot(P.x - spawn.x, P.z - spawn.z);
        if (d < 140 || d > 1500) continue;
        if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at(P.x, P.z) < 0.7) continue;
        if (blocked(P.x, P.z)) continue;
        cands.push({ x: P.x, z: P.z, quiet: w.A < 7 ? 1 : 0, r: hh(P.x * 0.01, P.z * 0.01) });
      }
    });
    cands.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.quiet - a.quiet) || (a.r - b.r); });
    var spots = [];
    cfg.forEach(function (r, k) {
      if (r.at) { spots.push({ x: r.at.x, z: r.at.z }); return; }
      for (var q = 0; q < cands.length; q++) {
        var c = cands[q], ok = true;
        for (var j = 0; j < spots.length; j++) if (Math.hypot(spots[j].x - c.x, spots[j].z - c.z) < 220) { ok = false; break; }
        /* spread them round the compass from the landing */
        if (ok) { var bear = Math.atan2(c.z - spawn.z, c.x - spawn.x), want = (k / cfg.length) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI; if (Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(bear - want), Math.cos(bear - want))) > 1.2) ok = false; }
        if (ok) { spots.push(c); cands.splice(q, 1); return; }
      }
      if (cands.length) spots.push(cands.shift());
    });
    var geo = new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.42, 0);
    cfg.forEach(function (r, k) {
      var s = spots[k]; if (!s) return;
      var id = r.id || ('find-' + (k + 1));
      var y = groundAt(s.x, s.z) + 0.1;
      var m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: new THREE.Color(r.kind === 'print' ? 0xCDEDFB : CHAMPAGNE) }));
      var wire = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.62, 0), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, wireframe: true, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 }));
      m.add(wire);
      m.position.set(s.x, y + 1.25, s.z);
      m.visible = !found[id];
      group.add(m);
      R.list.push({ id: id, cfg: r, x: s.x, z: s.z, y: y, mesh: m, found: !!found[id] });
    });
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] finds at ' + R.list.map(function (r) { return Math.round(r.x) + ',' + Math.round(r.z); }).join(' / '));
    return R;
  }
  function stepRewards(dt, t) {
    var R = LIFE.rewards; if (!R || !R.list.length) return;
    var reach = LIFE.driving ? 4.5 : 2.2;
    R.list.forEach(function (r, k) {
      if (r.found) return;
      var d = Math.hypot(r.x - pos.x, r.z - pos.z);
      r.mesh.visible = d < 60;
      if (!r.mesh.visible) return;
      r.mesh.rotation.y = t * 1.3 + k; r.mesh.position.y = r.y + 1.25 + Math.sin(t * 2 + k) * 0.12;
      if (d < reach && running) foundReward(r);
    });
  }
  function foundReward(r) {
    r.found = true; r.mesh.visible = false; LIFE.staticDirty = true;
    var ids = LIFE.rewards.list.filter(function (x) { return x.found; }).map(function (x) { return x.id; });
    try { localStorage.setItem('lala-city-found', JSON.stringify(ids)); } catch (e) {}
    showCard(r, ids.length, LIFE.rewards.list.length);
  }

  /* ======================= the screen ======================= */
  var elDrive = document.getElementById('ct-drive'), elSpeed = document.getElementById('ct-speed'), elSpeedN = document.getElementById('ct-speed-n');
  var elCard = document.getElementById('ct-card'), elFound = document.getElementById('ct-found');
  function showDrive(on) { if (elDrive) elDrive.hidden = true; }
  function setSpeedo(on, kmh) {
    if (!elSpeed) return;
    elSpeed.hidden = !on;
    if (on && kmh != null && elSpeedN) elSpeedN.textContent = String(Math.round(kmh));
  }
  function showCard(r, n, of) {
    if (!elCard) return;
    var c = r.cfg, isPrint = c.kind === 'print';
    elCard.querySelector('.k').textContent = 'found · ' + n + ' / ' + of;
    elCard.querySelector('.t').textContent = c.title || (isPrint ? 'A free print' : 'FYMA');
    elCard.querySelector('.d').textContent = c.text || '';
    var code = elCard.querySelector('.code'), img = elCard.querySelector('img'), dl = elCard.querySelector('.dl'), go = elCard.querySelector('.go');
    code.hidden = !c.code; if (c.code) code.querySelector('b').textContent = c.code;
    img.hidden = !c.preview; if (c.preview) img.src = c.preview;
    dl.hidden = !c.file; if (c.file) dl.href = c.file;
    go.hidden = !c.link; if (c.link) { go.href = c.link; go.textContent = c.linkLabel || 'open'; }
    elCard.hidden = false; LIFE.ui = true;
    if (document.exitPointerLock) document.exitPointerLock();
    hadLock = false;
  }
  function hideCard() { if (elCard) elCard.hidden = true; if (LIFE) LIFE.ui = false; }
  function lifeHint(hint) {
    var R = LIFE && LIFE.rewards; if (!R || !R.list.length) return hint;
    var f = R.list.filter(function (r) { return r.found; }).length;
    return hint + '  ·  ' + f + ' / ' + R.list.length + ' found';
  }
  function wireLife() {
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (!LIFE || !running) return;
      if (e.code === 'KeyF' || (e.code === 'KeyE' && !near)) {
        if (LIFE.driving) exitCar(); else if (LIFE.canDrive != null) enterCar();
      }
      if (e.code === 'Escape' && LIFE.ui) { hideCard(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); }
    });
    if (elDrive) { elDrive.style.pointerEvents = 'auto'; elDrive.addEventListener('click', function () { if (LIFE.canDrive != null) enterCar(); }); }
    if (elSpeed) { elSpeed.style.pointerEvents = 'auto'; elSpeed.addEventListener('click', function () { if (LIFE.driving) exitCar(); }); }
    if (elCard) {
      elCard.querySelector('.x').addEventListener('click', hideCard);
      elCard.querySelector('.cp').addEventListener('click', function () {
        var t = elCard.querySelector('.code b').textContent, btn = this;
        var ok = function () { btn.textContent = 'copied'; setTimeout(function () { btn.textContent = 'copy'; }, 1600); };
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(ok, function () {});
        else { var r = document.createRange(); r.selectNodeContents(elCard.querySelector('.code b')); var s = window.getSelection(); s.removeAllRanges(); s.addRange(r); }
      });
    }
  }


  /* ================= start ================= */
  /* ======================= MIDNIGHT CLUB PASS (v5) =======================
     v5.1 GROUNDED: groundAt reads the drawn plate, cars tilt with the street,
     feet, wheels and poles on the ground. */
  /* ===================================================================
     THE FACADES   every window is drawn inside the wall itself: a frame, a
                   sill, the glass set back in its opening, a room behind it
                   with walls, floor, ceiling and a lamp, blinds and curtains,
                   iron bars on the low floors; shopfronts on the street with
                   lit shelves, signs over them and roller shutters. Nothing
                   floats in front of the wall, so nothing can clash with it.
     THE GROUND    the streets, the pavements, the kerbs, the paint and the
                   zebras are one surface: drawn into a picture around you and
                   laid onto the ground itself - no strips over the plate.
     THE SIGNALS   traffic lights at the main junctions, a cycle each, and the
                   traffic stops at the line on red.
     ================================================================= */

  /* ---- the facade, in the wall shader ---- */
  var YB0U = { value: 2400 };
  var FACADE_GLSL = [
    'uniform float uYB0;',
    'vec3 skyRefl(vec3 rd){',
    '  vec3 c = mix(vec3(0.075, 0.045, 0.12), vec3(0.010, 0.016, 0.040), smoothstep(-0.05, 0.55, rd.y));',
    '  c += vec3(0.95, 0.42, 0.55) * exp(-abs(rd.y + 0.03) * 10.0) * 0.22;',
    '  c += vec3(1.0, 0.62, 0.30) * exp(-abs(rd.y - 0.01) * 26.0) * 0.12;',
    '  return c;',
    '}',
    'float inR(vec2 p, vec2 a, vec2 b){ return step(a.x, p.x) * step(p.x, b.x) * step(a.y, p.y) * step(p.y, b.y); }',
    /* a room behind the glass, in wall space: x along the wall, y up, z out of it */
    'vec3 roomCol(vec3 o, vec3 d, vec2 rx, vec2 ry, float D, float sd, float lit, vec3 glow, float shop){',
    '  float tx = d.x > 0.0 ? (rx.y - o.x) / max(d.x, 1e-4) : (rx.x - o.x) / min(d.x, -1e-4);',
    '  float ty = d.y > 0.0 ? (ry.y - o.y) / max(d.y, 1e-4) : (ry.x - o.y) / min(d.y, -1e-4);',
    '  float tz = (-D - o.z) / min(d.z, -1e-4);',
    '  float t = min(tx, min(ty, tz));',
    '  vec3 h = o + d * t;',
    '  float mid = (rx.x + rx.y) * 0.5, wv = rx.y - rx.x;',
    '  vec3 wallc = mix(vec3(0.62, 0.55, 0.44), vec3(0.42, 0.47, 0.55), h21(vec2(sd, 1.0))) * (0.75 + 0.45 * h21(vec2(sd, 2.0)));',
    '  if (shop > 0.5) wallc = vec3(0.86, 0.87, 0.85);',
    '  vec3 c;',
    '  if (t == tz) {',
    '    c = wallc;',
    '    float fy = h.y - ry.x;',
    '    if (shop > 0.5) {',
    /* shelves: rows of goods with a white edge, a counter in front */
    '      float row = floor(fy / 0.42), sh = fract(fy / 0.42);',
    '      vec3 goods = vec3(h21(vec2(floor(h.x / 0.28), row + sd * 9.0)), h21(vec2(floor(h.x / 0.28) + 3.1, row)), h21(vec2(row, floor(h.x / 0.28) + 7.7)));',
    '      c = fy < 2.1 ? mix(goods * 0.8 + 0.1, vec3(0.92), step(0.84, sh)) : wallc;',
    '    } else {',
    '      float furn = step(fy, 0.75 + 0.35 * h21(vec2(sd, 3.0))) * step(abs(h.x - mid - (h21(vec2(sd, 4.0)) - 0.5) * wv * 0.4), wv * 0.28);',
    '      c = mix(c, mix(vec3(0.10, 0.06, 0.05), vec3(0.20, 0.22, 0.26), h21(vec2(sd, 5.0))), furn);',
    '      float pic = inR(vec2(h.x - mid, fy), vec2(-0.35, 1.35), vec2(0.35, 1.85)) * step(0.45, h21(vec2(sd, 6.0)));',
    '      c = mix(c, vec3(h21(vec2(sd, 7.0)), h21(vec2(sd, 8.0)), 0.5) * 0.6, pic);',
    '    }',
    '  } else if (t == ty) {',
    '    c = d.y > 0.0 ? vec3(0.80, 0.80, 0.78) : mix(vec3(0.22, 0.14, 0.08), vec3(0.34, 0.33, 0.31), h21(vec2(sd, 9.0)));',
    '  } else {',
    '    c = wallc * 0.82;',
    '  }',
    /* one lamp under the ceiling, mid-room */
    '  vec3 lp = vec3(mid, ry.y - 0.25, -D * 0.5);',
    '  float L = 1.0 / (1.0 + dot(h - lp, h - lp) * 0.45);',
    '  vec3 lit3 = c * glow * (0.30 + 1.6 * L);',
    '  vec3 dark = c * vec3(0.010, 0.013, 0.024);',
    /* a television on in some of the dark rooms */
    '  if (shop < 0.5 && h21(vec2(sd, 11.0)) < 0.12) dark += vec3(0.08, 0.12, 0.22) * (0.6 + 0.4 * h21(vec2(floor(uT * 6.0), sd))) * L;',
    '  return mix(dark, lit3, lit);',
    '}',
    'void facade(float cls, float pal, float sd, float bseed, vec3 P, vec3 Pl, vec3 nW, float yb, out vec3 alb, out vec3 em){',
    '  vec2 t2 = normalize(vec2(-nW.z, nW.x) + 1e-6);',
    /* along the wall in exact numbers: a world position times a direction that is a hair off is metres off out here */
    '  float u = abs(nW.x) > abs(nW.z) ? Pl.z * sign(nW.x) : -Pl.x * sign(nW.z);',
    '  float hy = P.y - yb;',
    '  vec3 wallc = bogWall(cls, pal, sd, u, P.y);',
    '  float r1 = h21(vec2(bseed * 13.1, 1.7)), r2 = h21(vec2(bseed * 7.7, 4.1)), r3 = h21(vec2(bseed * 3.3, 8.9));',
    '  float bay = 2.7 + 0.9 * r1;',
    '  float ww, y0, y1; vec3 frameC;',
    '  if (cls < 0.5)      { ww = bay * (0.44 + 0.16 * r2); y0 = 0.95; y1 = 2.35; frameC = mix(vec3(0.80, 0.80, 0.78), vec3(0.10, 0.10, 0.11), step(0.6, r3)); }',
    '  else if (cls < 1.5) { ww = bay * (0.34 + 0.08 * r2); y0 = 0.70; y1 = 2.55; frameC = mix(vec3(0.20, 0.12, 0.07), vec3(0.08, 0.22, 0.15), step(0.5, r3)); }',
    '  else if (cls < 2.5) { ww = bay * (0.80 + 0.12 * r2); y0 = 0.90; y1 = 2.40; frameC = vec3(0.07, 0.075, 0.08); }',
    '  else               { ww = bay * (0.62 + 0.18 * r2); y0 = 0.55; y1 = 2.60; frameC = vec3(0.62, 0.64, 0.66); }',
    '  frameC = linC(frameC);',
    '  float cx = floor(u / bay), j = floor(hy / 3.0);',
    '  vec2 q = vec2(u - cx * bay, hy - j * 3.0);',
    '  vec2 cell = vec2(cx, j) + vec2(floor(bseed * 977.0), floor(yb));',
    '  float shop = (j < 0.5 && hy > -0.3) ? step(0.22, h21(cell + 0.5)) : 0.0;',
    '  float pix = max(fwidth(u), fwidth(P.y));',
    '  float det = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.03, 0.10, pix);',
    '  vec2 a = vec2((bay - ww) * 0.5, y0), b = vec2((bay + ww) * 0.5, y1);',
    '  if (shop > 0.5) { a = vec2(bay * 0.07, 0.10); b = vec2(bay * 0.93, 2.32); }',
    '  float fr = shop > 0.5 ? 0.09 : 0.065;',
    '  float win = inR(q, a, b);',
    '  float frame = inR(q, a - fr, b + fr) - win;',
    '  float sill = shop > 0.5 ? 0.0 : inR(q, vec2(a.x - 0.10, a.y - fr - 0.09), vec2(b.x + 0.10, a.y - fr));',
    '  float fascia = shop > 0.5 ? inR(q, vec2(0.0, 2.44), vec2(bay, 2.92)) : 0.0;',
    '  gOnWall = 1.0 - max(max(frame, sill), max(fascia, win));',
    /* the wall: grime from every sill down, darker toward the street, a band at each floor on concrete and render */
    '  float streak = 0.0;',
    '  if (shop < 0.5 && q.x > a.x && q.x < b.x && q.y < a.y - fr && q.y > a.y - 1.8) streak = vn(vec2(q.x * 5.0 + cx * 7.0, j)) * (1.0 - (a.y - fr - q.y) / 1.8);',
    '  wallc *= 1.0 - 0.34 * streak * det;',
    '  wallc *= mix(0.55, 1.0, smoothstep(-0.4, 1.4, hy));',
    '  if (cls > 1.5 && q.y < 0.24) wallc *= 1.14;',
    '  alb = wallc; em = vec3(0.0);',
    '  vec3 glow; float lit = windowLit(cell, clamp((q - a) / (b - a), 0.0, 1.0), uT, glow);',
    '  glow *= 1.25;',
    '  if (shop > 0.5) { lit = step(0.32, h21(cell + 7.7)); glow = mix(vec3(1.05, 0.96, 0.82), vec3(0.86, 0.98, 1.10), step(0.5, h21(cell + 2.2))) * 1.2; }',
    '  float shut = shop > 0.5 ? step(h21(cell + 1.1), 0.34) : 0.0;',
    '  if (frame > 0.5) alb = shop > 0.5 ? linC(vec3(0.09, 0.09, 0.10)) : frameC;',
    '  if (sill > 0.5) alb = linC(vec3(0.62, 0.61, 0.58));',
    '  if (fascia > 0.5) {',
    /* the sign board over the shop: a colour, block letters, lit or not */
    '    float hue = h21(cell + 12.3);',
    '    vec3 sc = hue < 0.2 ? vec3(0.75, 0.08, 0.10) : hue < 0.4 ? vec3(0.95, 0.75, 0.10) : hue < 0.55 ? vec3(0.10, 0.35, 0.75) : hue < 0.7 ? vec3(0.10, 0.55, 0.30) : hue < 0.85 ? vec3(0.92, 0.92, 0.90) : vec3(0.08, 0.08, 0.09);',
    '    float letters = step(0.42, h21(vec2(floor(q.x / 0.24), cell.x * 3.0 + cell.y))) * inR(q, vec2(0.35, 2.55), vec2(bay - 0.35, 2.80));',
    '    vec3 lc = hue > 0.7 && hue < 0.85 ? vec3(0.08) : vec3(0.95);',
    '    alb = linC(mix(sc, lc, letters * 0.9));',
    '    em = alb * 0.55 * step(0.45, h21(cell + 13.7)) * (1.0 - shut * 0.6);',
    '  }',
    '  if (win > 0.5) {',
    '    if (shut > 0.5) {',
    /* a roller shutter: corrugated, painted once, tagged since */
    '      vec3 pc = mix(vec3(0.42, 0.44, 0.46), mix(vec3(0.12, 0.30, 0.22), vec3(0.18, 0.24, 0.42), step(0.5, h21(cell + 3.9))), step(0.55, h21(cell + 2.9)));',
    '      float cor = 0.78 + 0.22 * sin(q.y * 6.2831853 / 0.085);',
    '      if (uAtOn > 0.5) { vec3 sn; vec3 st = atTex(8.0, vec2(q.x, -q.y) / 1.6, 1.6, vec3(0.502, 0.514, 0.525), pix, sn); cor = lumC(st) / 0.51; gTexN = sn; gOnWall = 1.0; }',
    '      float tag = smoothstep(0.62, 0.66, vn(q * vec2(2.2, 3.4) + cell * 5.0)) * step(0.3, h21(cell + 4.2));',
    '      vec3 tc = vec3(h21(cell + 5.1), h21(cell + 5.2), h21(cell + 5.3));',
    '      alb = linC(mix(pc * cor, tc, tag * 0.85));',
    '      em = vec3(0.0);',
    '    } else {',
    /* far away: a flat pane, lit or dark - the same window as up close */
    '      vec3 fAlb = vec3(0.012, 0.016, 0.026), fEm = glow * lit * 0.9;',
    '      vec3 dAlb = fAlb, dEm = fEm;',
    '      if (det > 0.01) {',
    '        vec3 V = normalize(P - cameraPosition);',
    '        vec3 vl = vec3(dot(V.xz, t2), V.y, dot(V.xz, nW.xz));',
    '        float dg = shop > 0.5 ? 0.06 : 0.15;',
    '        vec2 gp = q + vl.xy * (dg / max(0.06, -vl.z));',
    '        if (inR(gp, a, b) < 0.5) {',
    /* the reveal: the sides of the opening between the wall and the glass */
    '          float sh = gp.y > b.y ? 0.32 : gp.y < a.y ? 0.85 : 0.58;',
    '          dAlb = wallc * sh; dEm = vec3(0.0);',
    '        } else {',
    '          vec3 d = normalize(vl);',
    '          vec2 gn = (gp - a) / (b - a);',
    '          float D = shop > 0.5 ? 6.0 : 3.4 + 1.6 * h21(cell + 3.3);',
    '          vec3 room = roomCol(vec3(gp, -dg), d, vec2(0.0, bay), vec2(0.04, 2.92), D, h21(cell + 9.1), lit, glow, shop);',
