    '          float cv = h21(cell + 4.4);',
    '          if (shop < 0.5) {',
    '            if (cv < 0.22) {',
    '              float bh = 1.0 - (0.25 + 0.6 * h21(cell + 5.5));',
    '              if (gn.y > bh) { float sl = mix(0.62, smoothstep(0.0, 0.3, fract(gp.y / 0.055)), 1.0 - smoothstep(0.008, 0.02, pix)); vec3 bc = mix(vec3(0.75, 0.72, 0.66), vec3(0.50, 0.58, 0.68), step(0.5, fract(cv * 4.0))); room = bc * (0.55 + 0.45 * sl) * mix(vec3(0.02), glow * 0.8, lit); }',
    '            } else if (cv < 0.55) {',
    '              float cw = 0.16 + 0.28 * h21(cell + 6.6);',
    '              if (min(gn.x, 1.0 - gn.x) < cw) { vec3 cc = mix(vec3(0.75, 0.66, 0.52), vec3(0.58, 0.18, 0.16), h21(cell + 7.1)); room = cc * (0.7 + 0.3 * sin(gp.x * 40.0) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.015, 0.04, pix))) * mix(vec3(0.03), glow * 0.75, lit); }',
    '            }',
    '          }',
    '          float fres = 0.06 + 0.9 * pow(1.0 - clamp(-vl.z, 0.0, 1.0), 4.0);',
    '          vec3 refl = skyRefl(reflect(V, nW));',
    '          dAlb = vec3(0.01); dEm = mix(room, refl, fres) + refl * 0.18;',
    /* iron bars on the low floors - every second house in Bogota has them */
    '          if (shop < 0.5 && j < 1.5 && cls < 1.5 && h21(cell + 8.8) < 0.55) {',
    '            float bars = mix(max(step(abs(fract(q.x / 0.14) - 0.5), 0.07), step(abs(fract((q.y - a.y) / 0.6) - 0.5), 0.03)), 0.32, smoothstep(0.012, 0.035, pix));',
    '            dAlb = mix(dAlb, linC(vec3(0.06, 0.06, 0.07)), bars); dEm *= 1.0 - bars * 0.9;',
    '          }',
    '        }',
    '      }',
    '      alb = mix(fAlb, dAlb, det); em = mix(fEm, dEm, det);',
    '    }',
    '  }',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  /* the wall shader's injection: roofs, the glass towers, every other facade */
  var FACADE_FRAG = [
    '#include <emissivemap_fragment>',
    '{',
    '  if (onPlatform(vWp.xz) < 0.5) discard;',
    '  for (int hi = 0; hi < 4; hi++) {',
    '    if (uHoleB[hi].w < 0.5) continue;',
    '    vec2 hd = vWp.xz - uHoleA[hi].xy;',
    '    vec2 hn = uHoleA[hi].zw, ht = vec2(-hn.y, hn.x);',
    '    float hu = dot(hd, ht), hw = dot(hd, hn), hv = vWp.y - uHoleB[hi].z;',
    '    if (abs(hu) < uHoleB[hi].x && hv > -0.2 && hv < uHoleB[hi].y && abs(hw) < 2.5) discard;',
    '  }',
    '  vec3 nW = inverseTransformDirection(normal, viewMatrix);',
    '  vec2 mU = (vWp.xz - nW.xz * 1.2 - uMatBox.xy) / uMatBox.zw;',
    '  vec4 mT = texture2D(uMat, mU);',
    '  if (mT.a < 0.5) mT = texture2D(uMat, (vWp.xz - uMatBox.xy) / uMatBox.zw);',
    '  float mC = floor(mT.r * 255.0 + 0.5), pal = mod(mC, 16.0);',
    '  float cls = uMatOn > 0.5 ? (mT.a > 0.5 ? floor(mC / 16.0) : 0.0) : -1.0;',
    '  float gb = floor(mT.g * 255.0 + 0.5), bb = floor(mT.b * 255.0 + 0.5), ab = floor(mT.a * 255.0 + 0.5);',
    '  float yb = uYB0 + gb + mod(bb, 128.0) / 127.0;',
    '  float bseed = fract((ab - 128.0) / 127.0 + gb * 0.0137 + mC * 0.071);',
    '  float sdv = h21(vec2(bseed * 91.0, 3.3));',
    '  if (abs(nW.y) > 0.55) {',
    '    if (cls < -0.5) diffuseColor.rgb *= 0.5;',
    '    else if (bb > 127.5) {',
    '      if (uAtOn > 0.5) { vec3 rn; float rf = max(fwidth(vL.x), fwidth(vL.z)); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(atTex(9.0, vL.xz / 1.7 + bseed * 5.0, 1.7, vec3(0.349, 0.2, 0.11), rf, rn) * (1.25 + 0.3 * sdv)); gTexN = rn; gOnWall = 2.0; }',
    '      else { float rr = fract(vL.x / 0.32 + 0.5 * floor(vL.z / 0.42)); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(mix(vec3(0.48, 0.20, 0.12), vec3(0.64, 0.30, 0.18), sdv)) * (0.68 + 0.32 * sin(rr * 3.14159)); }',
    '    }',
    '    else if (uAtOn > 0.5) {',
    '      vec3 rn; float rf = max(fwidth(vL.x), fwidth(vL.z));',
    '      if (sdv > 0.72 && vWp.y - yb < 12.0) diffuseColor.rgb = linC(atTex(10.0, vL.xz / 1.9 + bseed * 5.0, 1.9, vec3(0.667, 0.702, 0.682), rf, rn) * (0.55 + 0.25 * sdv));',
    '      else { vec3 av = vec3(0.808, 0.722, 0.616); vec3 t = atTex(4.0, vL.xz / 3.5 + bseed * 5.0, 3.5, av, rf, rn); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(vec3(0.21, 0.215, 0.225) * (0.85 + 0.3 * sdv) * lumC(t) / lumC(av)); }',
    '      gTexN = rn; gOnWall = 2.0;',
    '    }',
    '    else diffuseColor.rgb = linC(vec3(0.20, 0.205, 0.215) * (0.85 + 0.3 * sdv) * (0.9 + 0.1 * vn(vL.xz * 0.3)));',
    '  } else if (cls > 2.5 && cls < 3.5) {',
    '    float u = abs(nW.x) > abs(nW.z) ? vL.z * sign(nW.x) : -vL.x * sign(nW.z);',
    '    vec2 gc = vec2(floor(u / 1.5), floor(vL.y / 3.0)), gf = vec2(fract(u / 1.5), fract(vL.y / 3.0));',
    '    float mull = 1.0 - step(0.035, gf.x) * step(gf.x, 0.965) * step(0.05, gf.y) * step(gf.y, 0.95);',
    '    vec3 vd = normalize(vWp - cameraPosition), rd = reflect(vd, nW);',
    '    vec3 refl = skyRefl(rd) * 1.4;',
    '    float fres = 0.3 + 0.7 * pow(1.0 - abs(dot(vd, nW)), 3.0);',
    '    diffuseColor.rgb = mull > 0.5 ? linC(vec3(0.16, 0.17, 0.19)) : linC(vec3(0.04, 0.06, 0.08));',
    '    vec3 glow; float lit = windowLit(gc, gf, uT, glow) * step(0.35, h21(gc + 3.1));',
    '    totalEmissiveRadiance += (1.0 - mull) * (refl * fres * 0.6 + glow * 1.05 * lit);',
    '  } else {',
    '    vec3 alb, em;',
    '    facade(max(cls, 0.0), pal, sdv, bseed, vWp, vL, nW, yb, alb, em);',
    '    diffuseColor.rgb = alb;',
    '    totalEmissiveRadiance += em;',
    '    float warm = exp(-max(0.0, vWp.y - (cameraPosition.y - 1.7)) / 4.5);',
    '    totalEmissiveRadiance += uWarm * 0.06 * warm * alb;',
    '  }',
    '  if (uAtOn > 0.5 && gOnWall > 0.5) {',
    '    vec3 Tw, Bw;',
    '    if (gOnWall > 1.5) { Tw = vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0); Bw = vec3(0.0, 0.0, -1.0) * sign(nW.y); }',
    '    else { Tw = abs(nW.x) > abs(nW.z) ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, sign(nW.x)) : vec3(-sign(nW.z), 0.0, 0.0); Bw = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0); }',
    '    vec3 wn = normalize(Tw * gTexN.x + Bw * gTexN.y + nW * gTexN.z);',
    '    normal = normalize((viewMatrix * vec4(wn, 0.0)).xyz);',
    '  }',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  /* ---- the ground as one piece: the streets drawn into a picture around you ---- */
  var GRT = null;
  var grtTexU = { value: null }, grtBoxU = { value: new THREE.Vector4(0, 0, 1, 0) };
  function grtFlat(frag) {
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
      vertexShader: 'attribute vec3 aCol; varying vec3 vC; void main(){ vC = aCol; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * modelMatrix * vec4(position.x, 0.0, position.z, 1.0); }',
      fragmentShader: 'varying vec3 vC; void main(){ ' + frag + ' }',
      depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.NoBlending, side: THREE.DoubleSide
    });
  }
  function initGroundRT(ribbons) {
    var Q = QUAL || TIERS[1];
    var S = Q.tier >= 1 ? 4096 : 2048, R = Q.tier >= 1 ? 220 : 170;
    try { S = Math.min(S, rend.capabilities.maxTextureSize || 4096); } catch (e) {}
    var rt = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(S, S, { format: THREE.RGBAFormat, type: THREE.UnsignedByteType, minFilter: Q.tier >= 1 ? THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter : THREE.LinearFilter,
      magFilter: THREE.LinearFilter, generateMipmaps: Q.tier >= 1, depthBuffer: false, stencilBuffer: false });
    try { rt.texture.anisotropy = Math.min(8, rend.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()); } catch (e) {}
    var sc = new THREE.Scene();
    var oc = new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-R, R, R, -R, -100, 100);
    oc.up.set(0, 0, -1);
    function m(geo, mat, order) { var o = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); o.renderOrder = order; o.frustumCulled = false; sc.add(o); return o; }
    /* R everything that is street, G the pavement, B white paint, A yellow paint */
    m(ribbons[0].geometry, grtFlat('gl_FragColor = vec4(1.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);'), 1);
    m(ribbons[1].geometry, grtFlat('gl_FragColor = vec4(1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);'), 2);
    var paint = m(new THREE.BufferGeometry(), grtFlat('float y = step(0.5, vC.r - vC.b); gl_FragColor = vec4(1.0, 0.0, 1.0 - y, y);'), 3);
    var marks = m(new THREE.BufferGeometry(), grtFlat('gl_FragColor = vec4(1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0);'), 4);
    ribbons.forEach(function (o) { o.visible = false; });
    GRT = { rt: rt, scene: sc, cam: oc, R: R, S: S, x: 1e9, z: 1e9, paint: paint, marks: marks, pgeo: null };
    grtTexU.value = rt.texture;
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] ground: one piece, ' + S + 'px over ' + (2 * R) + ' m');
  }
  function updateGroundRT(px, pz, force) {
    if (!GRT) return;
    var pg = PAINT ? PAINT.geometry : null;
    if (pg !== GRT.pgeo) { GRT.pgeo = pg; if (pg) { GRT.paint.geometry = pg; PAINT.visible = false; } force = true; }
    if (!force && Math.hypot(px - GRT.x, pz - GRT.z) < GRT.R * 0.22) return;
    var tex = 2 * GRT.R / GRT.S;
    var cx = Math.round(px / tex) * tex, cz = Math.round(pz / tex) * tex;
    GRT.x = cx; GRT.z = cz;
    GRT.cam.position.set(cx, 50, cz); GRT.cam.lookAt(cx, 0, cz); GRT.cam.updateMatrixWorld();
    var old = rend.getRenderTarget(), occ = new THREE.Color(), oa = rend.getClearAlpha(), ac = rend.autoClear, te = rend.toneMapping;
    rend.getClearColor(occ);
    rend.setRenderTarget(GRT.rt); rend.setClearColor(0x000000, 0); rend.autoClear = true;
    rend.render(GRT.scene, GRT.cam);
    rend.setRenderTarget(old); rend.setClearColor(occ, oa); rend.autoClear = ac; rend.toneMapping = te;
    grtBoxU.value.set(cx - GRT.R, cz - GRT.R, 2 * GRT.R, 1);
  }

  /* the plate's fragment shader: one surface - ground, park, pavement with
     its slabs and kerb, asphalt worn and wet, the paint, the light on it */
  var GROUND_FRAG = [
    'varying vec3 vW; varying vec2 vUv; uniform vec3 uInk, uPearl; uniform float uFog, uHasRoads, uVec;',
    'uniform sampler2D uRoads; uniform sampler2D uGrt; uniform vec4 uGrtBox;',
    'uniform float uLT;',
    MASK_GLSL,
    LOOK_GLSL,
    'float h21(vec2 p){ vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031); q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33); return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z); }',
    'float vn2(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f); return mix(mix(h21(i), h21(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x), mix(h21(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), h21(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y); }',
    STREET_LIGHT_GLSL,
    'void main(){',
    '  if (onPlatform(vW.xz) < 0.5) discard;',
    '  float grain = 0.90 + 0.20 * h21(floor(vW.xz * 2.3));',
    '  vec3 col = vec3(0.094, 0.100, 0.118) * grain;',
    '  if (uHasRoads > 0.5) {',
    '    vec4 r = texture2D(uRoads, vec2(vUv.x, 1.0 - vUv.y));',
    '    float rib = r.r, pav = r.g, pnt = smoothstep(0.16, 0.62, r.b), yel = 0.0, park = r.a;',
    '    float nearW = 0.0, lip = 0.0, gut = 0.0;',
    '    if (uGrtBox.w > 0.5) {',
    '      vec2 g = (vW.xz - uGrtBox.xy) / uGrtBox.z;',
    '      vec2 e = abs(g - 0.5) * 2.0;',
    '      if (max(e.x, e.y) < 1.0) {',
    '        vec2 gu = vec2(g.x, 1.0 - g.y);',
    '        vec4 q = texture2D(uGrt, gu);',
    '        nearW = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.82, 0.98, max(e.x, e.y));',
    /* the kerb: a pale lip where the pavement meets the asphalt, the gutter dark below it */
    '        float o = 0.24 / uGrtBox.z, g1 = q.g, g2 = q.g, g3 = q.g, g4 = q.g;',
    '        if (q.r > 0.02 && length(vW.xz - cameraPosition.xz) < 90.0) {',
    '          g1 = texture2D(uGrt, gu + vec2(o, 0.0)).g; g2 = texture2D(uGrt, gu - vec2(o, 0.0)).g;',
    '          g3 = texture2D(uGrt, gu + vec2(0.0, o)).g; g4 = texture2D(uGrt, gu - vec2(0.0, o)).g;',
    '        }',
    '        float gmin = min(min(g1, g2), min(g3, g4)), gmax = max(max(g1, g2), max(g3, g4));',
    '        lip = smoothstep(0.55, 0.9, q.g) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.15, 0.5, gmin)) * nearW;',
    '        gut = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.1, 0.45, q.g)) * smoothstep(0.5, 0.85, gmax) * q.r * nearW;',
    '        rib = mix(rib, q.r, nearW); pav = mix(pav, q.g, nearW); pnt = mix(pnt, smoothstep(0.2, 0.6, q.b), nearW); yel = smoothstep(0.2, 0.6, q.a) * nearW;',
    '      }',
    '    }',
    '    float asph = rib * (1.0 - pav);',
    '    col = mix(col, vec3(0.040, 0.082, 0.050) * (0.85 + 0.3 * h21(floor(vW.xz * 0.7))), park * (1.0 - rib));',
    /* pavement: pale concrete slabs, their joints, a few patched */
    '    vec2 sl = vW.xz / 1.25, sf = abs(fract(sl) - 0.5);',
    '    float joint = smoothstep(0.47, 0.5, max(sf.x, sf.y));',
    '    vec3 pc = vec3(0.27, 0.27, 0.28) * (0.86 + 0.24 * h21(floor(sl))) * (0.93 + 0.14 * vn2(vW.xz * 0.6));',
    '    pc *= 1.0 - 0.35 * joint * nearW;',
    '    col = mix(col, pc, pav);',
    /* asphalt: dark, aggregate in it, worn paler down the middle of each lane */
    '    float agg = h21(floor(vW.xz * 9.0)) * 0.10 + vn2(vW.xz * 0.35) * 0.25;',
    '    vec3 ac = vec3(0.040, 0.043, 0.050) * (0.85 + agg);',
    '    col = mix(col, ac, asph);',
    '    col = mix(col, vec3(0.42, 0.42, 0.41), lip);',
    '    col *= 1.0 - 0.6 * gut;',
    '    col = mix(col, vec3(0.78, 0.77, 0.74) * (0.85 + 0.15 * h21(floor(vW.xz * 4.0))), pnt * 0.92);',
    '    col = mix(col, vec3(0.86, 0.60, 0.12), yel * 0.92);',
    /* wet: puddles in the asphalt, the street's lights in them */
    '    float pud = smoothstep(0.55, 0.78, vn2(vW.xz * 0.28)) * asph;',
    '    col *= 1.0 - 0.35 * pud;',
    '    vec3 vd = normalize(vW - cameraPosition); float gr = pow(1.0 - abs(vd.y), 4.0);',
    '    col += vec3(0.20, 0.24, 0.40) * gr * (0.04 + 0.2 * pud) * asph;',
    '    if (rib > 0.02 && length(vW.xz - cameraPosition.xz) < 170.0) col += streetLights(vW, 0.35 + 0.65 * pud * asph + 0.25 * asph, uLT) * rib;',
    '  }',
    '  if(uPost > 0.5) col = col*2.1 + vec3(0.035);',
    '  col = poster(col);',
    '  col = dots(col);',
    '  float d = length(vW - cameraPosition);',
    '  float fog = 1.0 - exp(-d*d*uFog*uFog);',
    '  gl_FragColor = vec4(mix(col,uInk,clamp(fog,0.0,1.0)),1.0);',
    '}'
  ];
  function groundExtraUniforms() {
    return { uGrt: grtTexU, uGrtBox: grtBoxU, uLP: LIGHTU.p, uLC: LIGHTU.c, uLN: LIGHTU.n, uLT: LIGHTU.tu };
  }

  /* ---- the traffic lights ---- */
  var SIG_CYCLE = 36;
  function buildSignals(nodes) {
    var sigs = [], grid = {};
    Object.keys(nodes).forEach(function (k) {
      var nl = nodes[k], arms = [], ways = {}, maxA = 0, x = 0, z = 0;
      for (var q = 0; q < nl.length; q += 2) {
        var wi = nl[q], j = nl[q + 1], w = STREETS[wi];
        if (!w.on || w.A < 5.5) continue;
        var n = w.pts.length / 2; x = w.pts[j * 2]; z = w.pts[j * 2 + 1];
        for (var s = -1; s <= 1; s += 2) {
          var jj = j + s; if (jj < 0 || jj >= n) continue;
          var dx = w.pts[jj * 2] - x, dz = w.pts[jj * 2 + 1] - z, L = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (L < 0.5) continue;
          arms.push({ dx: dx / L, dz: dz / L, A: w.A, wi: wi, j: j });
        }
        ways[wi] = 1; maxA = Math.max(maxA, w.A);
      }
      if (arms.length < 3 || Object.keys(ways).length < 2 || maxA < 8) return;
      arms.sort(function (a, b) { return b.A - a.A; });
      var keep = [];
      arms.forEach(function (a) { if (!keep.some(function (b) { return a.dx * b.dx + a.dz * b.dz > 0.94; })) keep.push(a); });
      if (keep.length < 3) return;
      if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at(x, z) < 0.6) return;
      var gk = Math.floor(x / 30) + ',' + Math.floor(z / 30);
      for (var gx = -1; gx <= 1; gx++) for (var gz = -1; gz <= 1; gz++) {
        var l = grid[(Math.floor(x / 30) + gx) + ',' + (Math.floor(z / 30) + gz)];
        if (l && l.some(function (o) { return Math.hypot(o.x - x, o.z - z) < 24; })) return;
      }
      var rad = 0; keep.forEach(function (a) { rad = Math.max(rad, a.A / 2); }); rad += 1.2;
      var sg = { x: x, z: z, ax: keep[0].dx, az: keep[0].dz, arms: keep, rad: rad, off: hh(x * 0.011, z * 0.013) * SIG_CYCLE };
      (grid[gk] || (grid[gk] = [])).push(sg);
      sigs.push(sg);
    });
    sigs.forEach(function (s, si) {
      s.arms.forEach(function (a) { var w = STREETS[a.wi]; (w.sig || (w.sig = {}))[a.j] = si; });
    });
    return sigs;
  }
  /* 2 green, 1 amber, 0 red - for traffic heading along (fx, fz) */
  function sigState(s, fx, fz, t) {
    var g = Math.abs(fx * s.ax + fz * s.az) > 0.707 ? 0 : 1;
    var p = ((t + s.off) % SIG_CYCLE + SIG_CYCLE) % SIG_CYCLE - g * (SIG_CYCLE / 2);
    if (p < 0) p += SIG_CYCLE;
    return p < 13 ? 2 : p < 16 ? 1 : 0;
  }
  function signalGeometry() {
    var P = [], dark = [0.08, 0.085, 0.09], yel = [0.62, 0.48, 0.04], blk = [0.015, 0.015, 0.018];
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.2, 0.24, 0.3, 8), m: mt(0, 0.15, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.11, 5.7, 8), m: mt(0, 2.85, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.0, 0.13, 0.13), m: mt(-2.0, 5.55, 0), c: dark });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.03, 0.03, 1.6, 5), m: mt(-0.75, 5.1, 0, 0, 0, -0.8), c: dark });
    /* the head over the lane: yellow housing, black backplate, three visors */
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.40, 1.12, 0.30), m: mt(-3.2, 4.95, 0), c: yel });
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.66, 1.40, 0.04), m: mt(-3.2, 4.95, -0.17), c: blk });
    [0.36, 0, -0.36].forEach(function (dy) {
      P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.30, 0.04, 0.16), m: mt(-3.2, 4.95 + dy + 0.15, 0.22), c: blk });
      P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.115, 0.115, 0.02, 10), m: mt(-3.2, 4.95 + dy, 0.155, Math.PI / 2, 0, 0), c: [0.05, 0.05, 0.05] });
    });
    /* a second head on the pole, for whoever stopped at the line */
    P.push({ g: new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.36, 1.0, 0.28), m: mt(0, 3.1, 0.22), c: yel });
    [0.32, 0, -0.32].forEach(function (dy) {
      P.push({ g: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.1, 0.02, 10), m: mt(0, 3.1 + dy, 0.37, Math.PI / 2, 0, 0), c: [0.05, 0.05, 0.05] });
    });
    return mergeParts(P);
  }
  /* where an arm's pole stands and which way it faces: on the right of the traffic coming in */
  function armPole(s, a, out) {
    var rx = a.dz, rz = -a.dx, side = a.A / 2 + 0.9, back = s.rad + 3.6;
    out.x = s.x + a.dx * back + rx * side; out.z = s.z + a.dz * back + rz * side;
    out.fx = a.dx; out.fz = a.dz;
    return out;
  }
  /* zebras across every arm and a stop line in front of each, as paint in the ground picture */
  function signalMarks(sigs) {
    var pos = [], idx = [];
    function quad(cx, cz, ax, az, bx, bz, hl, hw) {
      /* a rectangle centred on (cx, cz): half-length hl along (ax, az), half-width hw along (bx, bz) */
      var b0 = pos.length / 3;
      pos.push(cx - ax * hl - bx * hw, 0, cz - az * hl - bz * hw, cx - ax * hl + bx * hw, 0, cz - az * hl + bz * hw,
               cx + ax * hl - bx * hw, 0, cz + az * hl - bz * hw, cx + ax * hl + bx * hw, 0, cz + az * hl + bz * hw);
      idx.push(b0, b0 + 2, b0 + 1, b0 + 1, b0 + 2, b0 + 3);
    }
    sigs.forEach(function (s) {
      s.arms.forEach(function (a) {
        var rx = a.dz, rz = -a.dx, half = a.A / 2 - 0.3;
        var zc = s.rad + 1.6;
        for (var l = -half + 0.3; l <= half - 0.25; l += 1.0) quad(s.x + a.dx * zc + rx * l, s.z + a.dz * zc + rz * l, a.dx, a.dz, rx, rz, 1.5, 0.25);
        var sc = s.rad + 3.55;
        quad(s.x + a.dx * sc + rx * (half / 2 + 0.15), s.z + a.dz * sc + rz * (half / 2 + 0.15), a.dx, a.dz, rx, rz, 0.22, half / 2);
      });
    });
    var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    g.setAttribute('aCol', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(pos.length), 3));
    g.setIndex(new THREE.Uint32BufferAttribute(idx, 1));
    return g;
  }
  /* how far the car has to go to the next signal on its way, and that signal */
  function aheadSignal(c, out) {
    var w = STREETS[c.w], n = w.pts.length / 2;
    if (!w.sig) return null;
    var vi = c.dir > 0 ? c.i + 1 : c.i, segL = w.len[c.i + 1] - w.len[c.i];
    var d = c.dir > 0 ? segL - c.s : c.s;
    for (var k = 0; k < 6 && d < 90; k++) {
      var si = w.sig[vi];
      if (si != null) { out.d = d; out.s = LIFE.sigs[si]; return out; }
      var nv = vi + c.dir; if (nv < 0 || nv >= n) return null;
      d += Math.abs(w.len[nv] - w.len[vi]); vi = nv;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* ======================= THE LOOK (v6) =======================
     The frame is no longer drawn straight to the screen. It goes into a
     buffer, and the buffer is finished the way a racing game finishes it:

       GLOW      everything bright - neon, lamps, headlights, lit windows -
                 bleeds light into the air around it (a chain of ever
                 smaller copies, blurred and added back)
       GRADE     cool blue-teal in the shadows, warm sodium in the lights,
                 an S-curve of contrast, a touch more colour
       LENS      a soft vignette, a hair of colour fringing at the edges,
                 film grain that moves
       SPEED     at the wheel, the picture streaks out from the centre
                 with your speed

     and the shiny things - car paint, glass, metal poles - reflect a night
     city painted for them (ENV), instead of nothing.

     CITY_CONFIG.LOOK can turn pieces off: { POST: false } draws the old way.
  */
  var LOOKX = CITY_CONFIG.LOOK || {};
  /* the building textures: Poly Haven (CC0), packed into one colour and one relief atlas */
  var ATU = { d: { value: null }, n: { value: null }, on: { value: 0 } };
  function loadFacadeAtlas() {
    if (LOOKX.TEXTURES === false) return;
    var tl = new THREE.TextureLoader(), got = 0, an = 1;
    try { an = Math.min(8, rend.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()); } catch (e) {}
    tl.setCrossOrigin('anonymous');
    function one(file, u, enc) {
      tl.load(CITY_CONFIG.BASE + file, function (t) {
        t.flipY = false; t.anisotropy = an; t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping;
        t.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; t.needsUpdate = true;
        u.value = t;
        if (++got === 2) { ATU.on.value = 1; if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] facades: textured'); }
      }, undefined, function () { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] facades: atlas missing (' + file + '), drawn walls kept'); });
    }
    one(LOOKX.ATLAS_DIFF || 'facade-atlas-v1-diff.jpg', ATU.d);
    one(LOOKX.ATLAS_NRM || 'facade-atlas-v1-nrm.jpg', ATU.n);
  }
  var POST = null;
  var POST_VS = 'varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = uv; gl_Position = vec4(position.xy, 0.0, 1.0); }';
  var POST_DOWN = [
    'uniform sampler2D tSrc; uniform vec2 uTexel; uniform float uThr, uFirst; varying vec2 vUv;',
    'vec3 pick(vec2 o){',
    '  vec3 c = texture2D(tSrc, vUv + o * uTexel).rgb;',
    '  if (uFirst > 0.5) { float l = max(c.r, max(c.g, c.b)); float k = clamp((l - uThr) / (1.0 - uThr), 0.0, 1.0); c *= k * k; }',
    '  return c;',
    '}',
    'void main(){',
    '  vec3 c0 = pick(vec2(0.0)), c1 = pick(vec2(-1.0, -1.0)), c2 = pick(vec2(1.0, -1.0)), c3 = pick(vec2(-1.0, 1.0)), c4 = pick(vec2(1.0, 1.0));',
    '  if (uFirst > 0.5) {',
    /* the first step weights each tap by 1 / (1 + its brightness): one bright pixel cannot flare the glow */
    '    float w0 = 4.0 / (1.0 + max(c0.r, max(c0.g, c0.b))), w1 = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(c1.r, max(c1.g, c1.b))), w2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(c2.r, max(c2.g, c2.b))), w3 = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(c3.r, max(c3.g, c3.b))), w4 = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(c4.r, max(c4.g, c4.b)));',
    '    gl_FragColor = vec4((c0 * w0 + c1 * w1 + c2 * w2 + c3 * w3 + c4 * w4) / (w0 + w1 + w2 + w3 + w4), 1.0);',
    '  } else gl_FragColor = vec4((c0 * 4.0 + c1 + c2 + c3 + c4) / 8.0, 1.0);',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');
  var POST_UP = [
    'uniform sampler2D tSrc, tAdd; uniform vec2 uTexel; varying vec2 vUv;',
    'void main(){',
    '  vec2 o = uTexel;',
    '  vec3 c = texture2D(tSrc, vUv).rgb * 4.0;',
    '  c += (texture2D(tSrc, vUv + vec2(o.x, 0.0)).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv - vec2(o.x, 0.0)).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv + vec2(0.0, o.y)).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv - vec2(0.0, o.y)).rgb) * 2.0;',
    '  c += texture2D(tSrc, vUv + o).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv - o).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv + vec2(o.x, -o.y)).rgb + texture2D(tSrc, vUv + vec2(-o.x, o.y)).rgb;',
    '  gl_FragColor = vec4(c / 16.0 + texture2D(tAdd, vUv).rgb, 1.0);',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');
  var POST_OUT = [
    'uniform sampler2D tScene, tBloom; uniform float uT, uBloom, uVig, uGrain, uBlur, uCA, uLift, uCon, uSat;',
    'uniform vec3 uShadowTint, uHiTint, uLiftTint; varying vec2 vUv;',
    'float h12(vec2 p){ return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453); }',
    'void main(){',
    '  vec2 d = vUv - 0.5;',
    '  vec3 c;',
    '  if (uBlur > 0.0005) {',
    '    c = vec3(0.0);',
    '    for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) { float k = float(i) / 9.0; c += texture2D(tScene, vUv - d * uBlur * k).rgb; }',
    '    c /= 10.0;',
    '  } else {',
    '    float ca = uCA * dot(d, d);',
    '    c = vec3(texture2D(tScene, vUv - d * ca).r, texture2D(tScene, vUv).g, texture2D(tScene, vUv + d * ca).b);',
    '  }',
    '  vec3 b = texture2D(tBloom, vUv).rgb;',
    '  c = 1.0 - (1.0 - c) * (1.0 - min(b * uBloom, vec3(1.0)));',          /* screen-add: glow never clips to flat white */
    '  float l = dot(c, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));',
    '  c = mix(c * uShadowTint, c * uHiTint, smoothstep(0.06, 0.62, l));',
    '  c += uLift * uLiftTint * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.28, l));',
    '  c = clamp(c, 0.0, 1.0);',
    '  c = mix(c, c * c * (3.0 - 2.0 * c), uCon);',
    '  float l2 = dot(c, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));',
    '  c = mix(vec3(l2), c, uSat);',
    '  float v = smoothstep(0.86, 0.22, length(d * vec2(1.0, 0.82)));',
    '  c *= mix(1.0, v, uVig);',
    '  c += (h12(gl_FragCoord.xy + fract(uT * 7.13) * 917.0) - 0.5) * uGrain * (1.0 - 0.6 * l2);',
    '  gl_FragColor = vec4(clamp(c, 0.0, 1.0), 1.0);',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  function postRT(ms, depth) {
    var o = { minFilter: THREE.LinearFilter, magFilter: THREE.LinearFilter, format: THREE.RGBAFormat, type: THREE.UnsignedByteType, depthBuffer: !!depth, stencilBuffer: false };
    var rt = (ms && THREE.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget && rend.capabilities.isWebGL2) ? new THREE.WebGLMultisampleRenderTarget(2, 2, o) : new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(2, 2, o);
    if (rt.isWebGLMultisampleRenderTarget) rt.samples = ms;
    rt.texture.generateMipmaps = false;
    return rt;
  }
  function postMat(fs, u) {
    return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({ uniforms: u, vertexShader: POST_VS, fragmentShader: fs, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
  }
  function initPost() {
    if (POST || LOOKX.POST === false) return POST;
    var tier = QUAL ? QUAL.tier : 1;
    var P = { tier: tier, levels: tier === 0 ? 4 : 6, down: [], up: [], sz: [] };
    P.main = postRT(tier >= 1 && !TOUCH ? 4 : 0, true);
    P.main.texture.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding;     /* the standard materials write what they would write to the screen */
    for (var i = 0; i < P.levels; i++) { P.down.push(postRT(0, false)); P.up.push(postRT(0, false)); }
    P.quad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2));
    P.quad.frustumCulled = false;
    P.scene = new THREE.Scene(); P.scene.add(P.quad); P.cam = new THREE.Camera();
    P.mDown = postMat(POST_DOWN, { tSrc: { value: null }, uTexel: { value: new THREE.Vector2() }, uThr: { value: LOOKX.GLOW_FROM != null ? LOOKX.GLOW_FROM : 0.62 }, uFirst: { value: 0 } });
    P.mUp = postMat(POST_UP, { tSrc: { value: null }, tAdd: { value: null }, uTexel: { value: new THREE.Vector2() } });
    P.mOut = postMat(POST_OUT, {
      tScene: { value: null }, tBloom: { value: null }, uT: { value: 0 },
      uBloom: { value: LOOKX.GLOW != null ? LOOKX.GLOW : 0.34 },
      uVig: { value: LOOKX.VIGNETTE != null ? LOOKX.VIGNETTE : 0.55 },
      uGrain: { value: LOOKX.GRAIN != null ? LOOKX.GRAIN : 0.0 },
      uBlur: { value: 0 }, uCA: { value: tier === 0 ? 0 : (LOOKX.FRINGE != null ? LOOKX.FRINGE : 0.012) },
      uLift: { value: 0.035 }, uCon: { value: LOOKX.CONTRAST != null ? LOOKX.CONTRAST : 0.3 }, uSat: { value: LOOKX.SATURATION != null ? LOOKX.SATURATION : 1.12 },
      uShadowTint: { value: new THREE.Vector3(0.9, 0.98, 1.1) }, uHiTint: { value: new THREE.Vector3(1.07, 1.0, 0.9) }, uLiftTint: { value: new THREE.Vector3(0.25, 0.42, 0.62) }
    });
    POST = P;
    postResize();
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] look: post on, ' + P.levels + ' glow levels' + (P.main.isWebGLMultisampleRenderTarget ? ', 4x MSAA' : ''));
    return P;
  }
  function postResize() {
    if (!POST) return;
    var v = rend.getDrawingBufferSize(new THREE.Vector2()), w = Math.max(2, v.x), h = Math.max(2, v.y);
    POST.main.setSize(w, h);
    POST.sz = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < POST.levels; i++) {
      var dw = Math.max(1, w >> (i + 1)), dh = Math.max(1, h >> (i + 1));
      POST.down[i].setSize(dw, dh); POST.up[i].setSize(dw, dh); POST.sz.push([dw, dh]);
    }
  }
  function postBlit(m, rt) { POST.quad.material = m; rend.setRenderTarget(rt); rend.render(POST.scene, POST.cam); }
  function renderPost(t) {
    var P = POST, i, ac = rend.autoClear;
    rend.setRenderTarget(P.main); rend.render(scene, cam);
    rend.autoClear = true;
    var src = P.main.texture, sw = P.main.width, sh = P.main.height;
    for (i = 0; i < P.levels; i++) {
      P.mDown.uniforms.tSrc.value = src; P.mDown.uniforms.uTexel.value.set(1 / sw, 1 / sh); P.mDown.uniforms.uFirst.value = i === 0 ? 1 : 0;
      postBlit(P.mDown, P.down[i]);
      src = P.down[i].texture; sw = P.sz[i][0]; sh = P.sz[i][1];
    }
    var low = P.down[P.levels - 1].texture, lw = P.sz[P.levels - 1][0], lh = P.sz[P.levels - 1][1];
    for (i = P.levels - 2; i >= 0; i--) {
      P.mUp.uniforms.tSrc.value = low; P.mUp.uniforms.tAdd.value = P.down[i].texture; P.mUp.uniforms.uTexel.value.set(1 / lw, 1 / lh);
      postBlit(P.mUp, P.up[i]);
      low = P.up[i].texture; lw = P.sz[i][0]; lh = P.sz[i][1];
    }
    var U = P.mOut.uniforms, L = typeof LIFE !== 'undefined' ? LIFE : null;
    U.tScene.value = P.main.texture; U.tBloom.value = low; U.uT.value = t;
    var sp = L && L.driving && L.me ? Math.abs(L.me.v) : 0;
    var want = LOOKX.SPEED_BLUR === false ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sp - 8) / 32)) * 0.05;
    U.uBlur.value += (want - U.uBlur.value) * 0.12;
    postBlit(P.mOut, null);
    rend.autoClear = ac;
  }

  /* ENV: a night city painted for the reflections - a dark sky going violet
     at the horizon, the glow of sodium over the city, a band of lit windows
     and neon round the horizon, lamps overhead, wet dark street below */
  function makeNightEnv() {
    var W = 1024, H = 512, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H;
    var x = c.getContext('2d'), r = 1234567;
    function rnd() { r = (r * 16807) % 2147483647; return r / 2147483647; }
    var g = x.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H / 2);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#04050b'); g.addColorStop(0.6, '#0d0f20'); g.addColorStop(0.88, '#251a33'); g.addColorStop(1, '#4a2d22');
    x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, W, H / 2);
    var g2 = x.createLinearGradient(0, H / 2, 0, H);
    g2.addColorStop(0, '#231713'); g2.addColorStop(0.15, '#0b0b0e'); g2.addColorStop(1, '#050506');
    x.fillStyle = g2; x.fillRect(0, H / 2, W, H / 2);
    /* the skyline: dark blocks with lit windows */
    var hz = H / 2, i, k;
    for (i = 0; i < 160; i++) {
      var bx = rnd() * W, bw = 6 + rnd() * 30, bh = 4 + Math.pow(rnd(), 2.2) * 60;
      x.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,8,14,0.95)'; x.fillRect(bx, hz - bh, bw, bh + 2);
      for (k = 0; k < bw * bh / 14; k++) {
        if (rnd() < 0.55) continue;
        var warm = rnd() < 0.7;
        x.fillStyle = warm ? 'rgba(255,' + (170 + (rnd() * 50 | 0)) + ',90,' + (0.5 + rnd() * 0.5) + ')' : 'rgba(190,220,255,' + (0.4 + rnd() * 0.5) + ')';
        x.fillRect(bx + 1 + rnd() * (bw - 2), hz - bh + 1 + rnd() * (bh - 2), 1.2, 1.2);
      }
    }
    /* neon */
    var neon = ['#ff2e9a', '#2ef3ff', '#ff6a1a', '#9b5cff', '#f2c200', '#ff3344'];
    for (i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
      x.globalAlpha = 0.6 + rnd() * 0.4; x.fillStyle = neon[(rnd() * neon.length) | 0];
      x.fillRect(rnd() * W, hz - 2 - rnd() * 26, 4 + rnd() * 22, 1.5 + rnd() * 2.5);
    }
    x.globalAlpha = 1;
    /* lamps overhead and down the street */
    for (i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      var lx = rnd() * W, ly = hz - 18 - rnd() * 120, lr = 4 + rnd() * 16;
      var rg = x.createRadialGradient(lx, ly, 0, lx, ly, lr * 3);
      var amber = rnd() < 0.7;
      rg.addColorStop(0, amber ? 'rgba(255,200,130,1)' : 'rgba(220,235,255,1)'); rg.addColorStop(0.25, amber ? 'rgba(255,150,60,0.55)' : 'rgba(160,190,255,0.5)'); rg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      x.fillStyle = rg; x.fillRect(lx - lr * 3, ly - lr * 3, lr * 6, lr * 6);
    }
    /* the wet street: the lights smeared downward */
    x.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
    var row = x.getImageData(0, hz - 30, W, 30);
    for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) { x.globalAlpha = 0.13 / (i + 1); x.putImageData(row, 0, hz + 2 + i * 9); }
    x.globalAlpha = 1; x.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    var tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
    tex.mapping = THREE.EquirectangularReflectionMapping; tex.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding;
    var pm = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(rend);
    var env = pm.fromEquirectangular(tex).texture;
    tex.dispose(); pm.dispose();
    return env;
  }
  /* how strongly each kind of surface takes the reflection */
  function tuneReflections(root) {
    root.traverse(function (o) {
      var ms = o.material ? (Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]) : [];
      ms.forEach(function (m) {
        if (!m || !m.isMeshStandardMaterial || m.userData.envTuned) return;
        m.userData.envTuned = true;
        if (m.transparent) m.envMapIntensity = 1.6;
        else if (m.metalness >= 0.12 && m.roughness <= 0.4) m.envMapIntensity = 1.25;   /* car paint */
        else if (m.metalness >= 0.3) m.envMapIntensity = 0.7;                           /* poles, signals */
        else m.envMapIntensity = 0.22;                                                  /* walls */
        m.needsUpdate = true;
      });
    });
  }

  /* ======================= BLOCKS (v7.3: anti-aliased windows, LALAGUNA cars, Esc menu) =======================
     The buildings are no longer the exported tiles. Every building is built
     here, at load, from its footprint: its own walls, its own roof, its own
     parapet - a separate thing from the house next door. Each wall knows
     where it starts and ends, where its roof is and where the street meets
     it, so the facade is laid out on THAT wall:

       BAYS      windows sit in bays fitted to the wall, with a margin at
                 each end - no window runs over a corner or into the next
                 building
       ROOF      no window row is cut by the roof: the top of every wall is
                 a thick cap, and flat roofs get a parapet
       STREET    the ground floor is set out from the pavement under each
                 bay: shopfronts with their signs, garages, doors - flush
                 with the street - and a planter with a bush where there is
                 none
       PARTY     walls that face a neighbour (not the street) are blank

     The tiles are still loaded, for the door placement's ray tests only.
     CITY_CONFIG.BLOCKS = { ON: false } draws the tiles the old way.
  */
  var BLOCKSX = CITY_CONFIG.BLOCKS || {};
  var BLOCKS = null;
  var GF_H = 3.4;          /* the ground floor's height over the pavement */
  /* a hash of small whole numbers that the GPU computes exactly too: every step stays under 2^24 */
  function hashI(n) { var a = (n * 171 + 12345) % 4093, b = (a * a + n) % 4093; return ((b * b + 7) % 4093) / 4093; }
  function wallSeed(b, i) { return (b * 31 + i * 17) & 255; }
  function wallBays(L, ws) {
    L = Math.fround(L);
    var m = 0.75, bay0 = Math.fround(2.8 + 0.8 * hashI(ws * 3 + 1)), nb = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Math.fround((L - 2 * m) / bay0)));
    return { m: m, nb: nb, bay: nb > 0 ? (L - 2 * m) / nb : 1 };
  }
  /* what a ground-floor bay is: 0 shop, 1 garage, 2 door, 3 nothing (a planter) */
  function gfKind(ws, k) { var t = hashI(ws * 64 + k); return t < 0.45 ? 0 : t < 0.58 ? 1 : t < 0.80 ? 2 : 3; }

  /* the buildings the GIS file does not have, recovered from the tiles */
  function loadExtraBuildings(url) {
    return fetch(url).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status); return r.json(); });
  }
  function mergeExtras(F, E) {
    if (!E || !E.length) return F;
    var n0 = F.n, n = n0 + E.length, ve = 0, i, b;
    for (i = 0; i < E.length; i++) ve += E[i].r.length;
    function grow(a, T) { var o = new T(n); o.set(a); return o; }
    F.style = grow(F.style, Uint16Array); F.floors = grow(F.floors, Uint8Array); F.top = grow(F.top, Float32Array);
    F.ringStart = grow(F.ringStart, Uint32Array); F.ringLen = grow(F.ringLen, Uint8Array); F.flagStart = grow(F.flagStart, Uint32Array);
    F.minx = grow(F.minx, Float32Array); F.maxx = grow(F.maxx, Float32Array); F.minz = grow(F.minz, Float32Array); F.maxz = grow(F.maxz, Float32Array);
    var V = new Float32Array(F.verts.length + ve * 2); V.set(F.verts);
    var FL = new Uint8Array(F.flags.length + ve); FL.set(F.flags);
    var NT = new Float32Array(F.flags.length + ve); if (F.ntop) NT.set(F.ntop);
    var WF = new Float32Array(F.flags.length + ve); if (F.wfoot) WF.set(F.wfoot);
    /* the GIS rings all turn one way; the recovered ones are turned to match */
    var pos = 0, neg = 0;
    for (b = 0; b < Math.min(n0, 400); b++) {
      var s0 = F.ringStart[b], m0 = F.ringLen[b], A = 0;
      for (i = 0; i < m0; i++) { var j0 = (i + 1) % m0; A += F.verts[(s0 + i) * 2] * F.verts[(s0 + j0) * 2 + 1] - F.verts[(s0 + j0) * 2] * F.verts[(s0 + i) * 2 + 1]; }
      if (A > 0) pos++; else neg++;
    }
    var want = pos >= neg ? 1 : -1, vp = F.verts.length / 2, fp = F.flags.length;
    for (var e = 0; e < E.length; e++) {
      var r = E[e].r, m = r.length, Ae = 0, o = E[e].o || [];
      for (i = 0; i < m; i++) { var k = (i + 1) % m; Ae += r[i][0] * r[k][1] - r[k][0] * r[i][1]; }
      var flip = (Ae > 0 ? 1 : -1) !== want;
      b = n0 + e;
      var fl = Math.max(1, Math.round((E[e].t - E[e].b) / 3));
      F.style[b] = Math.floor(hh(e, 4.4) * 999); F.floors[b] = fl; F.top[b] = E[e].t;
      F.ringStart[b] = vp; F.ringLen[b] = m; F.flagStart[b] = fp;
      var x0 = 1e9, x1 = -1e9, z0 = 1e9, z1 = -1e9;
      for (i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        var src = flip ? (m - 1 - i) : i;
        var x = r[src][0], z = r[src][1];
        V[vp * 2] = x; V[vp * 2 + 1] = z; vp++;
        /* the flag is per edge: edge i runs from vertex i to i+1; reversed, edge i is the old edge m-2-i */
        var oe = flip ? ((m - 2 - i + m) % m) : i;
        /* o: the neighbour's roof in dm over 2500 m, 0 where the edge faces the street */
        var wf = (E[e].w || [])[oe] || 0;
        WF[fp] = wf ? 2500 + wf / 10 : 0;
        NT[fp] = o[oe] ? 2500 + o[oe] / 10 : 0; FL[fp++] = o[oe] ? 0 : 1;
        if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (z < z0) z0 = z; if (z > z1) z1 = z;
      }
      F.minx[b] = x0; F.maxx[b] = x1; F.minz[b] = z0; F.maxz[b] = z1;
      for (var cx = Math.floor(x0 / F.CELL); cx <= Math.floor(x1 / F.CELL); cx++)
        for (var cz = Math.floor(z0 / F.CELL); cz <= Math.floor(z1 / F.CELL); cz++) { var key = cx + ',' + cz; (F.hash[key] || (F.hash[key] = [])).push(b); }
    }
    F.verts = V; F.flags = FL; F.ntop = NT; F.wfoot = WF; F.n = n; F.extra = E.length; F.want = want;
    return F;
  }

  /* which way each ring turns: os = 1 where the outside of edge (dx, dz) is (dz, -dx), as most of the file is */
  function ringTurns() {
    if (FAC.os) return;
    var n = FAC.n, V = FAC.verts, pos = 0, neg = 0, A = new Float64Array(n), b, i;
    for (b = 0; b < n; b++) {
      var s0 = FAC.ringStart[b], m = FAC.ringLen[b], a = 0;
      for (i = 0; i < m; i++) { var j = (i + 1) % m; a += V[(s0 + i) * 2] * V[(s0 + j) * 2 + 1] - V[(s0 + j) * 2] * V[(s0 + i) * 2 + 1]; }
      A[b] = a; if (b < 400) { if (a > 0) pos++; else neg++; }
    }
    var want = FAC.want || (pos >= neg ? 1 : -1);
    FAC.os = new Int8Array(n);
    for (b = 0; b < n; b++) FAC.os[b] = (A[b] > 0 ? 1 : -1) === want ? 1 : -1;
  }
  /* is (x, z) inside a building other than skip? */
  function inBuilding(x, z, skip) {
    var list = FAC.hash[Math.floor(x / FAC.CELL) + ',' + Math.floor(z / FAC.CELL)]; if (!list) return false;
    for (var q = 0; q < list.length; q++) {
      var b = list[q]; if (b === skip || x < FAC.minx[b] || x > FAC.maxx[b] || z < FAC.minz[b] || z > FAC.maxz[b]) continue;
      var s0 = FAC.ringStart[b], m = FAC.ringLen[b], V = FAC.verts, c = false;
      for (var i = 0, j = m - 1; i < m; j = i++) {
        var xi = V[(s0 + i) * 2], zi = V[(s0 + i) * 2 + 1], xj = V[(s0 + j) * 2], zj = V[(s0 + j) * 2 + 1];
        if (((zi > z) !== (zj > z)) && (x < (xj - xi) * (z - zi) / (zj - zi) + xi)) c = !c;
      }
      if (c) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /* ---- the geometry ---- */
  function buildBlocks() {
    var n = FAC.n, V = FAC.verts, CH = 1000, chunks = {}, b, i;
    FAC.f0 = new Float32Array(FAC.flags.length);
    ringTurns();
    var host = FAC.host || null;
    function chunkOf(b) {
      var k = Math.floor((FAC.minx[b] + FAC.maxx[b]) / 2 / CH) + ',' + Math.floor((FAC.minz[b] + FAC.maxz[b]) / 2 / CH);
      return chunks[k] || (chunks[k] = { list: [], nv: 0, ni: 0 });
    }
    for (b = 0; b < n; b++) {
      if (PLATFORM && PLATFORM.at((FAC.minx[b] + FAC.maxx[b]) / 2, (FAC.minz[b] + FAC.maxz[b]) / 2) < 0.2) continue;
      var m = FAC.ringLen[b]; if (m < 3) continue;
      var c = chunkOf(b); c.list.push(b); c.nv += m * 4 + m; c.ni += m * 6 + (m - 2) * 3;
    }
    var group = new THREE.Group(), meshes = [], mat = blockMaterial(), tris = 0;
    var tmpC = [], P2 = [];
    Object.keys(chunks).forEach(function (key) {
      var C = chunks[key], pos = new Float32Array(C.nv * 3), aw = new Float32Array(C.nv * 4), ag = new Float32Array(C.nv * 3);
      var idx = new Uint32Array(C.ni), nv = 0, ni = 0;
      C.list.forEach(function (b) {
        var m = FAC.ringLen[b], s = FAC.ringStart[b], hb = host ? host[b] : b;
        var top = FAC.top[b], cls = FAC.cls ? FAC.cls[hb] : 0, pal = FAC.pal ? FAC.pal[hb] : 0, roof = FAC.roof ? FAC.roof[hb] : 0, sd = FAC.sd ? FAC.sd[hb] : 0;
        var yb = top - 3 * FAC.floors[b];
        /* the street round it */
        var gmin = 1e9, gsum = 0, gn = 0;
        for (i = 0; i < m; i++) { var g = groundAt(V[(s + i) * 2], V[(s + i) * 2 + 1]); if (g < gmin) gmin = g; gsum += g; gn++; }
        var bottom = Math.min(yb, gmin) - 0.8, os = FAC.os[b];
        var par = roof ? 0.22 : (top - gmin > 5 ? 0.8 : 0.4);
        /* small whole numbers only: a large one does not survive interpolation exactly, and every hash built on it would flicker per pixel */
        var packedBase = (sd & 255) * 256, matK = cls * 16 + roof * 8 + pal;
        for (i = 0; i < m; i++) {
          var j = (i + 1) % m, x1 = V[(s + i) * 2], z1 = V[(s + i) * 2 + 1], x2 = V[(s + j) * 2], z2 = V[(s + j) * 2 + 1];
          var dx = x2 - x1, dz = z2 - z1, L = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (L < 0.05) continue;
          var nx = os * dz / L, nz = -os * dx / L, ws = wallSeed(b, i);
          /* a wall standing on a lower building starts at that roof; one inside a building of its own height is not drawn at all */
          var wfoot = FAC.wfoot ? FAC.wfoot[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] : 0;
          if (wfoot && wfoot >= top - 0.05) { FAC.f0[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] = top + 10; continue; }
          var wb = wfoot ? wfoot - 0.04 : bottom;
          /* what stands in front: 0 = the street, else the neighbour's roof (windows start above it) */
          var open = FAC.ntop ? FAC.ntop[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] : ((FAC.flags[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] & 1) ? 0 : top + 10);
          /* the upper floors start one ground floor over the highest pavement along this wall */
          var gmax = -1e9, steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L / 2));
          for (var q = 0; q <= steps; q++) { var t = q / steps, gq = groundAt(x1 + dx * t + nx * 1.2, z1 + dz * t + nz * 1.2); if (gq > gmax) gmax = gq; }
          /* a lower ground floor (down to 2.7 m) where that leaves room for a row of windows under the roof */
          var f0 = Math.max(gmax + 2.7, Math.min(gmax + GF_H, top - 3.1)); FAC.f0[FAC.flagStart[b] + i] = f0;
          var pk = packedBase + ws, y2 = top + par;
          var v0 = nv;
          [[x1, z1, wb, 0], [x2, z2, wb, L], [x2, z2, y2, L], [x1, z1, y2, 0]].forEach(function (p) {
            pos[nv * 3] = p[0]; pos[nv * 3 + 1] = p[2]; pos[nv * 3 + 2] = p[1];
            aw[nv * 4] = p[3]; aw[nv * 4 + 1] = L * os; aw[nv * 4 + 2] = top; aw[nv * 4 + 3] = pk;
            ag[nv * 3] = f0; ag[nv * 3 + 1] = open; ag[nv * 3 + 2] = matK; nv++;
          });
          idx[ni++] = v0; idx[ni++] = v0 + 1; idx[ni++] = v0 + 2; idx[ni++] = v0; idx[ni++] = v0 + 2; idx[ni++] = v0 + 3;
        }
        /* the roof */
        var r0 = nv, gav = gsum / Math.max(1, gn) + GF_H;
        for (i = 0; i < m; i++) {
          pos[nv * 3] = V[(s + i) * 2]; pos[nv * 3 + 1] = top; pos[nv * 3 + 2] = V[(s + i) * 2 + 1];
          aw[nv * 4] = 0; aw[nv * 4 + 1] = 0; aw[nv * 4 + 2] = top; aw[nv * 4 + 3] = packedBase;
          ag[nv * 3] = gav; ag[nv * 3 + 1] = 0; ag[nv * 3 + 2] = matK; nv++;
        }
        var faces = null;
        if (m > 4) {
          P2.length = 0; for (i = 0; i < m; i++) P2.push(new THREE.Vector2(V[(s + i) * 2], V[(s + i) * 2 + 1]));
          try { faces = THREE.ShapeUtils.triangulateShape(P2, []); } catch (e) { faces = null; }
        }
        if (faces && faces.length) faces.forEach(function (f) { if (ni + 3 <= idx.length) { idx[ni++] = r0 + f[0]; idx[ni++] = r0 + f[1]; idx[ni++] = r0 + f[2]; } });
        else for (i = 1; i < m - 1; i++) { idx[ni++] = r0; idx[ni++] = r0 + i; idx[ni++] = r0 + i + 1; }
      });
      var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
      g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos.subarray(0, nv * 3), 3));
      g.setAttribute('aW', new THREE.BufferAttribute(aw.subarray(0, nv * 4), 4));
      g.setAttribute('aG', new THREE.BufferAttribute(ag.subarray(0, nv * 3), 3));
      g.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(idx.subarray(0, ni), 1));
      g.computeBoundingSphere(); g.computeBoundingBox();
      var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat);
      mesh.castShadow = false; mesh.receiveShadow = true;
      group.add(mesh); meshes.push(mesh); tris += ni / 3;
    });
    if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] blocks: ' + n + ' buildings (' + (FAC.extra || 0) + ' recovered from the tiles), ' + meshes.length + ' pieces, ' + Math.round(tris / 1000) + 'k triangles');
    return { group: group, meshes: meshes, mat: mat };
  }

  /* the wall shader for the blocks: the tiles' shader, told where it is on its wall */
  var FACADE7_GLSL = [
    'float imod(float x, float y){ float r = x - y * floor(x / y); if (r < 0.0) r += y; if (r >= y) r -= y; return r; }',
    'float hashI(float n){ float a = imod(n * 171.0 + 12345.0, 4093.0); float b = imod(a * a + n, 4093.0); return imod(b * b + 7.0, 4093.0) / 4093.0; }',
    'float gAtF(vec2 p){ vec2 auv = (p - uOrigin) / uSize; vec4 t = texture2D(uAtlas, vec2(auv.x, 1.0 - auv.y), -16.0); return uLo + (t.r * 255.0 * 256.0 + t.g * 255.0) / 65535.0 * uSpan; }',
    /* the street exactly as the ground plate draws it: its 384 x 384 grid, two triangles a cell */
    'float plateAt(vec2 p){',
    '  vec2 dd = uSize / 384.0, f = (p - uOrigin) / dd, c = clamp(floor(f), 0.0, 383.0), w = clamp(f - c, 0.0, 1.0), o = uOrigin + c * dd;',
    '  float h00 = gAtF(o), h10 = gAtF(o + vec2(dd.x, 0.0)), h01 = gAtF(o + vec2(0.0, dd.y)), h11 = gAtF(o + dd);',
    '  return (w.x + w.y <= 1.0) ? h00 + w.x * (h10 - h00) + w.y * (h01 - h00) : h11 + (1.0 - w.x) * (h01 - h11) + (1.0 - w.y) * (h10 - h11);',
    '}',
    /* one opening in the wall - a window, a shopfront, a door, a garage - with what is behind it */
    /* edges as coverage, a pixel wide, so nothing blinks as it crosses a pixel */
    'float aaR(vec2 p, vec2 a, vec2 b, vec2 w){ vec2 lo = smoothstep(a - w, a + w, p), hi = 1.0 - smoothstep(b - w, b + w, p); return lo.x * lo.y * hi.x * hi.y; }',
    'void opening(vec2 q, vec2 a, vec2 b, float fr, float kind, vec2 cell, float bay, float jj, float cls, vec3 frameC, vec3 wallc, vec3 P, vec3 nW, vec2 tW, float det, float pix, float roomH, inout vec3 alb, inout vec3 em){',
    /* kind: 0 window, 1 shopfront, 2 garage, 3 door */
    '  float shop = kind > 0.5 && kind < 1.5 ? 1.0 : 0.0;',
    '  vec2 w = gAAw;',
    '  float win = aaR(q, a, b, w);',
    '  float frame = max(aaR(q, a - fr, b + fr, w) - win, 0.0) * step(0.0, q.y);',
    '  float sill = kind < 0.5 ? aaR(q, vec2(a.x - 0.10, a.y - fr - 0.09), vec2(b.x + 0.10, a.y - fr), w) : 0.0;',
    '  float fascia = shop > 0.5 && roomH > 2.95 ? aaR(q, vec2(0.0, 2.44), vec2(bay, 2.92), w) : 0.0;',
    /* far off, where a pixel is half a window or more, every edge becomes its share of the wall */
    '  float rowH = kind < 0.5 ? 3.0 : 3.4;',
    '  float far = smoothstep(0.3, 0.7, pix / max(0.05, min(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y)));',
    '  float aw = (b.x - a.x) * (b.y - a.y) / (bay * rowH), af = ((b.x - a.x + 2.0 * fr) * (b.y - a.y + 2.0 * fr)) / (bay * rowH) - aw;',
    '  win = mix(win, aw, far); frame = mix(frame, af, far); sill = mix(sill, kind < 0.5 ? (b.x - a.x + 0.2) * 0.09 / (bay * rowH) : 0.0, far); fascia = mix(fascia, shop > 0.5 && roomH > 2.95 ? 0.48 / rowH : 0.0, far);',
    '  gOnWall = 1.0 - max(max(frame, sill), max(fascia, win));',
    '  if (kind < 0.5) {',
    '    float streak = 0.0;',
    '    if (q.x > a.x && q.x < b.x && q.y < a.y - fr && q.y > a.y - 1.8) streak = vn(vec2(q.x * 5.0 + cell.x * 7.0, cell.y)) * (1.0 - (a.y - fr - q.y) / 1.8);',
    '    alb *= 1.0 - 0.34 * streak * det;',
    '  }',
    '  vec3 glow; float lit = windowLit(cell, clamp((q - a) / (b - a), 0.0, 1.0), uT, glow);',
    '  glow *= 1.25;',
    '  if (shop > 0.5) { lit = step(0.32, h21(cell + 7.7)); glow = mix(vec3(1.05, 0.96, 0.82), vec3(0.86, 0.98, 1.10), step(0.5, h21(cell + 2.2))) * 1.2; }',
    '  float shut = kind > 1.5 && kind < 2.5 ? 1.0 : shop > 0.5 ? step(h21(cell + 1.1), 0.34) : 0.0;',
    '  alb = mix(alb, kind > 0.5 ? linC(vec3(0.09, 0.09, 0.10)) : frameC, frame);',
    '  alb = mix(alb, linC(vec3(0.62, 0.61, 0.58)), sill);',
    '  if (fascia > 0.001) {',
    '    float hue = h21(cell + 12.3);',
    '    vec3 sc = hue < 0.2 ? vec3(0.75, 0.08, 0.10) : hue < 0.4 ? vec3(0.95, 0.75, 0.10) : hue < 0.55 ? vec3(0.10, 0.35, 0.75) : hue < 0.7 ? vec3(0.10, 0.55, 0.30) : hue < 0.85 ? vec3(0.92, 0.92, 0.90) : vec3(0.08, 0.08, 0.09);',
    '    float letters = step(0.42, h21(vec2(floor(q.x / 0.24), cell.x * 3.0 + cell.y))) * aaR(q, vec2(0.35, 2.55), vec2(bay - 0.35, 2.80), w);',
    '    letters = mix(letters, 0.45, smoothstep(0.03, 0.09, pix));',
    '    vec3 lc = hue > 0.7 && hue < 0.85 ? vec3(0.08) : vec3(0.95);',
    '    vec3 fa = linC(mix(sc, lc, letters * 0.9));',
    '    alb = mix(alb, fa, fascia);',
    '    em += fa * 0.55 * step(0.45, h21(cell + 13.7)) * (1.0 - shut * 0.6) * fascia;',
    '  }',
    '  if (win < 0.001) return;',
    /* what fills the opening, then laid in by its coverage */
    '  vec3 gA, gE = vec3(0.0);',
    '  if (kind > 2.5) {',
    /* a door: a painted or wooden leaf, its panels, a lit fanlight */
    '    vec2 dn = (q - a) / (b - a), dw = w / max(b - a, vec2(0.1));',
    '    vec3 dc = mix(mix(vec3(0.20, 0.11, 0.06), vec3(0.09, 0.22, 0.16), step(0.55, h21(cell + 6.1))), vec3(0.55, 0.13, 0.10), step(0.85, h21(cell + 6.2)));',
    '    float pan = aaR(dn, vec2(0.14, 0.08), vec2(0.86, 0.46), dw) + aaR(dn, vec2(0.14, 0.54), vec2(0.86, 0.80), dw);',
    '    gA = linC(dc * (0.75 + 0.25 * mix(pan, 0.5, far)));',
    '    float fan = aaR(dn, vec2(0.1, 0.86), vec2(0.9, 1.0), dw);',
    '    gA = mix(gA, vec3(0.01), fan); gE = glow * lit * 0.8 * fan;',
    '    gOnWall = 0.0;',
    '  } else if (shut > 0.5) {',
    /* a roller shutter: the photographed slats, in its paint, tagged since */
    '    vec3 pc = mix(vec3(0.42, 0.44, 0.46), mix(vec3(0.12, 0.30, 0.22), vec3(0.18, 0.24, 0.42), step(0.5, h21(cell + 3.9))), step(0.55, h21(cell + 2.9)));',
    '    float cor = mix(0.78 + 0.22 * sin(q.y * 6.2831853 / 0.085), 0.78, smoothstep(0.02, 0.05, pix));',

    '    float tag = smoothstep(0.62, 0.66, vn(q * vec2(2.2, 3.4) + cell * 5.0)) * step(0.3, h21(cell + 4.2));',
    '    vec3 tc = vec3(h21(cell + 5.1), h21(cell + 5.2), h21(cell + 5.3));',
    '    gA = linC(mix(pc * cor, tc, tag * 0.85));',
    '  } else {',
    '    vec3 fAlb = vec3(0.012, 0.016, 0.026), fEm = glow * lit * 0.9;',
    '    vec3 dAlb = fAlb, dEm = fEm;',
    '    if (det > 0.01) {',
    '      vec3 V = normalize(P - cameraPosition);',
    '      vec3 vl = vec3(dot(V.xz, tW), V.y, dot(V.xz, nW.xz));',
    '      float dg = shop > 0.5 ? 0.06 : 0.15;',
    '      vec2 gp = q + vl.xy * (dg / max(0.06, -vl.z));',
    '      float rv = aaR(gp, a, b, w);',
    '      float sh = gp.y > b.y ? 0.32 : gp.y < a.y ? 0.85 : 0.58;',
    '      vec3 rAlb = wallc * sh;',
    '      vec3 d = normalize(vl);',
    '      vec2 gn = (gp - a) / (b - a);',
    '      float D = shop > 0.5 ? 6.0 : 3.4 + 1.6 * h21(cell + 3.3);',
    '      vec3 room = roomCol(vec3(gp, -dg), d, vec2(0.0, bay), vec2(0.04, 2.92), D, h21(cell + 9.1), lit, glow, shop);',
    '      float cv = h21(cell + 4.4);',
    '      if (shop < 0.5) {',
    '        if (cv < 0.22) {',
    '          float bh = 1.0 - (0.25 + 0.6 * h21(cell + 5.5));',
    '          float bl = smoothstep(bh - 0.02, bh + 0.02, gn.y);',
    '          float sl = mix(0.62, smoothstep(0.0, 0.3, fract(gp.y / 0.055)), 1.0 - smoothstep(0.008, 0.02, pix)); vec3 bc = mix(vec3(0.75, 0.72, 0.66), vec3(0.50, 0.58, 0.68), step(0.5, fract(cv * 4.0)));',
    '          room = mix(room, bc * (0.55 + 0.45 * sl) * mix(vec3(0.02), glow * 0.8, lit), bl);',
    '        } else if (cv < 0.55) {',
    '          float cw = 0.16 + 0.28 * h21(cell + 6.6);',
    '          float cu = 1.0 - smoothstep(cw - 0.02, cw + 0.02, min(gn.x, 1.0 - gn.x));',
    '          vec3 cc = mix(vec3(0.75, 0.66, 0.52), vec3(0.58, 0.18, 0.16), h21(cell + 7.1));',
    '          room = mix(room, cc * (0.7 + 0.3 * sin(gp.x * 40.0) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.015, 0.04, pix))) * mix(vec3(0.03), glow * 0.75, lit), cu);',
    '        }',
    '      }',
    '      float fres = 0.06 + 0.9 * pow(1.0 - clamp(-vl.z, 0.0, 1.0), 4.0);',
    '      vec3 refl = skyRefl(reflect(V, nW));',
    '      vec3 gEm2 = mix(room, refl, fres) + refl * 0.18, gAl2 = vec3(0.01);',
    '      if (shop < 0.5 && jj < 1.5 && cls < 1.5 && h21(cell + 8.8) < 0.55) {',
    '        float bars = mix(max(step(abs(fract(q.x / 0.14) - 0.5), 0.07), step(abs(fract((q.y - a.y) / 0.6) - 0.5), 0.03)), 0.32, smoothstep(0.012, 0.035, pix));',
    '        gAl2 = mix(gAl2, linC(vec3(0.06, 0.06, 0.07)), bars); gEm2 *= 1.0 - bars * 0.9;',
    '      }',
    '      dAlb = mix(rAlb, gAl2, rv); dEm = gEm2 * rv;',
    '    }',
    '    gA = mix(fAlb, dAlb, det); gE = mix(fEm, dEm, det);',
    '  }',
    '  alb = mix(alb, gA, win); em = mix(em, gE, win);',
    '}',
    /* the wall */
    'void facade7(float cls, float pal, float sd, float bseed, vec3 P, float u, float L, float F0, float nTop, float top, float ws, vec3 nW, out vec3 alb, out vec3 em){',
    '  vec2 t2 = normalize(vec2(-nW.z, nW.x) + 1e-6);',
    '  vec2 tW = t2 * gWs;',
    '  vec3 wallc = bogWall(cls, pal, sd, u, P.y);',
    '  float m = 0.75, bay0 = 2.8 + 0.8 * hashI(ws * 3.0 + 1.0);',
    '  float nb = max(0.0, floor((L - 2.0 * m) / bay0)), bay = nb > 0.5 ? (L - 2.0 * m) / nb : 1.0;',
    '  float k = floor((u - m) / bay), qx = u - m - k * bay;',
    '  float inBay = (nb > 0.5 && k >= 0.0 && k < nb) ? 1.0 : 0.0;',
    '  float uc = m + (k + 0.5) * bay;',
    '  float gB = plateAt(P.xz + tW * (uc - u) + nW.xz * 1.2);',
    '  float gy = P.y - gB;',
    '  float pix = max(fwidth(u), fwidth(P.y));',
    '  float det = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.03, 0.10, pix);',
    '  gAAw = max(vec2(fwidth(u), fwidth(P.y)) * 0.75, vec2(1e-4));',
    '  wallc *= mix(0.62, 1.0, smoothstep(-0.2, 1.6, gy));',
    '  alb = wallc; em = vec3(0.0); gOnWall = 1.0;',
    /* the thick roof: a cap along the top of every wall, the parapet above the roof */
    '  float capY = top - 0.35;',
    '  if (P.y > capY) {',
    '    vec3 capC = linC(cls < 0.5 ? vec3(0.66, 0.62, 0.56) : cls < 1.5 ? vec3(0.88, 0.86, 0.82) : cls < 2.5 ? vec3(0.52, 0.52, 0.51) : vec3(0.80, 0.80, 0.78));',
    '    float lip = smoothstep(0.0, 0.05, P.y - capY);',
    '    float band = 1.0 - 0.28 * step(top + 0.45, P.y) * step(P.y, top + 0.52);',
    '    alb = mix(wallc * 0.4, capC * band * (0.9 + 0.1 * vn(vec2(u * 2.0, P.y * 4.0))), lip); gOnWall = 0.0; return;',
    '  }',
    /* the foot of the wall: a plinth where it meets the pavement */
    '  if (gy < 0.16) { alb = linC(vec3(0.30, 0.30, 0.31)) * (0.85 + 0.15 * vn(vec2(u * 3.0, P.y * 3.0))); gOnWall = 0.0; }',
    /* a wall with a building in front of it is blank up to that building's roof */
    '  if (inBay < 0.5 || (nTop > 1.0 && P.y < nTop + 0.3)) return;',
    '  float r3 = h21(vec2(bseed * 3.3, 8.9)), r2 = h21(vec2(bseed * 7.7, 4.1));',
    /* what opening this spot is in, if any - worked out first, drawn once below */
    '  float kind = -1.0, fr = 0.065, roomH = 3.0, jj = 0.0;',
    '  vec2 q = vec2(qx, gy), a = vec2(0.0), b = vec2(0.0), cell = vec2(0.0);',
    '  vec3 fc = linC(vec3(0.09, 0.09, 0.10));',
    '  if (gy < GF_H && P.y < F0) {',
    /* the ground floor, from the pavement under this bay - only on the street */
    '    if (nTop > 1.0) return;',
    '    roomH = min(F0, capY) - gB;',
    '    if (gy < 0.0 || roomH < 2.5) return;',
    '    float t = hashI(ws * 64.0 + k);',
    '    cell = vec2(k + ws * 37.0, floor(gB) + bseed * 131.0);',
    '    if (t < 0.45) { kind = 1.0; a = vec2(bay * 0.07, 0.0); b = vec2(bay * 0.93, 2.32); fr = 0.09; }',
    '    else if (t < 0.58) { float w = min(2.6, bay * 0.86); kind = 2.0; a = vec2((bay - w) * 0.5, 0.0); b = vec2((bay + w) * 0.5, 2.35); fr = 0.08; }',
    '    else if (t < 0.80) { kind = 3.0; a = vec2(bay * 0.5 - 0.5, 0.0); b = vec2(bay * 0.5 + 0.5, 2.2); fr = 0.08; }',
    '  } else if (P.y >= F0) {',
    /* the upper floors, every 3 m over the ground floor, none cut by the roof */
    '    float j = floor((P.y - F0) / 3.0);',
    '    q.y = P.y - F0 - j * 3.0;',
    '    float ww, y0, y1; vec3 frameC;',
    '    if (cls < 0.5)      { ww = bay * (0.44 + 0.16 * r2); y0 = 0.95; y1 = 2.35; frameC = mix(vec3(0.80, 0.80, 0.78), vec3(0.10, 0.10, 0.11), step(0.6, r3)); }',
    '    else if (cls < 1.5) { ww = bay * (0.34 + 0.08 * r2); y0 = 0.70; y1 = 2.55; frameC = mix(vec3(0.20, 0.12, 0.07), vec3(0.08, 0.22, 0.15), step(0.5, r3)); }',
    '    else if (cls < 2.5) { ww = bay * (0.80 + 0.12 * r2); y0 = 0.90; y1 = 2.40; frameC = vec3(0.07, 0.075, 0.08); }',
    '    else               { ww = bay * (0.62 + 0.18 * r2); y0 = 0.55; y1 = 2.60; frameC = vec3(0.62, 0.64, 0.66); }',
    '    if (F0 + j * 3.0 + y1 + 0.3 <= capY && (nTop < 1.0 || F0 + j * 3.0 + y0 - 0.1 > nTop + 0.3)) { kind = 0.0; a = vec2((bay - ww) * 0.5, y0); b = vec2((bay + ww) * 0.5, y1); fc = linC(frameC); cell = vec2(k + ws * 37.0, j + 1.0 + bseed * 131.0); jj = j + 1.0; }',
    '  }',
    '  if (kind > -0.5) opening(q, a, b, fr, kind, cell, bay, jj, cls, fc, wallc, P, nW, tW, det, pix, roomH, alb, em);',
    '}'
  ].join('\n').replace(/GF_H/g, GF_H.toFixed(2));

  var FACADE7_FRAG = [
    '#include <emissivemap_fragment>',
    '{',
    '  if (onPlatform(vWp.xz) < 0.5) discard;',
    '  for (int hi = 0; hi < 4; hi++) {',
    '    if (uHoleB[hi].w < 0.5) continue;',
    '    vec2 hd = vWp.xz - uHoleA[hi].xy;',
    '    vec2 hn = uHoleA[hi].zw, ht = vec2(-hn.y, hn.x);',
    '    float hu = dot(hd, ht), hw = dot(hd, hn), hv = vWp.y - uHoleB[hi].z;',
    '    if (abs(hu) < uHoleB[hi].x && hv > -0.2 && hv < uHoleB[hi].y && abs(hw) < 2.5) discard;',
    '  }',
    '  vec3 nW = inverseTransformDirection(normal, viewMatrix);',
    '  float pk = floor(vWd.w + 0.5), cp = floor(vGd.z + 0.5), sd8 = floor(pk / 256.0 + 0.001), ws = pk - sd8 * 256.0;',
    '  float cls = floor(cp / 16.0 + 0.0001), roofBit = step(7.5, mod(cp, 16.0)), pal = mod(mod(cp, 16.0), 8.0);',
    '  float bseed = sd8 / 255.0, sdv = h21(vec2(bseed * 91.0, 3.3)), top = vWd.z, F0 = vGd.x;',
    '  if (abs(nW.y) > 0.55) {',
    '    if (roofBit > 0.5) {',
    '      if (uAtOn > 0.5) { vec3 rn; float rf = max(fwidth(vWp.x), fwidth(vWp.z)); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(atTex(9.0, vWp.xz / 1.7 + bseed * 5.0, 1.7, vec3(0.349, 0.2, 0.11), rf, rn) * (1.25 + 0.3 * sdv)); gTexN = rn; gOnWall = 2.0; }',
    '      else { float rr = fract(vWp.x / 0.32 + 0.5 * floor(vWp.z / 0.42)); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(mix(vec3(0.48, 0.20, 0.12), vec3(0.64, 0.30, 0.18), sdv)) * (0.68 + 0.32 * sin(rr * 3.14159)); }',
    '    } else if (uAtOn > 0.5) {',
    '      vec3 rn; float rf = max(fwidth(vWp.x), fwidth(vWp.z));',
    '      if (sdv > 0.72 && top - (F0 - ' + GF_H.toFixed(2) + ') < 12.0) diffuseColor.rgb = linC(atTex(10.0, vWp.xz / 1.9 + bseed * 5.0, 1.9, vec3(0.667, 0.702, 0.682), rf, rn) * (0.55 + 0.25 * sdv));',
    '      else { vec3 av = vec3(0.808, 0.722, 0.616); vec3 t = atTex(4.0, vWp.xz / 3.5 + bseed * 5.0, 3.5, av, rf, rn); diffuseColor.rgb = linC(vec3(0.21, 0.215, 0.225) * (0.85 + 0.3 * sdv) * lumC(t) / lumC(av)); }',
    '      gTexN = rn; gOnWall = 2.0;',
    '    } else diffuseColor.rgb = linC(vec3(0.20, 0.205, 0.215) * (0.85 + 0.3 * sdv) * (0.9 + 0.1 * vn(vWp.xz * 0.3)));',
    '  } else if (cls > 2.5 && cls < 3.5) {',
    /* the glass towers: a curtain wall in 1.5 m modules, a glazed lobby, the cap on top */
    '    float u = vWd.x, capY = top - 0.35;',
    '    vec2 gc = vec2(floor(u / 1.5) + ws * 37.0, floor((vWp.y - F0) / 3.0)), gf = vec2(fract(u / 1.5), fract((vWp.y - F0) / 3.0));',
    '    vec2 gw = max(vec2(fwidth(u / 1.5), fwidth((vWp.y - F0) / 3.0)), vec2(1e-4));',
    '    float inX = smoothstep(0.035 - gw.x, 0.035 + gw.x, gf.x) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.965 - gw.x, 0.965 + gw.x, gf.x));',
    '    float inY = smoothstep(0.05 - gw.y, 0.05 + gw.y, gf.y) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.95 - gw.y, 0.95 + gw.y, gf.y));',
    '    float mull = 1.0 - mix(inX * inY, 0.84, smoothstep(0.12, 0.35, max(gw.x, gw.y)));',
    '    if (u < 0.3 || u > abs(vWd.y) - 0.3) mull = 1.0;',
    '    vec3 vd = normalize(vWp - cameraPosition), rd = reflect(vd, nW);',
    '    vec3 refl = skyRefl(rd) * 1.4;',
    '    float fres = 0.3 + 0.7 * pow(1.0 - abs(dot(vd, nW)), 3.0);',
    '    diffuseColor.rgb = mix(linC(vec3(0.04, 0.06, 0.08)), linC(vec3(0.16, 0.17, 0.19)), mull);',
    '    vec3 glow; float lit = windowLit(gc, gf, uT, glow) * step(0.35, h21(gc + 3.1));',
    '    if (vWp.y < F0) { lit = 1.0; glow = vec3(1.0, 0.95, 0.85) * 0.7; }',
    '    if (vWp.y > capY) { diffuseColor.rgb = linC(vec3(0.30, 0.31, 0.33)); }',
    '    else totalEmissiveRadiance += (1.0 - mull) * (refl * fres * 0.6 + glow * 1.05 * lit);',
    '  } else {',
    '    vec3 alb, em;',
    '    gWs = vWd.y < 0.0 ? -1.0 : 1.0;',
    '    facade7(max(cls, 0.0), pal, sdv, bseed, vWp, vWd.x, abs(vWd.y), F0, vGd.y, top, ws, nW, alb, em);',
    '    diffuseColor.rgb = alb;',
    '    totalEmissiveRadiance += em;',
    '    float warm = exp(-max(0.0, vWp.y - (cameraPosition.y - 1.7)) / 4.5);',
    '    totalEmissiveRadiance += uWarm * 0.06 * warm * alb;',
    '  }',
    '  if (uAtOn > 0.5 && gOnWall > 0.5) {',
    '    vec3 Tw, Bw;',
    '    if (gOnWall > 1.5) { Tw = vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0); Bw = vec3(0.0, 0.0, -1.0) * sign(nW.y); }',
    '    else { vec2 t2 = normalize(vec2(-nW.z, nW.x) + 1e-6); Tw = vec3(t2.x, 0.0, t2.y); Bw = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0); }',
    '    vec3 wn = normalize(Tw * gTexN.x + Bw * gTexN.y + nW * gTexN.z);',
    '    normal = normalize((viewMatrix * vec4(wn, 0.0)).xyz);',
    '  }',
    '}'
  ].join('\n');

  function blockMaterial() {
    var m = buildingMaterialV2(), base = m.onBeforeCompile;
    m.onBeforeCompile = function (sh) {
      base(sh);
      Object.assign(sh.uniforms, atlasUniforms());
      sh.vertexShader = 'attribute vec4 aW; attribute vec3 aG; varying vec4 vWd; varying vec3 vGd;\n' +
        sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\n  vWd = aW; vGd = aG;');
      sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader
        .replace('uniform sampler2D uAtD, uAtN;', 'varying vec4 vWd; varying vec3 vGd;\nuniform sampler2D uAtlas; uniform float uLo, uSpan; uniform vec2 uOrigin, uSize;\nuniform sampler2D uAtD, uAtN;')
        .replace(FACADE_GLSL, function () { var cut = FACADE_GLSL.indexOf('void facade('); return (cut > 0 ? FACADE_GLSL.slice(0, cut) : FACADE_GLSL) + '\n' + FACADE7_GLSL; })
        .replace(FACADE_FRAG, function () { return FACADE7_FRAG; });
    };
    m.customProgramCacheKey = function () { return 'blocks-v7'; };
    return m;
  }

  function start() {
    if (LIFE_ON) pickQuality();
    var cv = document.getElementById('ct-canvas');
    rend = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: cv, antialias: !SMALL, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    rend.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, SMALL ? 1.5 : 2, QUAL ? QUAL.px : 2));
    rend.setClearColor(INK, 1);
    if (FACADES_ON) {
      rend.outputEncoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding;
      rend.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
      rend.toneMappingExposure = FACX.EXPOSURE || 1.0;
      if (FACX.SHADOWS !== false) { rend.shadowMap.enabled = true; rend.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap; }
      try { initPost(); } catch (e) { POST = null; if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] look: post off (' + e.message + ')'); }
      try { loadFacadeAtlas(); } catch (e) {}
    }

    scene = new THREE.Scene();
    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(INK, CITY_CONFIG.FOG);
    if (FACADES_ON && LOOKX.ENV !== false) { try { scene.environment = makeNightEnv(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] look: no reflections (' + e.message + ')'); } }

    cam = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(72, 1, 0.3, CITY_CONFIG.FAR);

    var spawn = MANI.spawn;
    pos.set(spawn.x, groundAt(spawn.x, spawn.z) + CITY_CONFIG.EYE, spawn.z);
    yaw = -(spawn.yawDeg || 0) * Math.PI / 180;

    /* push out of a building if the film happened to end over a roof */
    var guard = 0;
    while (blocked(pos.x, pos.z) && guard++ < 400) {
      pos.x += Math.cos(guard * 0.7) * 4;
      pos.z += Math.sin(guard * 0.7) * 4;
    }
    pos.y = groundAt(pos.x, pos.z) + CITY_CONFIG.EYE;

    if (LIFE_ON && FAC) { try { buildingClasses(); buildMatMap(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] materials skipped: ' + e.message); } }
    var bmat = FACADES_ON ? buildingMaterialV2() : buildingMaterial(), rmat = roadMaterial(), gmat = greenMaterial();
    var useBlocks = FACADES_ON && LIFE_ON && FAC && FAC.cls && BLOCKSX.ON !== false;
    TILES.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t._city) {
        t._city.traverse(function (o) {
          if (!o.isMesh) return;
          o.material = bmat;
          if (FACADES_ON) {
            /* the walls take the relief's shadows; they cast none themselves - a
               2 km mesh shadowing its own walls is acne, and the moon is high */
            o.castShadow = false; o.receiveShadow = true;
            if (!o.geometry.attributes.normal) o.geometry.computeVertexNormals();   /* for the shadow bias only; the shading is flat */
          }
        });
        t._city.position.set(t.x, 0, t.z);
        if (useBlocks) t._city.updateMatrixWorld(true); else scene.add(t._city);
      }
      if (t._road && !ROADS) {
        t._road.traverse(function (o) {
          if (!o.isMesh) return;
          /* GLTFLoader names the second UV set "uv2" already in r128, but a
             tile exported without one would otherwise fail to compile the
             shader - give it a zero set so the material is always valid. */
          if (!o.geometry.attributes.uv2 && o.geometry.attributes.uv) {
            o.geometry.setAttribute('uv2', o.geometry.attributes.uv.clone());
          }
          o.material = rmat;
        });
        t._road.position.set(t.x, 0, t.z);   /* the lift is in the shader now */
        scene.add(t._road);
      }
      if (t._green && !ROADS) {
        t._green.traverse(function (o) { if (o.isMesh) o.material = gmat; });
        t._green.position.set(t.x, 0, t.z);   /* the lift is in the shader now */
        scene.add(t._green);
      }
    });

    if (PLX.ON !== false) {
      try { PLATFORM = buildPlatform(spawn); } catch (e) { PLATFORM = null; if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] platform skipped: ' + e.message); }
      if (PLATFORM && window.console) console.log('[CITY] platform: ' + PLATFORM.areaKm2.toFixed(1) + ' km2');
    }
    scene.add(buildGround());
    if (useBlocks) {
      try { BLOCKS = buildBlocks(); scene.add(BLOCKS.group); }
      catch (e) { BLOCKS = null; if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] blocks skipped, tiles drawn: ' + e.message); TILES.forEach(function (t) { if (t._city) scene.add(t._city); }); }
    }
    if (PLATFORM) scene.add(buildEdge());
    if (STREETS) {
      var ribs = buildStreets(); ribs.forEach(function (m) { scene.add(m); }); updatePaint(pos.x, pos.z, true);
      try { initGroundRT(ribs); } catch (e) { GRT = null; ribs.forEach(function (m) { m.visible = true; }); if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] one-piece ground skipped: ' + e.message); }
    }
    if (FACADES_ON) buildLights().forEach(function (l) { scene.add(l); });
    if (LIFE_ON) {
      try { var lifeGroup = buildLife(spawn); if (lifeGroup) scene.add(lifeGroup); if (GRT && LIFE && LIFE.sigs) GRT.marks.geometry = signalMarks(LIFE.sigs); }
      catch (e) { LIFE = null; if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] street life skipped: ' + e.message); }
    }
    sky = buildSky(); scene.add(sky);
    if (PLATFORM) sky.material.uniforms.uMarble.value = 1;
    startCanal().then(function (c) {
      if (!c || !sky) return;
      sky.material.uniforms.uCanal.value = c.tex;
      sky.material.uniforms.uMode.value = 1;
    });
    logo = buildLogo(spawn); scene.add(logo);

    placeShops(spawn).forEach(function (spot) {
      var p = FACADES_ON ? buildPortalV2(spot) : buildPortal(spot);
      portals.push(p); scene.add(p);
      var mk = buildMarker(spot);
      markers.push(mk); scene.add(mk);
    });
    if (FACADES_ON && FAC) {
      buildRelief().forEach(function (m) { scene.add(m); });
      updateRelief(pos.x, pos.z, true);
      moveLights(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z);
      if (window.console) console.log('[CITY] relief: ' + RELIEF.nb + ' pieces, ' + RELIEF.nw + ' panes on ' + RELIEF.built + ' buildings within ' + Math.round(RELIEF.radius) + ' m');
    }
    if (window.console) {
      console.log('[CITY] doors: ' + portals.map(function (p) {
        return p.userData.shop.label + ' at ' +
          Math.round(Math.hypot(p.userData.x - spawn.x, p.userData.z - spawn.z)) + ' m';
      }).join(', '));
    }

    clock = new THREE.Clock();
    resize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

    elLoad.classList.add('is-gone');
    setTimeout(function () { elLoad.hidden = true; }, 520);
    elStart.hidden = false;
    wire();
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  function resize() {
    var w = root.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    var h = root.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    rend.setSize(w, h, false);
    cam.aspect = w / h; cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    if (POST) postResize();
  }

  /* ================= controls ================= */
  var stickVec = { x: 0, y: 0 }, stickId = null, lookId = null, lookPrev = null;

  function wire() {
    elStart.addEventListener('click', function () {
      elStart.hidden = true;
      elHud.hidden = false;
      running = true;
      if (TOUCH) elStick.hidden = false;
      /* Ask for pointer lock, but never depend on it. Squarespace serves a
         Code Block inside an iframe that does not carry allow="pointer-lock",
         so the request is refused - and a refusal still fires a
         pointerlockchange with nothing locked. The old handler read that as
         "the viewer left" and put the WALK screen back up a frame after it was
         pressed, which is why the city could not be walked at all. */
      try {
        var pr = rend.domElement.requestPointerLock && rend.domElement.requestPointerLock();
        if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(function () {});
      } catch (err) {}
      if (rend.domElement.focus) rend.domElement.focus();
    });

    document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', function () {
      if (document.pointerLockElement === rend.domElement) { hadLock = true; return; }
      /* Only treat this as leaving if we actually held the lock. If it was
         never granted, stay in the city and look by dragging instead. */
      if (hadLock && running && !TOUCH) {
        hadLock = false;
        running = false; showMenu(); elStart.hidden = false; elHud.hidden = true;
      }
    });
    document.addEventListener('pointerlockerror', function () { hadLock = false; });
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
      if (!running || TOUCH) return;
      if (!document.pointerLockElement) return;
      yaw   -= e.movementX * 0.0022;
      pitch -= e.movementY * 0.0022;
      pitch = Math.max(-1.35, Math.min(1.35, pitch));
    });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      keys[e.code] = true;
      /* tap R to run, tap again to walk; Shift still runs while held */
      if (e.code === 'KeyR' && !e.repeat) runMode = !runMode;
      if (e.code === 'Space' && running) e.preventDefault();
      if (e.code === 'KeyE' && near) enterShop(near);
      if (e.code === 'Escape' && running) leave();   /* pause, on any device */
    });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', function (e) { keys[e.code] = false; });

    /* touch: the stick moves, anywhere else looks */
    elStick.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
      stickId = e.pointerId; elStick.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); stickMove(e);
    });
    elStick.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (e.pointerId === stickId) stickMove(e); });
    function stickEnd(e) {
      if (e.pointerId !== stickId) return;
      stickId = null; stickVec.x = stickVec.y = 0;
      elNub.style.transform = '';
    }
    elStick.addEventListener('pointerup', stickEnd);
    elStick.addEventListener('pointercancel', stickEnd);
    function stickMove(e) {
      var r = elStick.getBoundingClientRect();
      var dx = (e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2)) / (r.width / 2);
      var dy = (e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2)) / (r.height / 2);
      var len = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
      if (len > 1) { dx /= len; dy /= len; }
      stickVec.x = dx; stickVec.y = dy;
      elNub.style.transform = 'translate(' + (dx * 30) + 'px,' + (dy * 30) + 'px)';
    }

    /* drag to look - the path that carries the whole thing when the iframe
       refuses pointer lock, and on every touch device */
    var lastTapAt = 0;
    rend.domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
      if (!running || document.pointerLockElement) return;
      /* on a touch screen a quick double tap is the jump */
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') { var nowT = performance.now(); if (nowT - lastTapAt < 320) jumpTap = true; lastTapAt = nowT; }
      if (lookId === null) { lookId = e.pointerId; lookPrev = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }; }
      rend.domElement.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
    });
    rend.domElement.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
      if (e.pointerId !== lookId || !lookPrev) return;
      yaw   -= (e.clientX - lookPrev.x) * 0.0045;
      pitch -= (e.clientY - lookPrev.y) * 0.0045;
      pitch = Math.max(-1.35, Math.min(1.35, pitch));
      lookPrev = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    });
    function lookEnd(e) {
      if (e.pointerId === lookId) { lookId = null; lookPrev = null; rend.domElement.style.cursor = ''; }
    }
    rend.domElement.addEventListener('pointerup', lookEnd);
    rend.domElement.addEventListener('pointercancel', lookEnd);

    elPrompt.addEventListener('click', function () { if (near) enterShop(near); });
    var esc = document.getElementById('ct-esc');
    if (esc) { if (TOUCH) esc.textContent = 'Pause'; esc.addEventListener('click', function () { if (running) leave(); }); }
    elPrompt.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  }

  function showMenu() {
    var lb = document.getElementById('ct-start-lbl'), mw = document.getElementById('ct-menu-where');
    if (lb) lb.textContent = 'Paused';
    if (mw && elWhere) mw.textContent = elWhere.textContent;
  }
  function leave() {
    running = false;
    if (document.exitPointerLock) document.exitPointerLock();
    showMenu();
    elStart.hidden = false; elHud.hidden = true; elStick.hidden = true;
  }

  function enterShop(p) {
    var h = p.userData.shop.href;
    if (/^https?:/.test(h)) window.open(h, '_blank', 'noopener');
    else window.location.href = h;
  }

  /* ================= loop ================= */
  function frame() {
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    var dt = Math.min(0.05, clock.getDelta());
    var t = clock.getElapsedTime();
    if (scene && scene.environment && ((frame.n = (frame.n || 0) + 1) % 150 === 1)) tuneReflections(scene);

    if (sky && CITY_CONFIG.SKY_SPIN && !reduceMotion) sky.material.uniforms.uT.value = t;
    if (CANAL) CANAL.tick(dt * 1000);
    if (FACADES_ON) {
      if (wallShader) { wallShader.uniforms.uT.value = t; wallShader.uniforms.uReliefR.value = RELIEF ? RELIEF.radius : 0; }
      if (paneMat) paneMat.uniforms.uT.value = t;
    }


    if (running && !(LIFE && (LIFE.driving || LIFE.ui))) {
      var fwd = 0, side = 0;
      if (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp'])    fwd += 1;
      if (keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown'])  fwd -= 1;
      if (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft'])  side -= 1;
      if (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight']) side += 1;
      if (stickId !== null) { fwd -= stickVec.y; side += stickVec.x; }

      var sp = (runMode || keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight']) ? CITY_CONFIG.RUN : CITY_CONFIG.WALK;
      var len = Math.sqrt(fwd * fwd + side * side);
      if (len > 1) { fwd /= len; side /= len; }

      /* yaw 0 looks down -Z, which is the standard three.js forward */
      var sinY = Math.sin(yaw), cosY = Math.cos(yaw);
      var wantX = (-sinY * fwd + cosY * side) * sp;
      var wantZ = (-cosY * fwd - sinY * side) * sp;
      /* the velocity chases the intent: fast on the ground, a little in the air */
      var kv = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * (grounded ? 16 : 3));
      vel.x += (wantX - vel.x) * kv;
      vel.z += (wantZ - vel.z) * kv;
      if ((keys['Space'] || jumpTap) && grounded) { vel.y = CITY_CONFIG.JUMP; grounded = false; }
      jumpTap = false;
      vel.y -= CITY_CONFIG.GRAVITY * dt;
      var dx = vel.x * dt, dz = vel.z * dt;

      var R = 0.55, PR = 0.32, inProp = propHit(pos.x, pos.z, PR);
      var pf = function (x, z) { return inProp || !propHit(x, z, PR); };
      var nx = pos.x + dx, nz = pos.z + dz;
      if (!blocked(nx + Math.sign(dx) * R, nz) && !blocked(nx, nz + Math.sign(dz) * R) && pf(nx, nz)) {
        pos.x = nx; pos.z = nz;
      } else if (!blocked(nx + Math.sign(dx) * R, pos.z) && pf(nx, pos.z)) {
        pos.x = nx; vel.z = 0;                 /* slide along the wall */
      } else if (!blocked(pos.x, nz + Math.sign(dz) * R) && pf(pos.x, nz)) {
        pos.z = nz; vel.x = 0;
      } else { vel.x = 0; vel.z = 0; }
      /* up and down: the street is the floor, a jump leaves it and comes back */
      pos.y += vel.y * dt;
      var floorY = groundAt(pos.x, pos.z) + CITY_CONFIG.EYE;
      if (pos.y <= floorY) { pos.y = floorY; if (vel.y < 0) vel.y = 0; grounded = true; }
      else if (grounded && vel.y <= 0 && pos.y - floorY < 1.2) { pos.y = floorY; vel.y = 0; }   /* down a kerb or a slope, feet stay on it */
      else grounded = false;
    }

    if (LIFE) stepLife(dt, t);
    if (FACADES_ON) { updateRelief(pos.x, pos.z, false); moveLights(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z); }
    if (STREETS) { updatePaint(pos.x, pos.z, false); updateGroundRT(pos.x, pos.z, false); }
    cam.position.copy(pos);
    cam.rotation.set(0, 0, 0, 'YXZ');
    cam.rotateY(yaw); cam.rotateX(pitch);
    if (LIFE && LIFE.driving) driveCamera(dt);
    if (sky) sky.position.copy(pos);

    /* doorways */
    near = null;
    var bd = CITY_CONFIG.REACH * CITY_CONFIG.REACH;
    for (var i = 0; i < portals.length; i++) {
      var u = portals[i].userData;
      var ddx = pos.x - u.x, ddz = pos.z - u.z;
      var d = ddx * ddx + ddz * ddz;
      var pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 1.6 + i);
      u.hall.material.opacity = 0.12 + 0.10 * pulse;
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; near = portals[i]; }
    }
    if (near && running) {
      elPrompt.hidden = false;
      elPmtNm.textContent = near.userData.shop.label;
    } else {
      elPrompt.hidden = true;
    }

    /* the pins turn to face wherever you are standing */
    for (var k = 0; k < markers.length; k++) {
      var mk = markers[k].userData;
      var md = Math.hypot(pos.x - mk.x, pos.z - mk.z);
      /* ride up and grow with distance so the pin stays the same size on
         screen whether it is across the street or across the block */
      mk.tag.position.y = 11 + Math.min(70, md * 0.26);
      var k2 = Math.max(0.16, Math.min(1.1, md / 120));
      mk.tag.scale.set(k2, k2, 1);
      mk.tag.lookAt(pos.x, markers[k].position.y + mk.tag.position.y, pos.z);
    }

    if (elWhere && running) {
      var hint = 'Bogota · ' + Math.round(groundAt(pos.x, pos.z)) + ' m above the sea';
      var closest = null, cd = 1e18;
      for (var q = 0; q < portals.length; q++) {
        var pu = portals[q].userData;
        var qd = Math.hypot(pos.x - pu.x, pos.z - pu.z);
        if (qd < cd) { cd = qd; closest = pu.shop.label; }
      }
      if (closest) hint += '  ·  ' + closest + ' ' + Math.round(cd) + ' m';
      if (DATA_WARN) hint += '  ·  ' + DATA_WARN;
      elWhere.textContent = lifeHint(hint);
    }

    if (POST) {
      try { renderPost(t); }
      catch (e) { POST = null; rend.setRenderTarget(null); if (window.console) console.warn('[CITY] look: post off (' + e.message + ')'); rend.render(scene, cam); }
    } else rend.render(scene, cam);
  }

  }   /* end of main() */
})();
</script>
